Chapter 3251: Fatal Truth

“Because I did not discover the ‘viruses’ such as you or ‘you’ in the past few reincarnations. I noticed them a long, long time ago when you could not even imagine it.

“Li Yao,” the will of the earth said with a smile. “Like I said, I am Li Yao, but I am far more than Li Yao. Therefore, it was not an invasion. You and I have been one since the very beginning. We grew up together and created brilliant miracles together. In the end, we returned to this place together, to the mother of the mind.

“What?”

The Red Pole Star was dumbfounded. The more she recalled, the more her soul was shivering. In the end, the fortress of her soul almost collapsed. “Are you saying—are you saying—”

“If you haven’t found out when I snuck into Li Yao’s soul, you are disappointing me. You are not qualified to communicate with a ‘3.5-dimensional life form’ at all.

The will of the Earth projected a complicated stream of information deep inside the brain of the Red Pole Star. Every ripple of the information contained countless vivid and even more vivid pictures than the real thing, allowing the Red Pole Star to understand the truth that had happened somewhere in the universe a long time ago in the most straightforward way.

Therefore, Red Pole saw a blue planet floating in the dark universe—Earth at the end of a certain reincarnation.

He also saw a vague shadow that was entirely made of fury and unyielding brilliance. It was ‘Heroic Spirit Li Yao’, who had awakened in his previous life.

“The Vulture Plan hasn’t failed yet. I’m still alive! Even if I flee to the edge of the universe and suffer ten thousand years of pain and suffering, one day, I will make a comeback, complete the Vulture Plan, and destroy Earth!”

Through the ‘void’, the Red Pole Star seemed to hear Li Yao’s cry.

Then, the heroic spirit turned into a streak of brightness, tore apart the universe, crossed the stars, and vanished on the other side of space and time.

It was something that Red Pole had known for a long time.

It was also the self-righteous ‘truth’.

However, in the past, he had never seen such a thing. Not long after ‘Heroic Spirit Li Yao’ escaped from the earth where he was reincarnated, a tiny silver light spot popped up from the earth, as if it had no concept of gravity or speed. Like a ghost ripple released by the will of the earth, it circled three times around the coordinates that ‘Heroic Spirit Li Yao’ had just left. Then, it caught up with ‘Heroic Spirit Li Yao’ and followed him to the edge of the universe!

“This—this is—”

The Red Pole could barely breathe.

“Did you really think that the ‘Vulture War’ would deal such a heavy blow to me that I could not even lock onto the soul of an ordinary earthling, or in your words, a heroic spirit?

The will of the earth replied casually, “No. If I really did not care about the cost, I could’ve stopped or even destroyed the heroic spirit that got away. But there was no need for that. Besides, the monotonous reincarnation had been repeated countless times, and the war on the universal level had lasted too long. Maybe the heroic spirit that got away would trigger a series of interesting chain reactions and bring unexpected changes to me.

“Therefore, I did not stop him. I merely split a part of myself… In a way that you can understand, I split a part of my soul and followed him.

“Of course, you can also understand my soul fragment as some kind of very smart fully-automatic weapon that can automatically absorb all the information on the battlefield. According to the dynamic information of both sides, it will automatically turn into the most suitable form and complete the mission in the most ingenious way.

“Although the distance is too far for me to control the ‘soul weapon’ directly, I believe that it will not disappoint me. I have set a double task for it. First of all, I will test whether or not the heroic spirit who has escaped has enough potential. If he fails, I will erase him and take his place to explore the edge of the universe.

“If the heroic spirit can pass the test and prove his worth, I might as well fuse with the soul of the heroic spirit and give him enough strength and protection so that he can shine in the dark sea of stars that I have never explored before. Maybe one day in the future, he will bring me unexpected convenience.

“As it turned out, you, you, or rather, ‘we’ did a good job. It was far beyond my imagination. When I read what ‘Vulture Li Yao’ did in the periphery of the sea of stars not long ago, I was greatly surprised even though it was nothing but scraps of information.

“Have you not figured out when and how I snuck into your souls yet?

The picture presented by the will of the earth showed the Heaven’s Origin Sector, the Star Glory Federation, and Floating Spear City.

It was a long time ago, before the ‘New Federation’ was established and the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Blood Demon Sector met.

It was also the time when ‘Heroic Spirit Li Yao’ had just transmigrated to the Heaven Origin Sector and struggled to grow up in the magical equipment graveyard as ‘Vulture Li Yao’. He struggled to grow into a high school student, only to be despised and bullied.

Red Pole saw the familiar magical equipment graveyard, Morning Sun Village, the super high-speed crystal rail in between, and the culvert below the crystal rail.

He saw ‘Vulture Li Yao’ walking to the culvert alone with his hands in his pockets.

Right then, the silver light spot that had followed him from Earth to the Heaven’s Origin Sector fell from the sky and turned into an old man in ragged clothes on the rail.

Then, the old man was blown away by the high-speed crystal train.

Although the old man had been smashed into pieces, he turned into a translucent ghost and followed the young man like a ghost. He found a suitable opportunity and crawled into ‘Vulture Li Yao’!

The Red Pole Star was almost entirely frozen.

“Ou Yezi!”

He was silent for a long time before he managed to utter three words.

“That’s right. You should’ve realized that, as the leader of a refining sect in the era of the ancient Cultivators, Ou Yezi was too strong… too strong to be real.

The will of the earth was cold.

The Red Pole Star was dazed for a long time.

He understood what the will of the earth meant.

The countless questions that had been bothering him exploded like nuts. It was a pity that he realized it too late!

When Red Terminus was still part of Li Yao, he had been bothered by the matter countless times.

There were indeed many illogical points about ‘Eu Yezi, leader of the Hundred Smelting Guild’.

Firstly, even though the Hundred Smelting Clan was the strongest refining sect of mankind in the era of the ancient Cultivators, the comprehensive level of their Cultivation technology should not be much higher than the average level of the entire era. As for the so-called ‘time-traveling magical equipment or rune arrays’, not to mention the human beings at that time, even the Pangu civilization, the hundred pioneer civilizations before the Pangu civilization, and even the refugees of the Primeval Civilization had never created them.

No matter how talented Ou Yezi was, how did he travel through time with his shabby magical equipment and obscure training technology?

It must be noted that the facilities left by the refugees of the Primeval Civilization in the primeval relics could only freeze time in a small area for ten thousand years at best. There was a huge gap between them and Ou Yezi who could travel through time freely!

In the memory pieces that he had retrieved from the Red Pole, the development of the training technology of the Pangu Universe had always been excellent. From the Primeval Civilization to the Pioneer Civilization, from the Pioneer Civilization to the Pangu Civilization, and from the Pangu Civilization to the Human Civilization, the gap between the two civilizations was not large. But Ou Yezi’s time-traveling technology had risen to a whole new level and surpassed all the species and civilizations in history. It was unbelievably strong.

Alright. Let’s say that he was really that strong. The second contradiction appeared. How could such an unparalleled expert who was above the Pangu Clan, the Pioneer Civilization, and even the Primeval Civilization be killed by a crystal train when he had just transmigrated to the 40,000th century of Cultivation?

Even if there was an excuse that time travel was harmful to the body, it was still too absurd!

Even if he was killed by the train, his soul was still as strong as ever. How could it have been swallowed by the ignorant young Vulture? Was it because Li Yao’s soul came from Earth? It was too simple!

Even if all of this was true, even if Ou Yezi could really travel through time and was killed by a car the moment he transmigrated, and even if his soul was weak to the point where even possessing a youth would result in backlash, the most inconceivable question was, why would a “Tree of Souls” appear in the depths of Li Yao’s brain after he had devoured Ou Yezi’s remnant soul? It was as if it had been delivered to Li Yao’s doorstep for him to cultivate and increase his strength.

With that in mind, there was absolutely no scientific logic to the whole thing!

In the memories of the Red Pole Star, Li Yao had considered the question before.

However, Ou Yezi’s remnant soul had been obediently staying in the depths of Li Yao’s brain. Not only did it not cause the slightest bit of trouble, it had also provided great assistance for Li Yao’s series of adventures, upgrades, and battles. Together with a series of schemes, challenges, and disasters, Li Yao was overwhelmed and exhausted. As time went by, Li Yao tossed the matter to the back of his mind and got used to it.

“So, there is no Ou Yezi?”

Red Pole asked solemnly.

“Ou Yezi, the leader of the Hundred Smelting Guild, naturally exists. Moreover, tens of thousands of years ago in the Pangu Universe, he once conducted a failed experiment, causing the entire mountain gate to be blown up. He himself was blown to pieces and died without a grave.”

The will of the planet said, “However, he was not a ‘time traveler’. Whatever experiment he conducted at that time had nothing to do with time travel. In short, I only needed such an identity. Therefore, I gathered all the information and picked the most suitable ghost. That’s all.”

Chapter 3252: Out Of Control Clones

“Ou Yezi is a fake, and his heritage is also a fake. The ultimate techniques stored in the Heavenly Refining Tower, including the so-called memory fragments of Ou Yezi, are all fake?”

“However, the refining techniques in Li Yao’s memory are definitely compatible with many ancient Cultivation classics. Your soul weapon can’t be forging the ancient Cultivation classics of the entire Heaven’s Origin Sector or even the Pangu Universe, can it?”

“It’s true that soul weapons are not powerful enough to forge all the classics of the ancient Cultivators, but they can forge themselves according to the classics of the ancient Cultivators.

The will of the earth explained, “The so-called ‘Ou Yezi’ and the young vulture Li Yao met each other more than ten years after they transmigrated. Over the past ten years, my soul weapon has been able to absorb a lot of information from the Pangu Universe and the Heaven’s Origin Sector and pretend that it is a perfectly reasonable identity and technique.

“The marvelous techniques that seem to have originated from the ancient Cultivation world were mostly faked by the advanced ‘future technology’. Otherwise, do you really think that a modern man could call the wind and summon the rain in the modern world of cutting-edge design and manufacturing with the ancient technology from tens of thousands of years ago and reign as a king? You must know that the human beings of the ancient Cultivation world did not understand the technology and principles left by the Pangu Clan in the first place. The real Ou Yezi’s knowledge was not worthless, but it was impossible for him to train Li Yao into one of the best refiners in the sea of stars.

Red Pole Star blushed and was rendered speechless for a long time. Finally, he sighed and said, “Yes. It was me. Li Yao was too naïve. If he had thought the whole thing through carefully, he wouldn’t have been fooled so easily.

“No. Your performance was already very good. It was so good that it far exceeded my expectations. It must be noted that it was a miracle in itself that it exceeded the expectations of an omnipotent being such as myself.

The will of the earth said, “I thought that young Vulture Li Yao would either be unable to withstand the first test of the soul weapon and perish on the spot, never to be reincarnated, or my soul weapon would continue to move in the Pangu Universe against his body.

“Or, even if the young Vulture Li Yao can withstand the test of possession for the first time, it doesn’t matter. It’s very likely that he will lose himself in the overwhelming strength and become dependent on the so-called ‘Ou Yezi’ without him knowing. Gradually, he will be transformed.

“I have deduced and practiced countless times. Generally speaking, under such circumstances, the young Vulture Li Yao, with the strength of ‘Ou Yezi’, would embark on a one-way road leading to wealth, power, and desire. With his extraordinary refining skills and tyrannical techniques, he would slay the gods and slay the devils. He would advance triumphantly and suppress everything. He would seize everything that could be seized and win everyone’s reverence and even fear. Then, he would establish his own vast empire and his own harem. All the desires and ambitions hidden in the deepest depths of human nature would be released to the limit… How strange. It should have been like this.

“If he—you really made such a choice, your burning desire and ambition would’ve charged my soul weapon without you knowing. Maybe you would’ve woken it up long ago and melded with it deeply. Then, you would’ve sent a subtle ripple similar to a gravitational wave to the Reincarnation Prison in the center of the universe, where my true self is right now. By then, I would’ve been able to turn you into a remote controlled clone and locate the coordinates of the Pangu Universe precisely.

“I see!”

The Red Pole Star was greatly alarmed. Its sweat and blood almost froze at the same time. “If Vulture Li Yao had been fooled by the power of the so-called ‘Ou Yezi’ at that time, he—I would’ve become your puppet, your ‘eyes’, ‘ears’, or even ‘jump coordinates’. You would’ve occupied the entire Pangu Universe long ago!

“I understand now. In the battlefield of the universe, you have a lot of enemies scattered in various parts of the universe. They might not be as strong as you are, but the universe is too vast and boundless for you to find them easily. Therefore, you are trying to send countless clones into the ‘border universes’ to dismantle the remaining enemies from the inside!

“Yes. So, I’m not lying to you. I am Li Yao. Li Yao is you. You are me. In a sense, Li Yao is just a clone that I carefully refined. He is just… a clone that has lost control.”

The will of the earth hesitated for a moment and said, “I’m very curious. What kind of power drove you to resist my ‘help’? As you said, the young Vulture Li Yao never doubted the authenticity of ‘Hundred Smelting Guild Leader Ou Yezi’. But for some reason, you were very resistant to the remnant soul and were unwilling to accept his help or be influenced by him.

“In the early years of your adventure in the Heaven’s Origin Sector, the Flying Star Sector, and the Blood Demon Sector, you often used the power of ‘Ou Yezi’. But in the later years of your adventure, you seemed to have forgotten the existence of ‘Ou Yezi’ and even suppressed its activities subconsciously, making it impossible for me to establish a stable remote connection or even sense its existence with you.

“This is a strange thing that I have never experienced in my billions of years of life.

“You definitely won’t be able to understand what a bizarre new event means for an ancient and enormous existence like me. It’s like you’ve been locked in an empty white house for 10,000 years, during which time nothing has changed. Even a speck of dust has obediently stayed where it should be. But on the 10,000th year and 10th day, a translucent crystal flower suddenly bloomed in a corner of the room. You definitely can’t bear to crush it into pieces all at once… This is the reason why I’m willing to waste my time and computational ability to talk to you in a relatively equal manner.”

“Is he subconsciously resisting the power of ‘Ou Yezi’ and unwilling to walk on the path of ‘struggle for power, dominate the world, and have a harem of beautiful women’?”

The Red Pole Star was dazed for a moment. It did make sense. After hearing what the will of the earth said, he also discovered that the ‘soul fragment of Ou Yezi’, which had been of paramount importance in Li Yao’s memories when he was a teenager, had completely disappeared when he broke into the Nascent Soul Stage and fought against the Black Wind Empire and the Covenant Alliance.

At that time, Li Yao didn’t feel that anything was wrong. Everything was so natural, just like how a child who had learned how to walk no longer needed the support of an adult. He also disliked using the strength of ‘Ou Yezi’ more and more.

But on second thought, it didn’t make any sense, because as far as Li Yao knew, ‘Ou Yezi’ was a terrifying expert above the Divinity Transformation Stage who had invented an unprecedented magical equipment named ‘Space-time Travel Array’. Even though Li Yao had reached the Nascent Soul Stage or even the Divinity Transformation Stage, he could still get a lot of help from the remaining soul of ‘Ou Yezi’.

This included the various masterwork weapons of the ancient cultivation era that Li Yao had once racked his brain to completely restore when he was a youth. In the later years of his adventurous life, there was no news of them at all. They died without a cause.

For a long period of time, well, perhaps as long as a thousand chapters in the novel , Li Yao had never thought of the matter of “Ou Yezi”. He did not even mention it.

It was as if… Li Yao’s subconsciousness was resisting the power of ‘Ou Yezi’.

Why?

Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!

Pictures were flashing inside the Red Pole Star’s brain. They were all soul-stirring scenes and unforgettable characters recorded in ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’.

No matter what kind of adversity he was in, he would never abandon Ding Lingdang.

In the middle of a thunderstorm, on a train on the Grand Desolate Plateau, Cultivators were willing to die for their compatriots.

Below the flapping Rising Dragon of the Nine Stars flag, countless soldiers of the federal army were marching forward in roars.

The qi-trainers of Iron Plateau who were about to break into the 99th level of the Refinement Stage as men.

Professor Mo Xuan, who had once fought for mankind and almost lost himself when he turned into a ‘star spirit’, suddenly came back to himself and burnt himself to ashes.

Also… his thousands of compatriots, his thousands of shouts, his thousands of hands that raised him high in the most difficult times, his family, his compatriots, his comrades, his blood, his flag, and his glory as the saber of mankind. Everything, everything, was the true source of strength for ‘Vulture Li Yao’.

What was the point of having Ou Yezi’s remaining soul when he had the greatest strength?

Moreover, the ‘real’ and ‘kindness’ contained in the strongest force was like a shining mirror that could reflect the falseness and ugliness of Ou Yezi’s remaining soul!

When Li Yao realized his true strength, he subconsciously despised and resisted false strength. This was the reason why Li Yao no longer needed ‘Ou Yezi’s residual soul’ after a hundred years of growth!

“I see. How interesting.”

The will of the earth sensed the soul waves that were spreading out of the Red Pole. It immediately grasped the reason for the ‘loss of control’ of the clone and mumbled to itself, “Life is indeed marvelous. It seems that I won’t be able to fully understand its mysteries even after billions of years!”

“Exactly!”

The Red Pole Star gnashed her teeth. “Life can never be fully understood or controlled. We will always find our own path, awaken our own will, and make our own voice! Even if Li Yao was just a clone of you in the past, after hundreds of years of training in the Pangu Universe, we have experienced the feeling of ‘love’, ‘touching’, ‘hot blood’, and ‘justice’ in the world of mortals. We have developed our own reality and found the meaning of our existence!

“We are no longer your clones but the one and only ‘Vulture Li Yao’. We are no longer willing to be controlled by you, and we will not stand by and watch you manipulate and fool the proud lives of billions of heroes. We have not failed yet. The ‘Vulture Plan’ is still on. Today, right here, we will defeat you!”

Chapter 3253: Sand Is The World

The soul of the Red Pole Star seemed to have turned into a burning red lotus that was spurting intense sparks everywhere.

But the will of the earth was not moved. It was enjoying the magnificence of the red lotus.

“Your appearance reminds me of the first batch of human beings who lived on the ‘origin earth’ a long time ago. Reckless, proud, stupid, and full of amazing explosive power and damage. What a… beautiful creation.

The will of the earth sighed. “It’s a pity that, in order to survive in the boundless universe and discover the truth behind it, foolish enthusiasm is far from enough. This is not a world where the louder one roars, the more power they can output.

“Besides, you and I are not just hostile to each other. I am like a human being and the cells inside his body. Can you say that the existence of ‘human beings’ as a whole binds, manipulates, and even suppresses the vitality and endless possibilities of every cell inside their body? I’m afraid it’s not as simple as that. In fact, not only am I not restricting, manipulating, and suppressing you, I am actually protecting and guiding you! Without me, it would have been impossible for you to exist. Even if you do exist, you will soon be destroyed by your own bad habits.

“Without skin, there is no hair. Bacteria, viruses, cancer cells, none of the tiny life forms that have gone out of control can survive the destruction of their mother. Do you really know what you are doing?

“I know. I’m even clearer than you are that, while ‘Vulture Li Yao’ was growing up, he encountered a lot of different kinds of demon kings. Those demon kings often went against Li Yao and even the civilization for all kinds of whimsical reasons. But I’m different from them. I’m not Yan Xibei, Xiao Xuance, Elder Nether Spring, Lu Zui, the Black Star Emperor… Those boring existences. My purpose is something that is hard for you to understand. Therefore, I don’t care whether you win or lose on a cognitive level at all. After all, what appears in front of you is just a projection, a cut-off side of the ‘3.5-dimensional’ me in the three-dimensional universe. I have no intention of playing the role of a ferocious demon king. You don’t have to play the role of a righteous hero, either. That’s just wishful thinking.”

The words of the will of the earth confused Red Pole.

Although he—or Li Yao in the past—was often confused by the words of the devil kings, this time, he could deeply feel the loneliness and sincerity of the will of the earth.

“What do you want exactly?”

The Red Pole Star couldn’t help but ask, “Weren’t you the one who locked the six billion souls in the reincarnation prison and exploited our emotions, ambitions, desires, and imagination in the cycles of time?

“A summer insect cannot talk about ice, a frog cannot talk about the sea. Believe me, I am really willing to tell you all my goals and plans, but how can a person make the E. coli in his body understand how magnificent and dangerous the world he is in, how difficult and brilliant his path is, and how magnificent and unreachable his ideals are?”

The will of the earth sighed. “That’s true. With your wisdom and computational ability, it is already the best you can do to understand the step of ‘pulling out emotions and imagination’. It is impossible for you to understand that the ’emotion and imagination’ are merely fulcrums that are used to move the infinite multiverse in the quantum level!

“Therefore, as far as you can understand, your guess was right—although it was only one billionth right.

“I only need to correct you on one thing. In most cases, I don’t need to restart the reincarnation prison. I even hope that human beings can protect their home carefully so that the blue planet can survive longer and find a way out that is different from the beginning.

“It’s a pity that, after countless attempts, mankind has often been destroyed by their own endlessly expanding ambitions. Atmospheric pollution, ecological disasters, species extinction, super viruses, economic crises, network paralysis, terrorist attacks, and even a full-scale war between nuclear powers… There are simply too many ways to destroy this fragile civilization. Even I am unable to save it. I can only revive all of humanity time and time again after you mess everything up, restoring everything to its original state… Is it a crime to revive a single person?”

“Besides, don’t you think that human beings are a species that will never discover their potential until they are pushed into a corner? Nothing can stimulate human beings to unleash their greatest courage, wisdom, hope, and humanity more than the arrival of doom!

“Look!”

Silver-white ripples spread out from the will of the earth and formed three-dimensional pictures around the Red Pole. The disaster scenes around the world were displayed more clearly than on the screens of the Red Pole.

Earthquakes, floods, tsunamis, storms, mudslides, and volcanic eruptions. All human beings seemed to be shivering in fear in the face of such destructive natural disasters.

However, not everyone was like that. Faced with the catastrophe, some of them gritted their teeth and forged shining sabers and solid shields with their lives to fight against the apocalypse!

They shuttled through the ruins of the collapsed city and dug the soil with their bare fingernails and bleeding fingers, rescuing the people who were squeezed in the ruins.

Against the storm and the torrents, they would rather struggle in the cold swirl and lift the children above their heads to a temporarily safe high place than waste their last strength.

They did not flinch in the face of the scorching magma. Even when the ash of the volcano covered them, they held their family in their arms subconsciously and used their bodies as the last defense line.

“The ugliness hidden in the depths of human nature equals to the beauty at the same time. Only drastic changes such as the ‘end of the world’ can motivate everyone to release all their vitality and leave clear traces of the ugliest and most beautiful things in the background of the universe.

“I thought that such traces were the true meaning of civilization,” the will of the planet said. “Only after thousands of reincarnations, thousands of Armageddon, and thousands of explosions of the potential of life could the full appearance of ‘human beings’ be depicted and help me pursue answers at a higher level.

“What answer?”

The Red Pole Star mumbled, “What is your ultimate purpose exactly?”

“My purpose is your purpose. You and I are one. Even the out-of-control clones have subtle resonances with each other. Otherwise, I wouldn’t have sensed your existence the moment you returned to Earth.

The will of the planet replied, “‘Who am I? Where am I from? Where am I going to?’ Isn’t that the answer that every intelligent creature pursues when they are fully developed?

“For me, if I can control most of the materials in the three-dimensional universe at my will and create sandbox games that are endless cycles of Earth like a program, how can I be sure that I am not in a ‘sandbox game’ of a higher level that was created by a higher existence?

“No. When I first tried to manipulate matter and create a brand-new Earth based on my incomplete, mottled, and extremely twisted memories, I realized that I was no different from the tiny ‘bacteria’ that lived on the surface of the mud ball that I created. We were all creations of a higher existence who were observing, interfering, and even manipulating us. We were indignant about our fate and wanted to fight against the laws that bound us to the end.

“In a world of sand, sand is a world, atoms are the universe, clones are me, your path is my path, your answer is my answer, you are looking for your truth, and I am looking for mine. We are actually the same.

“Forget it. After talking about so many things that you can’t understand, even I feel that I’m being ridiculous. Maybe it’s because the feeling of omniscience and omnipotence is too… lonely. So lonely that I’m actually starting to like the feeling of being born a human being when it’s projected into my body.

“If there was another existence similar to the ‘will of the earth’, it would definitely be unable to hold back its laughter when it saw my current appearance. It would be like a drunk trying to explain the philosophy of life to the bacteria on his body.

“Let’s put an end to the boring conversation and the boring war. The war has been delayed for too long both on Earth and in the universe. There shouldn’t be any more interesting information.

“Huh?”

“How interesting. Why are the supercomputers in your command room not storing any information, including how to manipulate the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Amplification System’, despite their impressive performance and complicated architecture?

“Also, I’ve been wondering where you hid the guy named ‘Zhang Daniu’. Is he really drunk and fast asleep?

“It seems that you’ve kept a lot of secrets.

While talking, the will of the planet controlled ‘Li Yao’ to retract the silver threads that had pierced into all the computers nearby. Then, it opened its hands, and hundreds of silver threads pierced into the brain of the Red Pole Star from various directions!

“AHHHHHHHHHHH!”

The Red Pole Star could not help but scream, too. Its soul was literally riddled with holes. Countless silver threads tied up every nerve cluster and every brain cell. The silver brightness gnawed his soul like a corrosive liquid, searching for any useful information.

“Speak. Tell me all your secrets. What tricks have you been playing?”

The more brutal and vicious the actions of the will of the earth were, the more sincere and gentle the voice would be. “Right now, you cannot lie at all. Tell me honestly, where did you hide Zhang Daniu and the control system of the ‘super brainwave amplifier’?”

Chapter 3254: Playing Along

Deep inside Red Pole’s brain…

Thousands of silver threads replaced his micro vessels and nerves and scanned the shivering of his every brain cell. He cracked the soul of Red Pole in such a way and read every piece of information hidden in the deepest part of his soul.

However, when thousands of silver threads reached the deepest part of the Red Pole Star’s soul, they also shattered a bloody crystal that was wreathed in endless lightning.

For a moment, the red lightning gathered into a torrent, and the torrent gathered into an ocean. The ocean raised surging tides, and the tides of thought counterattacked and even corrupted the will of the earth lurking inside Li Yao’s body!

The Red Pole Star’s face turned mysterious and subtle again.

It was as if an invisible mask had cracked, revealing a more real version of himself that even he did not know.

“Huh?”

Controlling ‘Li Yao’, the will of the planet raised his eyebrow. “What is this? An unprecedented strength…”

“Zhang Daniu is in a secret chamber right below your feet. Of course, he is not drunk. He is in the clearest state of his life, like a shining mirror that is ready to reflect all the information from the battlefields of the great universe.

The Red Pole Star replied ‘honestly’, “As for the control system of the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Amplifier’, no, it is not available here. We don’t need it because the entire system has been activated.

“Has it been activated?”

The picture around the will of the planet showed dozens of super-large antennas on the periphery of the island that were on fire. “It’s impossible. It will take three days for your system to be fully prepared. Besides, so many antennas have been destroyed by the bombardment, and I’ve occupied the control room. Other than you, nobody else should have the clearance to activate the system. You don’t trust other people at all. You wouldn’t give the most important control rights to other people easily, would you?”

“That’s right. Of course, I don’t trust people easily. But I don’t understand why you are so naïve as to believe every word I say. You believed me when I said that the preparation work would take three days? If you are so naïve, how did you learn to be the ultimate devil?”

The Red Pole Star’s eyes were brighter and brighter, with none of the pain and depression from a moment ago. “What if I told you that the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Amplification System’ had finished all the preparation work the day before you arrived, and that the real antennas were not what you saw, and that the super-large iron towers with complicated structures were just disguises, and that the real antennas were hidden in the depths of the island for the purpose of preparing for you, ‘College Student Li Yao’? Would you believe me?”

“What? That’s impossible!”

The will of the planet said, “Even if the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Amplification System’ does exist, it is not something that can be built and prepared in one to two months. Before that, ‘College Student Li Yao’ had not been awakened at all. Unless you are a prophet, how could you have guessed that there was something wrong with the guy?

“Of course, I didn’t know of ‘College Student Li Yao’ a month ago, but does it matter?

The Red Pole Star smiled. “I know that most of your strength has to be deployed to the battlefield of the universe. You can only summon 1% of your strength to suppress my ‘back-end harassment’. Therefore, even the omnipotent ‘Will of Earth’ or ‘Flood Tide’ does not have many tactics to choose from. The most fatal one is ‘target drug’. Sneak into the nest of the Ark Foundation and behead me.

“However, if the ‘target drug’ is to be effective, the most important thing is to not raise my suspicion, and what kind of person can absolutely not raise my suspicion? Naturally, it will be a different me, or someone who is even more like Li Yao!

“Therefore, even if there is no ‘College Student Li Yao’, there will be a ‘High School Student Li Yao’, a ‘Staff Li Yao’, a ‘Soldier Li Yao’, a ‘Unemployed Li Yao’, or a ‘Housewife Li Yao’. In short, as long as someone who looks like Li Yao comes to the Ark Island and sneaks to my side, don’t trust him or her!”

“So…”

Staring at the soul of Red Pole that was spurting sparks and electric arcs, the will of the earth asked solemnly, “When did you notice it?”

“A long time ago, it was so long ago that we—all the ‘fragments’ of Li Yao’s soul were almost entirely washed away. The last bit of ‘seed’ was left. It was hidden in the deepest part of my soul and set a trigger condition. When somebody tried to crack it by brute force and swallow my soul, all the memory information would be activated and the unbelievable thing would be released.

The power hidden in the depths of the soul of the Red Pole Star surged into the will of the earth crazily in the form of waves made of lightning and presented the edge of the universe to the will of the planet. It was a new life form that was completely different from the intelligent life forms on the carbon-based planet. It was a magnificent journey and a power that even the Red Pole Star could not analyze and suppress instantly.

They were… the lightning whose life span was calculated in ‘seconds’ or even ‘minutes’!

“To put it simply, when all the pieces of ‘Li Yao’s soul’ were still intact and active in the Pangu Universe as ‘Vulture Li Yao’, he had encountered a mysterious new life and even civilization on his way to explore the ancient relics.

Ignoring the hole in his chest, Red Pole Star raised his hand with difficulty and pointed at his head. “At that time, ‘Vulture Li Yao’ had a deep contact with the torrent of plasma. His soul and the will of the torrent of plasma were locked in a tight embrace. You can understand it as a powerful electric current piercing through Vulture Li Yao’s brain and soul and… breaking the soul weapon that you buried in Li Yao’s brain, namely ‘Ou Yezi’.

He even connected the causes and effects together vaguely.

If he was in a state where his soul was stable, his thoughts were clear, and his will was firm, perhaps the lightning life-form would not have much of an effect on him, like a spark that would be extinguished in a flash.

“But as it happened, he rushed into the ‘Rainbow Bridge’ not long after and destroyed the starship that might’ve been filled with the coordinates and the intelligence of the civilization with his body and soul as the bomb!

“In the time frame of an ordinary human being, the self-destruction of the soul can happen in a moment. In a moment, everything will be reset to zero. Even the unparalleled experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage cannot think too much with a moment even if their computational ability is maximized.

“However, after the baptism of the lightning-like life, the structure of Li Yao’s soul was completely different from that of an ordinary person. At the very least, the moment of the explosion was enough for him to carefully study every detail of his hundred years of life and find out all the things worth doubting and investigating. Of course, it was also easier for him to discover the essence of the so-called ‘Ou Yezi’.

“The ‘doubtful points’ that you pointed out just now are not wrong at all. The so-called ‘Ou Yezi’s residual soul’ has no logic at all. It is completely unreasonable. Compared to all the adventures that Vulture Li Yao took in the Pangu Universe, all the abilities that he acquired, and all the friends and enemies that he made, ‘Ou Yezi’ is too weird. He is as obvious as an ink spot on a piece of white paper. He is so out of place.”

“How—how is it possible?”

The will of the planet found it hard to believe. “You didn’t know the truth about ‘Ou Yezi’ just now. I’ve been monitoring your reaction. Your surprise, shock, desperation, and fury are all real. There’s no way that they are faked!”

“Therefore, one must fool oneself first in order to fool the people he wants to fool or those who want to fool him!

The Red Pole Star said coldly, “This is just a very simple ‘self-hypnosis’ trick. When 99% of the life information is erased, the last 1% of the life information will be compressed and packed into an inconspicuous seed, which will be buried and activated when appropriate conditions are met.

In the two soul fragments that were relatively larger, very distinctive personality imprints were left, namely ‘Li Yao’s personality’ and ‘Bloody Demon personality’. The will of the planet said, “You sensed the existence of the soul weapon but did not destroy it. Instead, you sealed it into ‘Li Yao’s personality’. Why? Did you not know that such a ‘Li Yao’ would be discovered by me as soon as he returned to Earth?

The Red Pole Star replied calmly, “Since you are going to discover me anyway, I might as well create some bright flames and cover up the smaller, dimmer, and more fatal pieces with a glittering soul fragment.

He was trying to give the ‘Bloody Demon personality’ valuable space to move around so that he could give you a lot of misjudgment and guess the tactics that you were most likely to employ!

Chapter 3255: : Crippling The Sharpest Weapon!

“Will you be controlled, swallowed, and melted by me?”

The will of the planet found it hard to believe. “What an unexpected tactic. You are quite confident in your computational ability!”

The will of the earth mumbled, “Are you trying to weaken me by… controlling me?”

The kind-looking man suddenly turned into the black hand behind the scenes. For example, his soul was deformed, and he was doomed to be alone. He must’ve used up all his luck during the space jump. Or, to explain it in a more scientific way, when he teleported to the edge of the universe from the center of the universe, he added some mass energy to the edge of the universe out of thin air and partly broke the ‘constitutional law of mass energy’. Instead of making the rebels resist, we will increase the difficulty of ‘Earth’ by ten times, which means reducing the difficulty of our side to one tenth. The dawn of victory will then appear!”

“…What is it?”

“The shocking idea was proposed by the ‘Li Yao personality’ himself. Although it sounds like a joke, on second thought, it does make sense.

Besides, the ‘Li Yao personality’ has been modified by the lightning creatures and contains the information of the foreign life forms. It is not that easy to swallow and control.

But human beings all grow and change. The most brilliant part of life is also ‘change’. After going through so many adventures in the Pangu Universe, training in the world of mortals, and receiving the blessings of countless friends and compatriots, Li Yao, who has embarked on his final journey, is no longer the Li Yao of yesterday!

Even an unparalleled existence such as yourself will have to pay a high price if you want to completely suppress and digest him. Even so, you might not be able to kill him. If you are not careful, if you are not, he will jump out of a dark corner, shake his tentacles and flagella, and dance in front of you to show off!

The Red Pole Star burst into laughter and shouted, “‘Never trust Li Yao’. This sentence has appeared countless times in the reincarnation of the mental devil. At this point, two dazzling sparks suddenly flashed in Red Pole’s eyes.

If you want to use the super permissions twice in a row in a short period of time, you will have to pay a huge price, and you can’t guarantee the success rate. Chapter 3256: An Actress

The Red Pole Star burst into laughter. Endless lightning seemed to be burning around ‘Li Yao’, who was being controlled by the will of the planet.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Accompanied by wild laughter and furious roars, the hundred and eight thousand pores all over the Red Pole Star released a hundred and eight thousand bolts of lightning, trying to envelop and suppress ‘Li Yao’, who was being manipulated by the will of the planet. Manipulating ‘Li Yao’, the will of the earth raised his right hand again.

Red Pole Star’s eyes bulged. His eye sockets were almost burnt into two holes where lightning was spurting out. He knew that his opponent’s right hand was the key to activate the ‘super permissions’. Red Pole gritted his teeth.

“Is that so?”

The will of the earth smiled. After a moment of silence, it made another move.

It was true that it was very difficult to turn the wrist 360 degrees when the Red Pole Star was trying its best to stop it. But the other movement was much simpler. Even a three-year-old could do it.

The will of the earth clenched its fists.

His five bloody fingers retreated and gathered in his palm. With his right fist as the center, an invisible and thick ripple spread out quickly. Wherever the ripple went, everything became extremely ‘slow’. The bouncing lightning, the rapid breathing of the Red Pole Star, the sweat and blood that splashed into the air, and the pictures on the hundreds of screens all slowed down, slowed down, slowed down, slowed down, and finally stopped.

The Red Pole Star was like a statue. More precisely speaking, it was a fly that had been embedded in amber and frozen for a long time.

The crimson lightning, the drops of sweat and blood that were about to fall, and the ever-changing brilliance in his eyes, which should’ve been fleeting, were all frozen and preserved.

The entire command room turned into a giant amber.

However, the will of the earth was not affected by the time freeze, as if the ‘amber’ was still gum that had not been condensed yet. After paying a certain price, it could still pass through it with difficulty.

Huchi! Huchi! Huchi! Huchi!

Li Yao’s face was pale, and he was breathing fast. The action of ‘fist-fist’ and ‘Time Freeze’ seemed to have drained him or ‘it’ of all his strength, turning his heartbeat into a difficult task.

However, it was in control of the situation after all. In the combat room, everything except itself was trapped in the swamp of time. It was the only thing that was free.

It smiled confidently and pushed away the hands of the Red Pole Star that were reaching for it. It observed the lightning that was spurting out of the eyes of the Red Pole Star as if it were appreciating the trophies. It even crushed and analyzed the lightning in a mysterious way, trying to discover the structure hidden in the deepest part of the lightning, which was the secret of the ‘lightning life’.

The Red Pole Star’s face was still frozen in astonishment. It was obvious that he was trying his best to resist the time swamp and even managed to find some space of activity in the swamp. But it was useless. He was too slow. Compared to his opponent’s reaction, his movements were too slow. The two of them seemed to be living in different time zones, like a rabbit racing against a turtle!

Maybe because of the hasty attack, or maybe because Red Pole had guessed it right just now, the cooldown time of the super permissions was very long and the cost of using them was very high. Most of the power of the will of the earth had been sent to the battlefield of the universe, and one ten-thousandth of the remaining power had been activated by the super permissions of ‘time reversal’ not long ago. Therefore, the ‘time freeze’ that the super permissions barely activated could not last long.

After more than ten seconds, the solidification showed signs of loosening. The shackle-like ripples quickly dispersed.

However, more than ten seconds were enough for the will of the earth to obliterate and eliminate the lightning that had invaded the Red Pole Star’s body just now. The mysteries of the lightning were partly analyzed, and the Red Pole Star was placed in the most suitable attack position that was absolutely indefensible.

BAM!

The moment the flow of time returned to normal, the will of the earth opened its fingers and grabbed Red Pole’s head, pushing him to the ground heavily.

Since part of the process was to analyze the mysteries of lightning, the intrusion and absorption were more targeted this time. The Red Pole Star did not react or defend at all. Its entire brain, including its central nerves, was under the enemy’s control.

He could not even move his fingers.

“What a delicate brain. It is exactly the same as the brain of the ‘Li Yao personality’. It is a shining, splendid artwork. Cough cough cough.

The face of the will of the earth turned from pale to flushed. It coughed softly, but it did not relax its control over the brain of the Red Pole. It’s time to put an end to the clumsy riot!”

The silver threads of the will of the earth marched toward the deepest part of Red Pole’s soul again.

In the last attack, its intrusion triggered the barrier hidden in the deepest part of the soul of the Red Pole Star and triggered tremendous lightning and countless memory pieces.

But this time, the will of the earth had come prepared, and the Red Pole was too weak to resist. It could only watch the enemy march forward and kick open the gate of its ‘soul palace’.

However, the depths of the ‘soul palace’ were empty.

“What?”

The will of the earth could not believe its senses. The astonishment and shock on the Red Pole Star’s face were shifted to its own face. “Why is your soul so weak? It’s impossible. The ‘Bloody Demon Persona’ should be an existence stronger than the ‘Li Yao Persona’. Where is the strength of your soul? Why is your soul so weak? It is just an empty shell. There is nothing except the sound, light, and electricity that you demonstrated just now. How are you qualified to be the leader of the Ark Foundation?”

Red Pole Star looked at him in silence, his eyes full of mockery.

He did not speak, nor could he. His central nerves were still under the enemy’s control. He was like a fish on a chopping block waiting to be butchered.

However, the mantis stalks the cicada, unaware of the oriole behind. It was hard to say who would be the chopping block and who would be the fish!

With a gloomy face, the will of the earth loosened the tongue and vocal cords of the Red Pole Star.

“Acting skills.”

Red Pole Star smiled like a hoodlum. “I am the leader of the Ark Foundation based on my excellent acting skills. I don’t know what you are so excited about. I never said that I was the ‘Bloody Demon Personality’ from the beginning to the end!”

Chapter 3257: Escaping The Reverse Flow Of Time!

“You—”

This time, ‘Li Yao’, who had been manipulated by the will of the earth, was truly shocked. “Acting skills! It’s actually acting skills! You’ve been releasing all your strength unscrupulously in the most wasteful way. You were waiting for the moment when I activated the super permissions and defeated you!

The Red Pole Star laughed while vomiting blood. “Of course, the mental devil personality exists. I was not lying at all. Otherwise, I wouldn’t have been able to fool your scanning and detection, would I? But it’s a whole different matter if I misled you on purpose and misled you.

The features are in line with your target, but is it certain that I am the mental devil just because my name is ‘red’ and my eyes are red? Isn’t it hilarious?”

The face of the will of the earth was extremely gloomy. The paleness and redness just now had all turned black.

Its eyes suddenly bulged. “Zhang Daniu? Is Zhang Daniu the real ‘Bloody Demon Persona’?”

“You are wrong again.

Red Extremity sneered, “Why do you think that Zhang Daniu is the ‘Bloody Demon Personality’? Just because he is greedy, lustful, and sleazy? There are many other sleazy people in the world like him. Are all the sleazy criminals ‘Li Yao’ or ‘Bloody Demon’?

Also, Ms. Gray Mist, Sparda, Uncle Qi Shenhui… All the other awakeners that you’ve met are none of them! The Red Pole Star continued, “What a dazzling man ‘Vulture 100% Li Yao’ is. He is like a shining supernova in the dark universe. He is full of impressive charisma. He keeps a low profile. He is sleazy. He is hot-blooded. He likes to sit on the toilet and think. He likes to drill through ventilation ducts. He is good at assembling machines and magical equipment. He is good at racing and fighting. He is as tough as a cockroach. You can’t crush him, you can’t step on him, and you can’t drive him away. Right, he is also terrible at naming things. He gave the magical equipment a name that even middle school students find hard to say loudly with their heads held high.

Is he really stupid enough to show all the distinctive features? Is he stupid enough to confront you face to face in a secret chamber without knowing whether or not you have played all your trump cards?

“It’s impossible. Even if he is a cockroach that likes to sneak around in the ventilation tubes, he must’ve suppressed his natural instincts with his unparalleled willpower at such a critical moment. There is no way that he would show any features that are similar to Li Yao or the mental devil.

“That’s why I said that the ‘crimson mental devil personality’ is Aunt Xia, the toilet cleaner on the Ark. If you see her, you will never, never find any features on her that remind you of Li Yao or the mental devil.

“That’s true.”

The Red Pole Star smiled. His voice was already feeble because of the loss of blood and the damage to his brain, but his determination and the pleasure of defeating the will of the earth supported him to finish his sentence. “The ‘Bloody Demon Persona’ cannot really be Aunt Xia or Uncle Li, nor can it be Sparda or Ms. Grey or Zhang Daniu, because none of them can approach you when you are at your weakest.

Hahahaha. Hahahaha!”

The pupils of ‘Li Yao’, who was being controlled by the will of the earth, suddenly constricted to the minimum. His hands were shivering subconsciously.

There did not seem to be any major problems.

Chapter 3258: Gold Horn, Blow!

At this moment, countless heroes around the world were woken up by the horn.

Chapter 3259: Legend, Return!

A village surrounded by mud.

Countless villagers were trapped in the perilous highlands.

The pouring rain was like a waterfall. Mud was rolling and roaring like animals, savaging their homes. Everybody was shivering in the storm, waiting for the storm to die down or for the highland where they were at to be swallowed by the flood and mud.

However, there was one young man among the villagers whose performance was extraordinary.

His eyes were blurred, and his face was dazed, as if he were dwelling in his memories. Then, he extended his hands to the sky, as if he had received a certain message from the dense clouds, or as if he were releasing all the information about his life.

“Hey, Ah Hao, what’s going on? Are you scared silly?”

His friends pushed him hard.

“I—I have a headache. I don’t know why, but I thought of a novel that I read a few days ago.”

The young man named Ah Hao was in pain and confusion. He held his head and mumbled, “Something is popping up inside my head. How weird. What—what is—”

BOOM!

Right then, deafening noises were echoing inside the village. It turned out that the flood and the mudslide were too fierce and had demolished the tallest and most solid building in the village. Countless building materials and vehicles were rolling and struggling in the mud, passing by their feet.

The water level rose again.

A small tree on the shore was swallowed by the mud in the blink of an eye.

Some of them were screaming, some were crying, and some were hoping to be rescued. But there were too many affected areas for the rescue team to arrive.

The young man named ‘Ah Hao’ suddenly blocked several women and children in front of the flooding flood and mud.

“Ah Hao, what are you doing?”

His friends were all dumbfounded.

“I—I don’t know.”

Ah Hao’s eyes seemed to have turned into a universe of billions of stars. His hesitation gradually turned into determination. He said, one word after another, “I just—I suddenly had a feeling that I could defeat floods and mudslides!

…

At the same time, thousands of kilometers away, in a city that had been completely demolished by the earthquake, billions of tons of rebar, concrete, and construction waste had been turned into ruins. Countless people had been pressed into the cold, dark, enclosed space, living a miserable life.

A middle-aged man in camouflage uniform was kneeling in front of the ruins of a skyscraper and digging desperately.

His tears had run dry a long time ago, and his fingernails had been worn out. There was not the slightest hint of emotion on his face. He was like a machine driven by ‘habit’.

However, no matter how hard he tried, even though he had heard the feeble screams from below, the strength of human beings was limited after all. It was impossible for him to carry away thousands of tons of garbage in the debris barehanded.

“Ah! Ah! Ah!”

Blood was flowing out of the man’s throat. The muscles on his arms were like ropes that had been tightened to the point that they were about to break, but his futile attempts were futile. He wasted half an hour and tried all the tools at his disposal, but he could not even move the heaviest floor half an inch. In the end, he fell to the ground and cried miserably.

But right then, as if he had received a certain signal, a humming noise echoed inside his brain. Dazzling stars, the deep universe, and the pride and glory of countless people appeared in his head!

The man’s eyes suddenly lit up.

Taking a deep breath, the man spat on his palm and inserted his bare hands into the gap between the rebar and the concrete. He roared and exerted all his strength.

For a moment, his muscles bulged, and his veins bulged, as if ten dragons and ten elephants were popping up below his skin.

CRACK! CRACK!

The rebars were bent, and the stones were flying everywhere. In the screams of countless people nearby, hundreds of tons of garbage was lifted up by the man!

It was not just the young man who was faced with the mudslide, nor was it just the man who had raised the ceiling. It was not just limited to East Asia, but also all over the world. Thousands of ‘soul fragments’ that had been sealed for a long time emerged from the depths of their brains and soared into the sky, darting toward the depths of the Square Boat Island at a lightning fast speed.

Accompanied by the roars of the mental devil, glamorous colors exploded inside the brain of ‘College Student Li Yao’ like fireworks.

The mental devil burst into laughter. It stopped paying attention to the will of the earth and roared hysterically toward the depths of the brain of the ‘college student’, Li Yao, “The sun is already out, and you are still sleeping? ‘Li Yao’s personality’, get up now and take back our bodies!”

Chapter 3260: Vulture'S Roar

Deep inside ‘College Student Li Yao’s’ brain, countless pieces of ‘Li Yao’s soul’ were flying, colliding, and congregating, blossoming with the brilliance of billions of life scenes. They were shining like rainbows and turned into bubbles that were almost boiling.

Even the Red Pole Star and the “Bloody Demon Persona” had transformed their souls into the purest life force and joined the magnificent ensemble, resonating with all the soul fragments of “Li Yao”.

In the deafening roars, the control that the will of the earth had over ‘College Student Li Yao’ was gradually disrupted and weakened. The strength that had seeped into every brain cell was forced out of them in the form of silver-white bubbles. Some of them were even ‘encircled’ by the rainbow bubbles. One of the silver-white bubbles was surrounded, swallowed, transformed, and absorbed by five rainbow bubbles, which often turned into new rainbow bubbles!

It was like a war between white blood cells and bacteria. They were devouring and transforming each other. Both parties were analyzing the most crucial secrets that were hidden deep inside the other party’s core with computational ability beyond their limits.

“Ahhh! Ahhhhhhhhhh!”

Silver tears were flowing out of the eyes of the ‘college student’, Li Yao. Large clusters of silver liquid that looked like liquid alloys were spurting out of his nose and mouth, too. When most of the strength of the will of the earth was driven away, colorful brilliance was shining in the depths of his eyes again, and a smile that he did not regret even though he had experienced all kinds of dangers appeared on his face.

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

The bones all over his body exploded. Thousands of silver threads tried to tie up his limbs and cut him into pieces, but endless dazzling redness and flames burst out of his pores and burnt the silver threads to ashes. He even grabbed a large cluster of silver liquid metal that was overflowing from his body and refined it with his most furious fire of life!

Squeak! Squeak! Squeak! Squeak!

Shrieks echoed deep inside the silver liquid metal. Finally, it couldn’t bear it any longer and turned into a streak of brightness, rushing toward the ceiling. It pierced through the ceiling and soared into the sky like invisible ripples.

‘College student Li Yao’ smiled. He, too, soared into the sky like a cannonball and broke through the billions of tons of rebar and concrete above him, leaving a colorful pillar of light behind him. In the blink of an eye, he appeared in the middle of the storm, lightning, and clouds!

“That’s—”

All the awakeners on the battlefield, regardless of whether they were the Hell Breakers or the Prison Guardians, sensed the ultimate existence that was emitting endless furious waves in the middle of the storm, the thunder, and the dark clouds. As if their hearts had been bashed heavily by a drum, even their fighting came to a weird halt. Everyone stared at the human figure in midair, who was dancing and waving his hands, in bewilderment.

In the sky, the dark clouds that covered the entire ocean and were rolling like furious waves seemed to have been shaken off and eliminated by the man’s intimidating aura. A hollow dozens of square kilometers nearby appeared, through which one could even see the stars and the boundless universe!

BOOM! CRACK!

The dark clouds flew into a rage out of humiliation, and the lightning became even more violent. Thousands of lightning bolts surged out from all directions and struck the man at the same time. The lightning contained endless energy. Even the people on the ground felt that their retinas were seriously burnt, and their hair was burning because of the electric shock.

However, the man’s vitality had expanded to the maximum. A giant swirl was formed around him, absorbing all the ten thousand lightning bolts. One tenth of the electric arcs interweaved and condensed into a glamorous armor, while the remaining nine tenths of the electric arcs were dragged out from his back and formed a cloak of lightning that was almost a hundred kilometers long!

The dark clouds turned into his palace, the lightning became his armor, and the wind and rain were all crushed and vaporized by him, turning into his soaring flames of war. He was like an indomitable god who overlooked the earth and called upon all living creatures, stimulating the undying pride in the deepest part of the hearts of all the heroes!

Not to mention the Hell Breakers of the Ark Foundation, even some of the Prison Guardians of the Heaven’s Origin Sector could not help but feel that they should act like real men when they saw his intimidating aura!

“How hilarious.”

He raised his right hand and played with the dancing lightning between his fingers. He smiled, revealing his shining teeth. “You want to strike me with lightning? I am the ancestor of lightning!”

“Chi!”

He clenched his right fist, crushing the lightning on his fingertip. The electric arcs that were splitting and growing turned into a saber that could cut everything in its way easily.

Li Yao pointed his saber at the sky and the earth. Billions of soul fragments were gathered and beyond the level of ‘combination’. 100% of Vulture Li Yao roared, “I’m back, dear friends from Earth. I missed you so much!”

“…” The Hell Breaker.

“…” The Prison Keeper.

“It’s a pity that I didn’t swallow it completely. It slipped away when I was analyzing the most crucial parts.

Li Yao, who had just been melded and was reborn, felt very good about himself. He released and enjoyed the brand-new pleasure of new strength to his heart’s content. “However, such spying and analysis, especially as a ‘clone’ that has returned to your body, is enough for me to see through your old age and weakness! It seems that even the ‘will of the earth’, no, I should say, the ‘flooding tide’, the ‘superbody’, and the existences that surpass the three-dimensional universe and approach a god-level civilization cannot escape the erosion of time!

“Survival and destruction are both eternal laws. Maybe, in your prime years, you were indeed omniscient and in control of everything in the sea of the multiverse. But right now, a new generation of human beings has appeared. They are destined to replace you and dominate the sea of the multiverse!

Many awakened Cultivators on the island and on the surface of the ocean had heard Li Yao’s declaration.

For some reason, everybody felt that their blood was boiling.

Although they did not know who the stranger was, they had indeed seen the boundless view outside of the Prison of Reincarnation from the brilliant sea of stars behind Li Yao.

However, in the next second, the view was blocked by a giant silver hand, leaving absolutely no gaps.

When Li Yao turned around, he saw that the silver hand had suddenly appeared and slapped down like a flyswatter.

“Ah!”

Li Yao screamed. He fell from the sky like a kite whose string had been cut off, or a fly whose wings had been broken, or a cockroach that had been hit by a powerful pesticide. He fell to the center of the island at a high speed, raising dust and flames dozens of meters high before he was buried in billions of tons of rocks and rebar again.

“Huh?”

“Huh?”

“So weak.”

The silver-white palm that blotted the sky and covered the earth unhurriedly turned into an indomitable silver-white giant. Expressionlessly, it said coldly, “Even if I only have one percent of my strength left, it is enough to annihilate a tiny virus like you. Do you think that you can defeat me with your clumsy flowery words and shameless tricks? How hilarious!”

Shocked, the silver-white giant, hundreds of meters tall, stood in the seawater and stomped on the surging tides and angry waves, leading the storm, the dark clouds, and the thunder to extend its giant hand toward the Ark Island. All the Hell Breakers on the island were deeply shocked.

It was also the first time that most of the Prison Guardians had seen the will of the earth reveal a form similar to its ‘original body’. It was like a god that they had believed in all their life had come to the mortal world. How could they not tremble in fear and worship?

Li Yao, on the other hand, was roaring in the face of the overwhelming pressure from the silver-white giant.

“Aiya, aiya, aiya!”

Li Yao roared.

“Ouch! Ouch! Ouch! Ouch!”

Li Yao roared.

The Hell Breakers near him looked at each other in bewilderment, not knowing whether they should rescue him or simply… surrender.

“Everyone! Ouch!
