Li Yao’s intermittent voice came over from the depths of the ruins. “As you can see, I might be the first person to step into the legendary ‘Body Synthesis Stage’ in the thousands of years after the Universe Ocean was suppressed by the Flood Tide. The so-called ‘Body Synthesis Stage’ is, of course, a very mysterious, dangerous, and unpredictable super level. In order to break into the Body Synthesis Stage sooner, I had to gamble with some unconventional methods. Although I was lucky enough to win the gamble, I was apparently very unstable and messy after I just entered the Body Synthesis Stage and reconstructed thousands of soul fragments. That’s why I was slapped by the ‘Flood Tide’, or ‘Will of Earth’, as you call it.

“But it doesn’t matter. Everyone, listen to me. In fact, everything is under my control. Even this slap was intentionally made by me so that it could slap me. The purpose is to borrow its strength to help me completely condense thousands upon thousands of split souls, allowing me to firmly stand in the Body Integration Stage!”

“It is like sticking countless metal scraps together again. Although it is not visible on the surface, there are countless tiny cracks inside that are invisible to the naked eye. It is very likely that the hardness and stability of the entire component will be affected. At that time, if a certain strong and even external pressure is applied, all the metal scraps might be completely melded into an indestructible whole!

“Believe it or not, aya, it hurts. In short… Hiss… In short, this is all part of my plan. Besides, this guy has been forced to gather more forces to deal with me, which means that its strength in the battlefield of the universe has been seriously weakened. It can’t resist the attacks of all the rebels in the universe at all. It is already running out of strength.

“Therefore, gather your courage and don’t give up hope. Fight side by side with ‘Vulture Li Yao’. Let’s break through this damn prison, this damn tide, and this damn universe!”

Chapter 3261: Giant God Soldier

Although he appeared to be in a sorry state, his deafening roars were still deeply rooted in everyone’s heart. Many prison breakers were encouraged by Li Yao, and they clenched their fists again.

However, when they saw the magnificent silver giant, their belief collapsed again.

Like a devil king that could swallow everything, the giant stood in front of the Ark Island. Thunder and the tides were its slaves, and they were all surging around it. The hundreds of large ships that had been floating on the surface of the ocean—not just the ten-thousand-ton cargo ships and tankers, but also the warships in the army and even an entire carrier group—were all wreathed in invisible force fields and floating in midair.

On the cargo ships and the warships, countless Prison Guardians and crew members were panicking and running on the deck like ants in a hot pan.

However, even when they reached the end of the deck, all they could see was the surface of the ocean, which was hundreds of meters above the ground. Some of them summoned their courage and jumped down, only to be swallowed by the furious waves immediately. Some of them clung to the rails and the ropes, trying to keep themselves alive for one more second.

One second later, almost a hundred giant ships, millions of tons of steel, cannonballs, and fuel, were all smashed into the center of the Ark Island by the silver-white giant.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The arsenals of dozens of warships exploded one after another, igniting the fuel tanks of the other warships. A million tons of steel was burning furiously, as if it were about to turn into scorching magma and turn the entire island into silver statues.

“Shameless man. You have no idea how terrifying the real super bodies are when they gather the wisdom and strength of billions of creatures!

Hong Chao, the will of the earth, sneered in disdain. “Do you think that defeating 1% of me is enough to make you dance with joy? I only spared your life multiple times because I was curious about what tricks you could play before your death, silly.

“This is the reincarnation prison, an absolute domain created by me. I understand all the laws of this small world and can freely manipulate most of the matter and energy here. No one can defeat me here. No one!

“Is that so?”

Below the burning steel and billions of tons of rebar and concrete, Li Yao’s voice came over like a sharp blade. “Then, how many percent of your strength am I worth right now?”

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Squeak squeak squeak!

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

The burning steel was uttering weird noises. The twisted debris was floating in midair, wreathed in flames, as if it had been dismantled by a pair of, no, countless invisible hands into the most fundamental pieces.

Then, the scraps of countless warships, including the carriers, the divine shield warships, and the guided missile destroyers, were put together in an unbelievable way. When two completely different scraps were put together, glamorous flames of the rainbow would spurt out from the joints. When the flames died down, the two scraps melded seamlessly into one.

Very soon, the entire carrier group and dozens of ten-thousand-ton ships were born in the debris of destruction. The magnificent iron beasts were presented to the will of the earth, the tides, as well as all the Hell Breakers and Prison Guardians.

“This—this is—”

The Hell Breakers could not believe their eyes.

They saw an iron demon hundreds of meters tall, with black and red spiritual stripes and electric arcs dancing in colorful flames, standing in front of the silver giant unstoppably.

On the back of the iron god, nine indestructible blades seemed to have been condensed out of the barrels of the warship. They were also like nine lightning rods that attracted all the lightning between the sky and the earth to ‘charge’ themselves. The spiritual stripes on the iron god’s body and the saber on its right hand that was made of the entire destroyer were almost transparent.

“Remove the entire carrier group and refine it into a giant robot hundreds of meters tall?”

The Hell Breakers looked at each other in bewilderment and exclaimed, “Is it really necessary?”

“Hehe. Although the intrusion and analysis just now did not swallow 1% of the will of the earth, it was enough for me to understand your structure and the mysteries of the reincarnation prison.

Li Yao sneered at the core of the steel demon. “Right now, I’ve broken through the legendary ‘Body Synthesis Stage’ and grasped the mysteries of the reincarnation prison. I know that this is not an ordinary three-dimensional space or the original Earth, but a ‘3.5-dimensional Earth’ built in an enormous ‘cosmic bubble’!

“I’ve partly stolen and copied your permissions in the ‘3.5-dimensional Earth’. You can manipulate the materials and energy here freely. Let’s see who has more permissions!

“Ladies and gentlemen, please pay attention to your safety. ‘Super Giant Soldier, King of Jailbreak, Memorial Vulture Li Yao, Magnificent Return, Strengthened Version’ is here!”

“You—”

The entire Ark Island was awed by Li Yao’s ‘super-large divine weapon’. The Hell Breakers were fleeing in panic. The grey mist lady and Sparda could not help but shout, “Do you have to name your weapon?”

Before he had the chance to respond, Li Yao had stomped on the reefs at the edge of the island on his super-large weapon and stepped into the cold seawater again.

Since the super-large magical equipment had just been refined, the plate armor on the outside, which had been suppressed by spiritual energy, was still extremely hot. When the hot metal touched the cold seawater, sizzling noises immediately echoed. The seawater was vaporized into steam, adding to the intimidating aura of the super-large magical equipment.

“Pu!”

The saber cut into the silver-white giant’s skull and cut it in half from head to chest.

However, the silver-white giant, which had split into two halves, did not show any sign of pain or dullness. Instead, it turned its body into a new arm agilely and clutched Li Yao’s saber, while it launched a fierce attack at Li Yao with its redundant hands. Li Yao’s ‘super-large divine weapon’ was blown away with a single punch and almost hit the ark, raising a tsunami more than ten meters high.

“Idiot.”

The silver-white giant waved his four arms, healing his wounds instantly. He said coldly, “Such a clumsy attack is completely useless against me.”

“Is that so?”

Li Yao chuckled.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Intense explosions suddenly broke out inside the silver-white giant’s body. Since the point of explosion was inside the body, there was no room for the damage to be vented at all. The shell of the silver-white giant, which had been as smooth and flawless as a mirror, seemed to be blossoming with countless silver flowers.

As it turned out, Li Yao had sent dozens of cannonballs into the enemy’s body while he was waving his saber.

“Even if one slash is useless, maybe a hundred, a thousand, or ten thousand slashes will be enough to knock you down. No one can predict the outcome of a battle. This is the most brilliant part of a battle. Die, Hong Chao!”

The legs of the super-large weapon exerted strength again. Countless engines and units that were far more powerful than normal engines were roaring earsplittingly. The air currents they spurted out even blew away the seawater nearby, revealing the dry seabed. Li Yao jumped high with the counterforce and slashed his saber at the silver giant for the second time.

There was not the slightest hint of deception in his attack. It was the purest symbol of his will and vitality.

Before the saber hit its target, the aura of the saber had already left a trace dozens of kilometers long on the surface of the ocean behind the silver-white giant.

It was too fast and too powerful. Even the saber condensed by the destroyer could not withstand it. The moment it rushed out, it bent into an even larger curve and turned from a straight saber into a saber. The speed of the saber was even higher than before.

The saber slashed at the will of Earth, but it seemed to be able to split the entire planet.

Even the silver-white giant, who was the embodiment of the will of the earth and the projection of the tide in the Prison of Reincarnation, whose strength had been improved to 3% or even 5%, was no longer as calm as before when faced with Li Yao’s earthshaking attack. It could only dodge backwards and block the attack by extending four sharp blades from the ends of its four silver arms.

For a moment, two giants, one red and the other white, were fighting on the ocean.

The raging tides were their battlefield, the lightning and thunder were their weapons, and the torrential rain reflected their apocalyptic aura. All the Hell Breakers and the Prison Guardians could do was watch with their mouths agape and their heads numb. This was no longer a battle that they could participate in. It was beyond their comprehension and… the domain of gods!

“Isn’t it too much?”

Zhang Daniu and the other awakeners had just escaped from the underground chamber that was about to collapse when they saw such a soul-stirring, magnificent scene. They were so shocked that their eyeballs almost fell out of their sockets. “Are you kidding me? What is this? A devil or an alien? Is this the strength of ‘100% Li Yao’? One person is enough to finish the battle!”

“You are wrong. I am not a god, and the battle is not something that I can resolve alone!

While Li Yao was confronting the silver-white giant head-on, he had been paying close attention to the situation on the Ark Island, especially the situation of Zhang Daniu and the other former ‘soul holders’ of Li Yao. Seeing that they had all escaped safely, he was somewhat relieved and sent a message to Zhang Daniu. “It is difficult to explain the specific scientific theories to you in a short period of time. In short, when I was invading and analyzing the mysteries of the Reincarnation Prison, I discovered a few very interesting rules. Maybe, the so-called Reincarnation Prison was not decided by me, nor was it decided by this guy, but by the six billion martyred spirits built by you and decided by you!

“Yes, the future of the Reincarnation Prison—Earth—universe is in your hands. The stronger your desire for freedom is, the weaker the barrier to seal the Reincarnation Prison will be. The more you believe in victory and hope, the weaker the guy will be. The more you believe that we can defeat it, the more confident we will be!”

Chapter 3262: True Desperation!

“Does the future… depend on us?”

Even Zhang Daniu was slightly stunned when he heard Li Yao’s roar. An unprecedented solemn expression appeared on his face.

As for the other Awakeners, both the Hell Breakers and the Prison Guardians felt that a gate deep inside their brain had been opened and was emitting infinite gold brightness.

Not just the Ark Island, even the people who were fighting against the apocalypse everywhere on Earth seemed to have heard a call from the unseen world. The golden horn was louder and louder, telling them—not to give up hope!

At this moment, countless solid shackles were torn apart, and countless ancient seals were broken. Countless heroic spirits recalled the glory and dreams before countless reincarnations, their dignity and pride, the monstrous mistakes they had made, and all the efforts they had made to make up for their mistakes!

Every human being, every earthling, and every heroic spirit who once dominated the sea of stars and roared in the universe had brainwaves that soared into the sky like burning flames and broke through the feeble walls of the reincarnation prison!

On the battleground of the Ark Island, the shell of the silver-white giant had dimmed significantly. There were even ugly black spots that looked like silver that had been oxidized.

It was the weakness brought by the awakening of the heroic spirits.

Li Yao, on the other hand, had received the blessings of countless heroic spirits. The more he fought, the braver he became, and the more frustrated he became. The traces of man-made carving that could be seen on the super-large weapon were gradually melted by the flames of war. In the end, the weapon was integrated into one, as if it were a living creature made of millions of tons of steel.

BOOM!

He stomped on the ocean, raising furious tides that were then vaporized by the flames of war, forming hundreds of raging dragons of steam that attacked the silver giant simultaneously.

Pa!

The reefs on the Ark Island were all driven by Li Yao’s remote manipulation ability and flew into the sky, only to be crushed into the tiniest dust. The dust rubbed against each other at a high speed and turned into a sword of fire hundreds of meters long, stabbing at the silver giant’s chest.

CRACK!

Manipulated by Li Yao’s soul, thousands of thunderbolts lunged at the silver giant, trying to cut it into pieces. The surface of the silver giant was riddled with shocking dents like a pond in a storm.

“Tell me, what is your ultimate purpose?”

While launching a storm-like attack, Li Yao shouted at the top of his lungs, “An unparalleled existence such as yourself, who was born by condensing the souls of billions of creatures, is definitely not going to fulfil your lifelong destiny with such a low-level goal as ‘dominance of the universe’. Tell me, why are you doing this? Why are you imprisoning billions of heroes in the Reincarnation Prison where they will not be free for billions of years?”

“You wouldn’t understand even if I told you.”

The silver-white giant took thousands of blows in a few seconds. But after each blow, the wounds deep inside his bones would be healed instantly. A black gap that looked like a mouth suddenly appeared on his face, while he sneered, “A one-sided creature such as yourself will never understand what I’m going to do!

“You didn’t say anything. How do you know that I don’t understand?”

Li Yao punched the silver giant’s head one punch after another. “People who love peace and are against violence like me hate fighting and killing the most. Why can’t we sit down and talk about everything clearly? Whether you are the ‘Will of Earth’ or the ‘Flood’, you were made of billions of innocent souls abandoned on the ‘Original Earth’ in the beginning. Am I right? We are of the same origin. We should be helping each other in the dark, boundless central ocean of the multiverse. Why are we fighting each other and even coming up with such a long and torturous method? What are you going to get from the six billion heroic spirits?”

The silver-white giant coldly snorted. It didn’t reply, but its speed suddenly doubled. It was clearly an indomitable giant, but its body was even more agile than the armadillo and the butterfly. It dodged all of Li Yao’s attacks and instead circled around to Li Yao’s back. Its four arms turned into four sharp cones, ruthlessly stabbing towards the “super-large divine weapon”.

Even though Li Yao had stepped into the ‘Body Synthesis Stage’ and received the blessing of countless heroic spirits, he was still caught off guard by the sudden change of rhythm of the silver-white giant’s attacks. However, he refused to relax his tongue.

“I know that a thermonuclear war took place on the ‘Original Earth’. On the wasteland where the nuclear dust was floating, a small group of nobles and rich men ruled over most of the civilians. In the end, when the ‘Original Earth’ was doomed, the nobles and rich men even deceived the civilians and escaped on their own starships, leaving billions of civilians on the planet to die. As a result, billions of people were burnt to death by the flames when the meteoroids hit the sun, but the souls of their hatred somehow survived and condensed into a new life form—your predecessor!

Li Yao shouted, “I know that you are a super life form that exists for revenge, including the war between the ‘super body civilization’ and the ‘Primeval civilization’. It was a war of revenge between the souls of billions of Earthlings and a handful of cosmic nobles!

“Of course you have a reason for revenge. If I were one of the billions of abandoned civilians, I would do the same!

“But—but you’ve already won the war. Billions of years have passed. The nobles and magnates who fled from Earth have been obliterated. The survivors of the battle, including the six billion heroic spirits that you locked up in the Prison of Reincarnation, most of them had nothing to do with the nobles and the rich at all. Even if you were wronged, shouldn’t you be cleared by now? Why are you so determined to destroy everyone, even the entire civilization of mankind?”

“Therefore, you are a noisy cockroach. You don’t know anything. You don’t know anything, and yet you are talking nonsense here.

The four arms of the silver-white giant turned into four silver storms in the blink of an eye and resolved Li Yao’s new wave of attacks made of reefs, lightning, and tides easily. “You were right. When billions of innocent souls were condensed into immaculate super bodies for the first time, there was only one thing on our mind, and that was revenge. However, the war of revenge was already over the moment the ‘super body civilization’ defeated the ‘Primeval civilization’. Even though the Primeval Civilization was not completely wiped out, there were still a few fish that escaped the net, and they were not within the range of my revenge. I did not establish the ‘reincarnation prison’ for something as boring as ‘revenge’.

“Then, what was the purpose of everything?

Li Yao thought quickly and suddenly realized something. “Do you really want to achieve the purpose of ‘universal peace’ by connecting everyone’s soul and sharing their emotions and memories so that there will be no war, violence, pain, and hatred in the universe?”

“No, you must not do that. Love and hate are two sides of the same coin. Selfishness brings violence and hatred, but it is also the source of motivation for the evolution of life. Our curiosity, desire to explore, imagination, and creativity are all born because of our independent souls. We are all kings of our own souls. Please don’t turn us into slaves for everybody!

“It is like, as the thermodynamic theorem states, heat can only be transmitted between objects of different temperatures, and it is precisely such heat transmission that leads to the birth of a brilliant universe and civilization. In accordance with your methods, everyone will open their soul, and everyone will meld into the super body. Their emotions and will will will be evenly distributed. It is as if all objects are at the same temperature. There will be no more heat transmission, no more molecular changes, and the entire world will become dead silent. Whether it is the civilization or the universe, both will fall into the miserable hell of absolute zero. Do you understand?”

“Of course I do. You just don’t understand.”

The silver-white giant said coldly, “I don’t care whether the multiverse sea is a war or a peace. I don’t care whether I win or the rebels defeat the strong enemy, and I don’t care about the outcome of the war that is being fought right now.

“No. I did not exist for the ridiculous mission of ‘keeping the Universe Ocean in absolute peace’. It is just a wrong assumption that a ridiculous cockroach such as yourself made about me with your shallow wisdom. It is utterly, utterly meaningless.

“What—what?”

Li Yao was dazed. He subconsciously thought that the silver-white giant was lying, but his intuition told him that the silver-white giant truly did not care whether or not he was defeated, the outcome of the battlefield in the universe, or whether or not the reincarnation prison that had been built for billions of years was going to be broken.

Otherwise, judging from his performance just now, the one named ‘Will of Earth’ or ‘Flood Tide’ was too weak.

“What—what are you trying to do?”

Li Yao did not know whether it was out of fear or out of fear. He did not even dare to ask any more questions. Instead, he waved his saber made of dust, lightning, waves, hydrogen atoms, and oxygen atoms and stabbed at the enemy’s chest.

This time, the silver-white giant did not dodge. It simply allowed Li Yao’s fury to leave a shocking wound on its chest. The wound was even splitting apart, as if a black, bloody mouth had opened in the center of its chest. Behind the wound was not the surface of the ocean but a glittering mist.

“Since you are so curious, I will grant your wish!”

The silver-white giant opened its bloody mouth. Its entire body split into two halves and swallowed Li Yao together with his super-large divine weapon like a giant armadillo. “I will let you see through the illusion of victory and defeat and face the deepest desperation!”

Chapter 3263: Parallel Universe

Li Yao did not expect that the silver-white giant’s speed would be increased by ten times in the blink of an eye, nor did he expect that it would tear its wounds into an overwhelming bloody mouth. It was too late to run away. The entire world was swallowed by the silver tide. He fell into the bottomless crevice inside the silver-white giant’s body together with the ‘super-large divine weapon’.

Li Yao felt that he was falling into an endless abyss. He could not find any place to exert his strength, nor could he sense his body or the super-large weapon.

The chaotic darkness around him gradually turned into spots of brightness. Then, stars that looked like sand poured down like a waterfall, as if he had passed through the silver giant and returned to the boundless universe.

However, this was not an ordinary universe.

There were too many stars in this place.

It must be noted that, in the ‘real’ universe, the distance between stars was extremely far. No matter how furious the light and heat were, they would become extremely dim after thousands of lightyears. Therefore, sailing in the sea of stars was like an ant passing through a sealed, dark box. At most, a few tiny holes would appear on the surface of the box, through which a few feeble rays of light would leak in.

However, the stars here were too dense, too bright, and even too dazzling, as if all the stars in the universe had been attracted to Li Yao’s head. Or rather… billions of universes overlapped.

“This is—”

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. He immediately realized what was going on. “Inside the flood!”

He had been planning to go deep into the enemy’s territory and give the flood tide a ‘bloom in the center’. Right now, the flood tide had really opened all the barriers openly and allowed him to march forward. However, the inside of the flood tide was beyond his expectation. The information and data behind the stars were too profound for him to comprehend.

Li Yao could only let the tides push him forward where the stars were densest, as if billions of suns had exploded at the same time.

For a moment, endless energy flooded into his soul, making him feel that he could not resist at all and that his soul was about to be obliterated.

However, after he gradually adapted to the indescribable brightness, he finally ‘saw’, or rather, ‘feel’, that countless blue planets were floating above the brilliant ocean of flames. Every blue planet had their own small, delicate galaxies as their ‘protection covers’. There was also vague gold brightness around every protective cover. There was no telling whether it was a protection or a seal.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

When Li Yao extended his consciousness, the information of the blue planets flooded into the depths of his soul like a tide. He could see the terrain of every blue planet clearly. The mountains, the oceans, the scattered towns, and the faces of the creatures living in the towns who called themselves ‘the spirits of all living creatures’.

On countless Earths, the emotions, will, and imagination of countless human beings, which were the foundation of intelligent life, were all transformed into a pure and powerful force that was absorbed and sent into the brilliant ocean of flames.

Li Yao found the scene extremely familiar, as if he had seen it somewhere before.

Right. ‘Fuxi’, the super artificial intelligence behind the scenes of the Holy Alliance!

In the past, the Fuxi Clan had established countless ‘laboratories of human nature’ in the virtual world, which were designed to collect big data and support their ideal country.

The actions of the tides were quite similar. No, they were more ‘advanced’, because most of the laboratories of human nature of the Fuxis were virtual. The tides, on the other hand, were exploiting the real planets, or rather, the earth in the three-dimensional universe, to harvest what they needed!

No… The more Li Yao observed, the more terrified he became. Everything presented in the depths of the tide was beyond his comprehension. It was like the amalgamation of countless ‘parallel worlds’, countless ‘parallel Earths’, dancing in the boundless, brilliant ocean of flames.

“Is this the truth of the Reincarnation Prison?

“Every time the time loop ends and is about to be restarted, the old ‘Earth’ is not completely destroyed. Instead, it is completely digitized and informationized, fed into the depths of the tide, and transformed into a ‘parallel Earth’.

“It’s like when a computer system runs for a period of time and produces a lot of data changes and computation results, all the data will be copied, saved, transferred to another storage space, and restarted.

“Therefore, as many ‘parallel Earths’ as there were reincarnations in the past, the flood tide not only controlled the emotions and imagination of all the heroes on the ‘present’ planet but also the strength of countless heroes on the ‘parallel Earths’!

The thoughts burst out in the depths of Li Yao’s consciousness like a series of sparks.

Although the fog of darkness had been cleared, he was more confused than ever. If that was the case, the Flood Tide was much stronger than they had imagined. They were definitely no match for it, at least not with his petty tricks.

But the flood tide did not attack them with the strength of countless parallel Earths.

What was this unbelievably terrifying Quasi God doing with such tremendous strength?

“Now, do you believe me?”

A calm voice came from behind Li Yao. “This is not an illusion. It is a real ‘parallel planet’. With such tremendous strength, I don’t care about the harassment of a bug like you at all. Your noise and your biting mean nothing to me.

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. He turned around and saw the silver ball that looked like a balloon floating behind his head.

The silver ball had no facial features. The round mirror reflected Li Yao’s own hilarious expression coldly.

“Even so, your wisdom and courage, or rather, your meanness and stupidity, still won me over… a little bit.”

The silver ball continued sending messages to Li Yao. “Because of the ‘touching’ and the ocean-like energy that you provided for me, I can let you die knowing, no, not ‘death’, but a new life in the distant past.

Li Yao did not know how to react. He had been lost in countless ‘parallel Earths’ and the overlapping, interfering, splitting, and rebirth stars.

The information link between him and the silver ball immediately expanded.

The enormous data that looked like billions of stars flooded toward Li Yao’s soul and painted an unbelievable, even unimaginable, picture of the universe for Li Yao in the most concise way.

Everything started with quantum mechanics.

Quantum mechanics was a branch of physics that studied the movement of the microscopic particles in the world of matter. It mainly studied the fundamental theories of the structure and nature of atoms, molecules, and condensed matter as well as the nucleus and the fundamental particles. It surpassed the category of classical physics and fundamentally changed the understanding of human beings about the structure of matter and its interactions.

Different from the classical mechanics that could be tested intuitively, there were many things in the field of quantum mechanics that defied common sense or even imagination. For example, the understanding of quantum mechanics for ordinary people almost all began with the famous thought experiment named ‘Schrodinger’s cat’.

Li Yao now knew that ‘Schrodinger’s cat’ was not the work of the flood but a real thought experiment on the ‘origin earth’ billions of years ago that was proposed by Schrodinger, a famous physicist in Austria at the time, in order to explain the mechanism of quantum superposition on a macroscopic scale.

The thought experiment went like this: Suppose there was a live cat and a small amount of radioactive material in an ideal box. Then, there was a 50% probability that the radioactive material would decay and release poisonous gas to kill the cat, and a 50% probability that the radioactive material would not decay and the cat would survive.

According to classical physics, one of the two outcomes was bound to occur inside the box, and only by opening the box would the observers outside know the outcome inside.

In the quantum world, when the box was closed, the entire system would remain in an uncertain wave state, which was the superposition of life and death.

Whether a cat was dead or alive could not be determined until it was in the form of particles when the box was opened and observed by outside observers.

The famous thought experiment was designed to prove the extraordinary understanding and understanding of quantum mechanics about the micro-particle world, which turned micro-uncertainty into macro-uncertainty. A cat that was both alive and dead defied logic.

Schrödinger’s cat was a hypothetical concept. Even Schrödinger himself had thrown out the experiment with a hint of mockery. But with the development of technology, human beings had achieved the ‘Schrödinger cat state’ in photons, atoms, and molecules. They had even discovered that the Schrödinger cat state, which was the quantum superposition state, was a universal state in life.

This greatly shook the previously stable, only, and firm world of mankind.

It even led to the concept of ‘parallel universes’.

The reason was simple. All the micro-particles were ‘unpredictable’ because they were in the superimposed state of non-living, non-living, non-left, non-right, and non-imaginative. All the matter in the macroscopic universe, including the entire universe, was made up of countless micro-particles. Therefore, theoretically speaking, the macro-unpredictable universe should be in the superimposed state of ‘everything is possible’. If we cannot perceive the ‘unpredictable’ and ‘unpredictable’, it can only be because there are countless parallel universes that are roughly similar but have subtle differences. We only live in one of them. We don’t know the real face of the Lushan Mountain because we are in the mountain’. That’s all.

Chapter 3264: Returning To Youth

As to how parallel universes were born, the scientists had put forward a lot of hypotheses and conjectures. The most popular one was the ‘Observer Hypotheses’, which suggested that the observation of the observers caused the collapse of the quantum state and the birth of parallel universes.

The simplest example was the thought experiment named ‘Schrödinger’s Cat’. According to the ‘Observer’s Hypotheses’, before the box was opened, the universe was in the quantum state where two probabilities overlapped. When the box was opened and the ‘Observer’ saw the result inside the box, the universe would split into two. The two universes were almost identical, except that the cat was alive in one universe and dead in the other.

It was hard to accept the theory that the life and death of a cat could create a whole new universe. However, if it were a more important historical decision, it would be much easier to understand. The civilization of mankind had walked on the fork of history countless times. If the monkeys in the beginning had not looked up at the starry sky, if the tribe that had first learned to roast food with flames had been destroyed in a flash flood, if Emperor Qin Shihuang had been assassinated by assassins during the unification of the six kingdoms, if Newton had been killed by a terrible and common infectious disease before the famous apple fell, if Nazi Germany and not the United States had developed nuclear weapons in advance… If—if—the civilization of mankind in the current universe had only chosen one of the countless forks, the other countless forks could still lead to countless colorful and completely different universes.

Or rather, the ‘theoretical explorers’ of part of the parallel universes believed that any decision that everybody made every second of every day would trigger a tsunami of the quantum level that would divide the entire universe and give birth to a new parallel universe.

In other words, even if an ordinary person went out in the morning with their left foot or right foot, it was possible that a series of chain reactions would occur, leading to the birth of a new parallel universe.

Such an idealistic argument placed human beings at the center of the infinite universe. Naturally, it was hard to accept.

However, from a different perspective, ‘the universe exists because of me’ seemed to be an undeniable truth.

It was simple. When people talked about the concept of ‘universe’, they often thought of the vast space, the shining stars, the brilliant sun and the gentle moon, and perhaps the various magnetic fields, waves, and forces, as well as black holes, neutron stars, nebulas, and all the cosmic dust.

However, the reason why people believed that the universe was a ‘glittering sea of stars’ was because human beings were ‘observers’ who had ‘eyes’, could observe light, receive radiation, and were adept at all kinds of calculations. They could infer the orbit of a planet thousands of kilometers away with the most fundamental mathematical formulas.

If there were no human beings but a different kind of intelligent creatures who did not have light-sensing organs and could not even perceive the waves and the heat radiation as observers, their universe would certainly not be the ‘glittering sea of stars’, but something else that human beings could not understand at all and might be even more chaotic and splendid.

On second thought, was it possible that the universe was billions of times richer, brighter, more splendid, and more splendid than what human beings had observed? There were all kinds of waves and force fields and celestial bodies, too. But human beings lacked the ‘organs’ to observe and calculate them. Therefore, they had been dwelling in the colorful ocean of fertility without knowing anything.

It was like a primitive tribe that had lived in the deepest part of an underground cave their entire life and had never seen light before. They did not know or need to know the meaning of color. To them, the world was a completely different form.

Finally, what if there were no human beings?

There were no human beings, nor were there any intelligent creatures that could serve as ‘observers’. Did the universe still exist? Even if it did, what was the significance of a universe that had never been observed and might remain in the superposition forever?

Therefore, in a ‘meaningful universe’, the ‘Observer’ must be the center of the universe. The universe would be exactly what the eyes and other sensory organs of the Observer looked like. It could turn into whatever the Observer wanted it to be. When the Observer was full of hope, the universe would be infinitely bright. When the Observer fell into desperation, the universe would sink forever.

Such information flooded into Li Yao’s soul like a raging ocean.

What do I do?

Li Yao’s face was gloomy as if he were faced with a great enemy. “I don’t quite understand what Hong Chao is talking about. It seems a bit awkward. What should I do?”

Hardly had the thought occurred to him when he felt that the information flooding into his brain became clearer and concise.

The specific theories and complicated conjectures, hypotheses, theorems, and formulas were not important. After all, Li Yao wouldn’t understand them even if they were explained to him. In short, Li Yao only needed to understand that the ‘Observer’s Hypotheses’ were partly valid under certain conditions. Parallel universes did exist, and their birth was related to whether or not Schrödinger’s cat was alive.

Of course, this was not to say that if such a cat existed, its life and death would really create a new parallel universe.

It was even more impossible for an ordinary person to go to work in the morning with sleepy eyes, yawning, and stepping out of the door first to create a new parallel universe.

No. It was not that simple. The key lay in the conservation of mass and energy and the conservation of heat. Creating a new parallel universe required a huge amount of energy, and the simple, low-level observational behaviors such as ‘cat’s life and death’ and ‘first step left foot right foot’ were not enough to trigger enough energy to ‘push’ the birth of a new parallel universe.

Only when enough observers triggered the strongest energy stored inside their bodies and performed high-level observational actions could a new parallel universe be born.

In the universe where the flood tide was located, the observers were all human beings.

On the other hand, the highest level of energy contained in the human body might even be able to break the ‘conservation of mass and energy’ or even the ultimate force to break the barriers of dimensions and the universe, which were emotions, will, and imagination.

Parallel universes might be endless, but the emotions, will, and imagination of all the observers in the infinite universe were the same.

Therefore, as long as the observers were placed in an extreme environment and triggered their strongest emotions and strongest imagination, it was possible to create a new parallel universe!

What was a “very extreme environment”?

The answer was obvious. It was when the world was coming to an end and the earth was going to be destroyed!

So that was the case!

Li Yao finally understood what Hong Chao was doing.

At first, it created countless Earths by manipulating materials and energy with the power of more than half of the universal ocean that could almost be called ‘Quasi-God’. Billions of heroic spirits were trapped inside.

Then, the planets would be destroyed one by one. The intense stimulation of the apocalypse would trigger the strongest emotions, will, and imagination that were hidden inside every heroic spirit.

As long as the forces were strong enough, they could trigger the ‘Observer’s Effect’, which would divide the current universe and give birth to a new ‘parallel universe’.

However, creating new parallel universes was still not the ultimate purpose of the tides. On the contrary, when the new parallel universes were created and were about to explode and expand, the tides confined and sealed the parallel universes in a way that Li Yao could not understand!

The division of atoms would generate tremendous energy, and the division of the universe was even more so.

Gathering and sealing the forces of atom division and outputting them steadily and continuously was the mechanism of some nuclear power plants. The flood tide, on the other hand, triggered the ‘fission chain reaction’ on purpose and turned countless newly-born parallel universes into its ‘energy source’!

The flood tide was not lying after all.

What it was really trying to make use of was not the emotions, will, and imagination of human beings. Such an ‘energy source’ might be powerful, but it was nothing compared to the ‘energy source for cosmic fission reactions’.

The power of human beings as observers was merely the fulcrum of the cosmic splitting energy.

The flood tide that controlled infinite quantum energy was indeed not something that Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, or the remaining forces of the Primeval Civilization could deal with.

However, Li Yao discovered that the flood did not intend to destroy them with quantum energy at all. Instead, most of the quantum energy, as well as the forces of countless parallel universes, had been dedicated to something more mysterious and inexplicable than a black hole.

In the words of the Cultivators, it seemed to be a piece of magical equipment at the level of the universe. No. It was the core of the magical equipment!

“What are you doing exactly?”

Seeing the ultimate picture of the universe, Li Yao felt that his blood was freezing. He mumbled, “You created countless parallel universes and sealed them at the moment of their birth. In such a way, you acquired countless ‘energy sources’, countless ‘cosmic batteries’, and countless ‘world engines’. You—you control such enormous energy. What are you going to drive them to achieve your purpose?”

“I’m already old.

The tide spoke behind Li Yao in an ancient voice. “As you said, time has left too many scars and wounds on my body. Even a real god-level civilization could not resist the erosion of time. If you consider me to be a human being, I will be a grey-haired, dying old man.

“For an old man who is about to die, even if he has all the wealth and power in the world, it is meaningless, isn’t it? If there is an opportunity where he can exchange all his wealth and power for a wish, what kind of wish do you think he will make?

“That’s right. There won’t be a second answer even if there are a hundred, a thousand, or ten thousand old people. What I want is none other than to turn back time and regain my youth!

If there were no accidents, the main story would be finished today. It would be so easy… to miss everyone. Boohoo!

Chapter 3265: Immortality In The Circulation Of Time!

“Turn back time and become young again?”

Li Yao chewed the words over and over. Then he thought of the trick that Hong Chao had played with the sound of glass. He was greatly alarmed and immediately realized what was going on. “You—you are going to perform a super large-scale ‘reboot’ across the universe and reset the entire timeline to a time billions of years ago when nothing happened?”

“Yes.”

The flood’s voice was deep, but the silver surface of the tide was surging with the souls of the deceased who had been stranded on Earth billions of years ago, the war between the ‘superhuman civilization’ and the ‘Primeval civilization’, and the entire process of the flood’s control over everything from the three-dimensional universe to the three-point-five-dimensional universe and even the march toward the four-dimensional universe. “You were right. In the beginning, I was indeed made of billions of ordinary people who had been abandoned on Earth. When I, or rather, ‘we’, were reborn in the solar storm, the waves that we released had no room for anything except the roars of fury and revenge.

“After we defeated the ‘Primeval Civilization’ as a ‘superhuman civilization’, the fury of revenge had subsided. We were indeed thinking about how to connect everyone’s thoughts and emotions and ensure the absolute peace of the entire Universe Ocean in such a way.

“However, the more we explore, the more we learn about the universe and the universe above it, and the more we are enveloped in darkness, desperation, and regret. We can destroy our enemies, but we cannot revive the dead. We can create an incomparably beautiful world, but we cannot repair yesterday’s evilness and ugliness. We can control matter and energy at our will, but we cannot return to the past, to the time when the ‘origin earth’ had not been destroyed, when everything was so beautiful and vibrant.

“The river of time is always flowing toward the deep sea named ‘Obliteration’. We are like old men who have endless wealth and power. We are tired of everything, but we can only watch the years cut us into pieces and leave indelible marks on our faces, bodies, and souls.

“So what if we control the universe? So what if we dominate the sea of stars? Age is our inevitable destiny. Destruction is our only destination.

“You said that, if there was a way to reverse the flow of time in the entire sea of the multiverse and return every drop of water in the flood to the past when everything had yet to begin, wouldn’t such a thing be worthwhile for us to pursue at the cost of all our energy?

Li Yao did not know what to say.

He had never felt so terrified or so angry.

“No matter how much you yearn for your lost childhood, if billions of years are reset, what do you think we, who were born in billions of years, are?

Li Yao gritted his teeth. He clenched his fists so hard that they looked like two black holes. “Are we all plain words that can be erased by an eraser at any time?

“No. Time cannot be reversed. We can only march forward!

“Advancing forward? Hehe. Hehehehe. You don’t understand at all. There’s no way forward. If you don’t want your head to bleed and your soul to scatter, you can only turn back!”

The silver ball’s expression flooded Li Yao with endless information again. This time, the content of the information was even more terrifying than the deepest level of hell.

“Do you think that the Universe Ocean is the cruelest battlefield? Do you think that there are warm and friendly god-level creatures in the real four-dimensional space? Do you think that the so-called ‘god-level creatures’ are really merciful gods who will be moved by your prayers, your hot blood, and your so-called justice? How hilarious!

Hong Chao roared in a low voice, “You dust-like human beings know nothing about the real danger. Do you know what the ‘Great Purging Agreement’ is? Do you know what the ‘Fast Child Collapse’ is? Do you know how many universes and dimensions the rage of ‘Lord Singularity’ can affect? Have you ever seen the ‘Ancient Devourer’ awaken from the ‘Black Hole Abyss’? Even ‘Lord Singularity’ has to shiver in fear. Countless civilizations have sacrificed their futures just to seal the ‘Ancient Devourer’ for one more second?

“No. You don’t know anything at all. You are just a tiny cockroach. Bacteria and viruses are even smaller than cockroaches. You have no idea how terrifying the real hunters and prey are in the universe!

“Dark forest. Hehe. Hehehehehe. You guys love to describe the vastness and horror of the universe with the words ‘dark forest’. But I have to tell you that the most terrifying thing is not that you are living in a dark forest, but that the so-called ‘dark forest’ is actually a ‘biological reserve’ in the eyes of a higher existence, or even a weed in a zoo of a higher existence. You’ve finally ignited the ‘dark forest’ and escaped from it, only to find countless pairs of mocking eyes staring at you!

“You are on the ground, and he is in the sky. You are a bacterium, but he can pass through nineteen different dimensions. How are you going to fight him?

“As I said, I admire your courage and stupidity, but courage and stupidity are just stupidity. I used to be as ignorant as you, thinking that I had grasped the power to dominate the multidimensional space. But as soon as I stuck my head out, I was beaten back to my original self and trapped in this broken body in the awkward state of ‘3.5D’. If I hadn’t taken the risk to march forward and suffered heavy wounds, do you think that floating dust, cockroaches, bacteria, and viruses like you would have any chance at all?

“Do you know what the saddest thing is? The saddest thing is that, a long time later, when I inferred what had happened with all my computational ability, I discovered that the enemy was not aiming at me at all. I was merely hit by a stray bullet in a battlefield of a higher dimension. Or rather, a marmot that crawled out of a cave only to be stomped back by a soldier’s boot. That’s all! From the beginning to the end, the existences of a higher dimension did not even look at me!

“This is the gap between us. It is even larger than the gap between you and me.

“Since then, I’ve come to understand one thing. There is an end to evolution. All civilizations must behave themselves and hide in the food chain. In their place, any civilization that tries to ‘show up’ will be ruthlessly attacked, and the attackers won’t even realize that the civilization exists.

“On behalf of the civilization of mankind, I have reached the end of evolution. If I go any further, I will have to break into the four-dimensional world again. I will certainly suffer another fatal strike. Maybe it will no longer be a stray bullet but a real destruction!

“Tell me, other than ‘reversing time and returning to the past’, is there any better way to protect the hard-earned civilization?”

Li Yao was rendered speechless, not knowing what to do.

In fact, his soul had been stuffed by the terrifying information sent by the flood tide, which included the ‘Great Purge Agreement’, ‘Fast Collapse’, ‘Lord Singularity’, and ‘Aeon Devourer’. He was simply caught in a state of ‘data overflow’.

“Going back to the past…”

Li Yao mumbled, “Can everything be solved?”

“You can.”

Hong Chao said, “As long as we go back to the past, all the mistakes can be rectified, all the regrets can be erased, and all the evilness and ugliness can be eliminated before they happen. The next reincarnation will certainly be better than this one. Everything can be changed.

“What about us?”

Li Yao narrowed his eyes, his soul aching. “Are we going to disappear forever?”

“Not necessarily. Remember what I said just now? The universe is determined by the observers.”

“As long as your ‘obsession’ is strong enough, you will be able to interfere with part of the quantum when you turn back time. You and your loved ones will be reborn in the beautiful past in a happier way!

“But…”

Li Yao hesitated. “Even if we go back in time, we will still evolve. Ten thousand years, a million years, a hundred million years. Eventually, we will touch the barrier of… evolution that you talked about. If we break through the four-dimensional space, we will still be destroyed by the ‘Great Purge Agreement’, ‘Fast Seed Collapse’, ‘Lord Singularity’, and ‘Ancient Devourer’.

“Then let’s rewind the clock and reverse the flow of time again.”

Hong Chao said, “Reset to the beginning of everything that hasn’t happened yet.

“Well—”

Li Yao frowned and struggled to resist. “Isn’t he digging a hole to bury himself and running away without fighting?”

“No. This is the position of our civilization in the food chain of the universe!

Hong Chao said, “If we are destined to be grass, there is no need for us to grow so high. If we are herbivores, we should try our best not to make any noise and live our life quietly. This is the way we exist. As long as we do what we are told, no matter how chaotic the universe outside is, no matter how many civilizations are destroyed, we will live forever in the cycle of time!

“Eternal… Life…”

Li Yao mumbled to himself. His entire body, including his throat, was enveloped and swallowed by the silver liquid.

“Yes, immortality. I’ve spent billions of years collecting the ‘parallel cosmic chain reaction energy’. Right now, I’ve finally reached the threshold of ‘time reversal’. I only need the last step, the last bit of energy, to complete the first ‘time reversal’. When the dust has settled, you will know that I was right and that I made a choice to protect the civilization!

Hong Chao sounded as if he was crying for blood.

Chapter 3266: Family And Friends

On the Ark Island.

Ever since Li Yao’s ‘super-large divine weapon’ was swallowed by the silver-white giant, the hostile parties had fallen into a weird silence. Faced with the ‘battle between gods and devils’ that had just been aborted, both the Hell Breakers and the Prison Guardians felt that their battle was ridiculous and meaningless.

However, the silence would not last forever. Or rather, it was changing at a visible speed.

When the ‘super-large magical equipment’ had just been swallowed by the silver-white giant, the silver-white giant’s appearance had been quite eye-catching because of their similar size, as if it had been stuffed by the ‘super-large magical equipment’.

However, as time went by, the silver giant gradually turned round and smooth, like an absolute ball, as if… it had completely digested the ‘super-large divine weapon’ together with Li Yao!

With the melting of the ‘super-large magical equipment’ and Li Yao, the dark clouds in the sky gathered again, and the storm became even more furious. The lightning unleashed its most hideous strength toward the deep sea, the earth, and all living creatures unscrupulously like a transformed beast.

“Something doesn’t seem right!”

Zhang Daniu hugged a protruding reef tightly to prevent himself from being swept away by the storm. The raindrops almost made him unable to open his eyes. “Li Yao’s aura is getting weaker and weaker. I simply can’t feel his existence. What do I do?”

“Hey, everyone. We can’t just sit here and watch Li Yao save the world by himself. He was right just now. No one can save everything by themselves. The world is exactly what we are. We have to save everything by ourselves!

“If—if this is really a world where ‘strong imagination can reverse reality’ and ’emotion and determination are limitless’, then even if we can’t do anything else, we can at least cheer for him and cheer for him, right?

“Everyone, let’s do it together. Let’s see whether or not we, the masses, will do our best—Li Yao will win! Long live mankind! Li Yao will win! Long live mankind!”

The grey mist lady, Sparda, and the other awakened Cultivators looked at each other in bewilderment, not knowing whether or not they should go crazy together with Zhang Daniu.

“Will it work?”

The grey mist lady could not help but ask.

“Something seems to be missing. It doesn’t look intimidating enough.

Zhang Daniu pondered for a moment. “In such an impassioned and magnificent battlefield, it seems that we should shout some more domineering lines. The ‘Will of the Earth’ that we are faced with, which is also known as the ‘Ways of Heaven’ in many webnovels, more or less has the same meaning, right? Then it will be easy to handle. I know these lines like the back of my hand. I will give you a demonstration first so that you can roar along with me and release all your hot blood, passion, and determination!”

“Listen, it’s like this—my life is up to me and not the heavens. I, Zhang Daniu, am going to defy the heavens today!”

“Kill the gods! Kill the devils! Kill them all!”

“God, I’m going to kill you today!”

“Roar! Why are you not roaring with me? Why are you looking at me in such a daze?”

“Uh…”

The grey mist lady looked at Sparda and said, “We are still brewing.”

“It can’t be. What’s there to be worried about? Isn’t it simple?”

Zhang Daniu rolled up his sleeves and gritted his teeth. He pointed his middle finger at the dark sky and declared, “I want the sky—”

No one expected Zhang Daniu’s cheer to really work.

Therefore, when an earsplitting explosion that was thousands of times louder than thunder really came from beyond the clouds, everybody was dumbfounded, including Zhang Daniu himself.

His entire body was cramping. His middle finger stabbed at the sky, shivering, as if a hole thousands of kilometers in diameter had been pierced through the dark clouds, revealing the battlefield of the universe in the brilliant stars beyond the clouds and the atmosphere.

No. Not one hole, but thousands upon thousands of holes appeared on the high wall of the sealed prison of reincarnation. Billions of dazzling stars pierced through the high wall, the atmosphere, and the dark clouds like billions of shining blades, spraying thousands upon thousands of rays of golden light on the ground.

Everyone on the Ark Island, no, everyone on Earth, was staring at the sky in bewilderment.

The sky seemed to have turned into a glamorous, translucent stage. The shadows of countless super-large starships that were even larger than the moon were overlapping in the middle of the stage. The billions of thunderbolts unleashed by the turrets eclipsed the lightning and the volcanic eruptions. The most eye-catching spots under the spotlight belonged to the iron deities who were hundreds of meters tall, thousands of meters tall, and even tens of thousands of meters tall. The debris of countless starships surrounded every iron deity and rained upon the earth like spectacular meteor showers!

“This is—”

Zhang Daniu mumbled to himself, “Holy sh*t. This is the main force of the battlefield of the universe!”

…

Deep inside the tide.

Li Yao was about to be lost.

Lost in regret for the past and fear for the future.

“There’s no way forward!”

“All the beautiful things can start again. We will live forever in the infinite reincarnation!

“The Great Purge Agreement. The collapse of the fast child. Lord of Singularity, the eternal devourer—you have no idea how terrifying such forces are!

The ripples invaded his soul bit by bit like demonic music.

He was in a trance and did not know what was going on. He did not even notice that the silver ball was slowly floating behind him and swallowing him.

At first, it was his legs, then his waist, then his hands, his chest, his neck, and finally his mouth, nose, eyes, and ears. He gradually melted into the silver ball, which meant that he had completely melted into the tide. This time, even the mental devil was ‘digested’ together with him. There was no other self that could save him.

However—

Li Yao had never fought alone. Never.

“Li Yao!”

He suddenly heard a very familiar roar. It was the happiest and loudest voice in the world.

“Wait!”

He shivered and craned his head out of the lost silver maze. “I think… my wife is calling me!”

Deep inside the tide, in the middle of the endless stars, unusual ripples were spreading out, as if a certain force had drilled in from the outside and opened a channel and a window.

Inside the window was the scene that was taking place in the sky of the reincarnation prison. Countless resistors from the battlefields of the universe charged into the atmosphere together with ten thousand wrecked starships that looked like shooting stars.

Maybe it was because of the high-speed friction between the debris and the atmosphere, or maybe it was because of the distortion and conflict when they crashed into the three-point-five-dimensional space from the three-dimensional universe, but the entire sky was burning furiously. The magnificent flames burnt away all the dark clouds and the torrential rain, leaving a bright and sunny sky.

Although it was the awe-inspiring iron deities that appeared in the window, Li Yao could sense countless familiar auras from the cold metal and the scorching flames.

“My wife, Li Jialing, Long Yangjun, Han Te, the boxing champion, Yan Liren, Boss Bai, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and… thousands of friends and compatriots, you are all here!”

Hot tears flowed out of Li Yao’s eyes as he broke the silver seal. “What—what is going on exactly? How many years have passed in the outside world? How did you have the ability to break out of the Pangu Universe and come to the center of the cosmos, and even become the vanguard against the flood?”

“It’s been several days. A thousand years have passed in this world. The flow of time inside and outside of the Reincarnation Prison is not consistent. I don’t know how long you’ve been locked in this place, but many years have passed outside!

Ding Lingdang was still the same as before. She was riding an iron demon that looked like a furious red lotus. More than half of the sky was filled with her raging flames. Billions of red dragons seemed to be shooting out from the gaps of her fists. She waved her fists hard and roared at the window, “I said that no existence or place can take away my husband without my permission. Even if it is the deepest level of hell or the deepest level of hell, if they dare to touch even a hair on your head, I will tear down their ancestral temple and crush their souls. They will be cut into pieces and beg for death!

“How about you, Li Yao? Count carefully. How many hairs did this bastard touch?”

“Well, not bad. Not many.”

Li Yao felt dizzy, as if he had been hit in the waist by happiness. “No. I’ve been through countless reincarnations in this place. Now that so many years have passed in the outside world, what do you look like right now? Why don’t you—”

“It’s a long story. Since you were teleported away by the Bifrost Bridge, a lot of things have happened in the Pangu Universe and the entire multiverse. Every one of us has experienced soul-stirring, perilous adventures, including me, who first raised a small revolution in a place named ‘Battle Aura Universe’ and turned it upside down. Then, I went to the center of the multiverse, passed through the fortress of the black hole, found the Resistance Alliance, and figured out a way to send messages through novels.

Ding Lingdang shouted passionately, “You have to tell everyone’s adventures and stories clearly. Even if you spend ten days and ten nights writing a 10-million-word novel, you won’t be able to finish it. After we kill this wretched scumbag, we will drink wine, eat meat, and slowly chat with each other. Right now, the most important thing is that we are here. Everyone is here. We want you to remember it deeply and believe it forever. No matter when, no matter where, no matter how desperate the situation seems to be, you are not fighting alone. You still have me. You still have us. You still have—the entire universe!”

“Yes, Dad. You’ve saved us countless times. It’s our turn to save you now!”

Xiao Ming and Wen Wen spoke at the same time—the two little guys were steering two super fortresses that looked like satellites. They said in a low and muffled voice, “Countless wonderful and interesting stories have happened to the two of us, and we have evolved to an inconceivable state. We will slowly tell them to you later!”

“Yes, Brother Yao. We are here!”

Riding an iron demon that looked like a lion spreading its wings, Li Jialing bellowed, “The imperium, the federation, and the Holy Alliance are all fine. Rest assured!”

“Master Yao, I’m here, too!”

Han Te’s iron devil was wreathed in a mysterious, mysterious mist. It was supposed to be an exciting occasion, but there was sorrow in his voice that could not be erased. “I… paid a terrible price, too. I’ve become the ultimate, invincible Grand Magus in the universe. Sob. Anyway, I’m very awesome right now. Let me rescue you with the power of magic!”

“And me.”

Boss Bai burst into laughter. “I’m told that we will be granted the strength to march into the four-dimensional universe as long as we defeat the flood. Very good. Bring it on!”

“And me.”

The Fist King’s voice was like thousands of cannons firing at the same time. “My ultimate goal is to become the strongest in the universe. Then, the flood tide will be the target that I must defeat.”

“Hong Chao, come out.”

Yan Liren said casually, “Wash your neck and die.”

“In fact, I didn’t want to come.

Long Yangjun yawned. “But everybody insisted that I come. I had no choice.

“This—this bunch of—”

Deep inside the tide, it was half confused and half angry. “Who are they?”

“They are all my family and friends.

Tears were flowing down Li Yao’s face, but his smile was as splendid as a morning glory. He mumbled, “How can a Cultivator dominate the universe and blow up a flood without a group of family and friends?

—

When he reached the fifth chapter, he suddenly discovered that if he wrote it like this, he could have finished it in two days.

This was a miscalculation. It couldn’t be helped. He had boasted that he would finish writing even if he had to stay up all night with tears in his eyes…

Chapter 3267: Goading

“No. It’s impossible!”

The flood’s voice was shaken for the first time, like the first crack on an ‘impregnable’ dam. “I’ve considered every detail of the battlefield in the universe. All the information and data are under my control. I calculated at the cost of the energy of almost a hundred stars. It is impossible for you to break through the defense line of the Prison of Reincarnation within twenty-four hours, much less infiltrate the depths of the super bodies and reach out to Li Yao. How did you do it? It’s impossible. It’s absolutely impossible!

“I’m sorry. It was my fault.”

A voice that was both distant and close came from the sky that was riddled with holes. Billions of lightning bolts condensed into an ugly face that looked like a bean sprout. Nobody could tell whether it was ‘him’ or ‘it’, a human being or a ghost, a god or a devil.

Lightning sniffed her ‘nose’ unwillingly.

Seeing the face through the window, Li Yao was both surprised and delighted. He shrieked, “Lu Qingchen, you are Lu Qingchen. Why are you not dead yet? You—you have melded with the lightning creature?”

“Hello, Uncle Li. Is this how you greet a polite junior after hundreds of years?”

The expression on Lightning’s big face was extremely distressed. With a sour face, she said, “In fact, I’m in quite an awkward position. You said that I’m clearly a legendary villain who should hide in the darkness and watch the two of you suffer heavy losses in secret. You said that I should play some tricks to balance the two of you and then come out in the end to reap the benefits. However, your so-called ‘Resistance Alliance’ is really too lousy. All the tactics and strategies have been calculated clearly by the other party. I want to play the ‘Beheading Tactic’, but I can’t find any loopholes at all. I simply can’t enter through the door. If I don’t lend you a hand, I can only watch as you are played to death by this flood. At the same time, my most beloved federation will also be completely exposed. I really don’t want to make a move, but what can I do?

“It’s true. You are too weak, especially you. You’ve received the legacy of the lightning life, too, but you didn’t wield the strength that you should have. Even though you’ve entered the legendary Form Synthesis Stage, you were still fooled by the guy. You’ve disgraced the lightning life and the Star Glory Federation. I’m too lazy to talk to you, but I can’t stand it any longer!

“You—”

Li Yao had mixed feelings. “Anyways, it’s great that you are still alive!”

“Stop. Don’t say anything mushy. I remember clearly what I owe you and what you owe me. Don’t ever consider me a comrade on your side. I will never be one. In short, after everything is over, I will settle the scores between you, me, the federation, and the Holy Alliance however I want. I will accept all the grudges.”

Lu Qingchen smiled. “But right now, you may consider me a… minor miracle!”

“Miracle…”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, his eyes brimming with tears. There seemed to be two black holes in his fists. “Yes. A miracle!”

“What miracle? They are just a bunch of ignorant fools.

The surging tide raised enormous tides and flooded toward all the iron demons in the sky at the same time. “Do you bacteria and viruses even know what the ‘Great Purge Agreement’ is? What is the ‘quick collapse’? What is ‘Lord Singularity’? What are the ‘Eternal Swallowers’?”

“I don’t know.”

Ding Lingdang shook her head.

“Not interested.”

The boxing champion rubbed his iron fists.

“Is it important?”

Xiao Ming and Wen Wen spoke at the same time.

“I do.”

Yan Liren looked at his sword. “Soon, you will all be dead souls under my sword.

“…” Hong Chao.

“Save it.”

Boss Bai chuckled and said slowly, “Although we don’t know exactly what the bullsh*t is, we can imagine how terrifying it is beyond the four-dimensional world.

“However, so what?

“Even if your bullsh*t is a hundred times, a thousand times, or even ten thousand times more awesome than yours, so what? You are already awesome enough. You have reached the limits of our imagination.

“If we fight with you, only death awaits us. If we fight with the ‘Great Purge Agreement’, ‘Fast Collapse’, ‘Lord of Singularity’, or the ‘Ancient Devourer’, the best outcome we can expect is death. What’s the difference? It doesn’t matter if we die. Even if we are cowards, we are not immortal. Even if we are, we are still cowards. Is it interesting?”

“You…”

Hong Chao was lost for words. “That was too reckless!”

“Maybe it was a bit reckless, but it was satisfying enough.

Ding Lingdang said, “Compared to the quasi-god level civilization that dominates the center of the multiverse like yourself, we are just small fries from the countryside. We don’t know much about the four-dimension and five-dimension, the Singularity and Quickness, and so on. However, in our countryside, even the dog slaughterers in the slums can sing to their heart’s content after drinking their fill of wine. We are people who have cultivated for hundreds of years after all. If we can’t even understand this bit of thought, then before we draw our sword and pull out our fist, we have to be cautious, hesitant, and overcautious. If we can calculate every detail, then we might as well become actuaries. If we can’t even be compared to dog slaughterers, then how can we cultivate!?”

“Exactly!”

Everyone roared at the same time, “The most important thing for us Cultivators is to have a clear mind and to be f*cking happy!

“My mind is clear. I can do whatever I want. One against a thousand. How f*cking satisfying…”

Li Yao’s entire body began to tremble. The silver liquid enveloping his body evaporated and retreated, revealing his fiercely fluctuating chest and his fists that were about to explode. “This is me. This is a cultivator. This is—a human being!”

“You…”

He didn’t expect that Li Yao would be able to summon such tremendous strength under the cheers of his family and friends. Even the flood tide was shivering in fear. Then, it flew into a rage out of humiliation and summoned more silver liquid to drown Li Yao.

Gulp! Gulp! Gulp!

The near-infinite ‘quantum energy’ bombarded Li Yao’s soul and suppressed his self-awareness. He was lost in the depths of the tide of regret and fear again.

“Honey…”

Li Yao’s eyes turned silver. He mumbled as if he were hypnotized, “Say, if there is a way to reverse time, to fix all the mistakes, to fix all the flaws, and to make all the beautiful things eternal, how about it?”

“What are you talking about? You are being controlled by it again. Wake up, Li Yao. Not good. Of course not good!”

Ding Lingdang shouted anxiously, “Every one of us is the result of the carving of time. We are the amalgamation of all our advantages and disadvantages. Everything that happened in the past is irreversible. That’s why we are who we are right now. If we go back in time and go back to the beginning, won’t we be denying the meaning of our existence? Even if everything starts over again, and all the regrets, mistakes, and shortcomings are changed, will we still be the real us when a ‘perfect’ us is born?

“No, I don’t like such an idea. I don’t like it at all! It’s true that everybody has regrets that they want to change, mistakes that can’t be changed, and even fatal flaws. But it is exactly those things that together with the happy, happy, and right things that constitute the truest, unique us!

“Take me for example. I know I have a lot of shortcomings. I’m too competitive, too headstrong. I’m too stubborn and even too unyielding. Moreover, I’m often too busy with work and cultivation to accompany you. I don’t like to put on makeup or dress up. I don’t know how to sweet talk and act like a little bird. Sometimes, when I think about it, I feel quite guilty. I feel that I’ve let you down. But—but this is me. This is the one and only Ding Lingdang who can’t be found anywhere else in the entire universe! If I had a second chance in life, I would have changed everything. I would have become a woman who likes makeup and beautiful pictures. I would often lean on you and act cute. I would say things like ‘my husband is bad’ all day long. Do you still like me that way?”

Li Yao thought carefully for a moment.

Then, he shuddered and lost control over the flood tide.

“Am I right? It’s terrible, isn’t it? I feel terrible just thinking about it.”

Ding Lingdang continued to roar, “It’s the same for you. Although you also have a lot of flaws… As the father of the federation, the supreme ruler of all worlds, your personality is too wretched. It’s very rare for you to reveal your domineering aura for more than half an hour. When you can’t defeat the enemy, you will still run away without shame. You like to crawl through ventilation ducts. Your kidney function is so-so. Often, when your brain is fried, you appear to be stupid. At the drop of a hat, you say some hot-blooded and shameful words. However, this is you. Everything in your past life has created these flaws, or rather, these ‘characteristics’. And these flaws and characteristics have created a unique, charismatic, and deeply loved you. If you go back in time and restart your life, you seem to have chosen a ‘better’ direction, but in the end, these unique and charming flaws no longer exist. If that happens, will you still be the real you? Will you still be the Li Yao that I have entrusted everything to? The Li Yao that I deeply love?”

“It—it does make sense…”

Li Yao mumbled to himself and suddenly felt that something was not right. “Wait. What did you say just now about my flaws?”

“Huh?”

Ding Lingdang’s eyes were wide open. “They say that you are sometimes sleazy, that you don’t last long enough to reveal your dominance, and that you have other shortcomings that everybody in the universe knows about. Are you denying it?”

“No. I admit that.

Li Yao was completely awakened. All the silver liquid on his body was gone. He was no longer under the flood’s control. “What happened next? What did you say next?”

“… Say that you often burn your brain to make you look stupid and say hot-blooded and shameful words?”

Ding Lingdang said, “Don’t mind these small problems. I don’t despise them at all. Really. On the contrary, I think they are quite cute and have quite a personality. This is my most beloved husband!”

“No. I admit all the shortcomings. It doesn’t matter whether you despise them or not.

“However,” Li Yao said, “I vaguely heard that you said something between ‘I like to crawl through ventilation tubes’ and ‘I’ll look stupid if my brain is fried’. Did you say anything else?”

“I don’t think so. I don’t really remember. Anyway, my dear husband, there’s something that I’ve kept in my heart for hundreds of years that I haven’t been able to say. In fact, I’ve always felt that you are the strongest man in the universe. I’ve always secretly admired you from the bottom of my heart. It’s just that I’m too embarrassed to say it because of my reputation as a strong woman.”

Ding Lingdang put her hands together in prayer. “Now, it’s time for you to show all your valiancy and break out of this damn cage. How can a mere flood trap a man like you?”

Chapter 3268: Yesterday, Today, And Future!

Motivated by Ding Lingdang, the flames of war flowing out of Li Yao’s every pore were like magma roasting a glacier, melting the flood.

“So that’s how it is. My wife has always admired me?”

Li Yao clenched his fists and punched his chest hard. “That’s right. I’m a man of steel who marches forward bravely. I’m definitely not a mediocre man who wallows in the mistakes of the past, sighs, and regrets. If I don’t like my current appearance and want to go back in time, what’s the difference between me and a rogue who stirs up trouble with his chess pieces and refuses to admit his mistakes?

“I have no regrets. Hong Chao, you are a petty man who won’t admit defeat, but I am a man!”

…

BOOM!

From the perspective of the awakened Cultivators on the Ark Island, they could only see that, when the silver-white giant was about to turn into an absolutely precise ball, intense flames suddenly burst out of the core. The silver-white liquid metal was blown everywhere, and clusters of red lotuses and furious flames jumped out of the broken debris.

Li Yao broke free from the flood and stood proudly in the sky of Earth’s—Reincarnation Prison again!

“Is this… the legendary ‘Body Synthesis Stage’?”

High in the sky, Ding Lingdang sensed Li Yao’s soaring flames of war. She discovered that, although Li Yao was only wearing scraps of copper and iron, his aura was ten times more intimidating than those who had been armed by the iron demons. She bulged her cheeks in surprise and turned to Lu Qingchen, asking suspiciously, “What happened exactly? How did you know? You said that he was ‘so-so’, and that I admired him so much that I could trigger the endless potential in the deepest part of his soul and unleash the greatest strength?”

“It’s hard to explain to Speaker Ding.

In short, no matter how tough and dominating you pretend to be, you are a woman after all. You will never understand what men are thinking and how to trigger our… fury.
