Lu Qingchen looked at Li Yao in astonishment.

At this moment, Li Yao became the center of attention for all the awakeners, all the visitors from outer space, and even Hong Chao.

The ‘super-large magical equipment’ that had been refined by the carrier fleet a moment ago was torn apart into scraps, but the new equipment was attracted by his vitality and rushed at him.

They were the debris of billions of starships that had fallen from the battlefield of the universe.

All the debris of the starships were controlled, compressed, and refined by the invisible giant hands. They were assembled next to Li Yao and gradually formed the framework of an indomitable giant. Then, the nerves, bones, and muscles came close and formed an inseparable, organic whole.

Not hundreds of meters, not thousands of meters, but a giant tens of thousands of meters tall, standing on the sun, the moon, and the stars, shocking the entire universe.

The boundless ocean was boiling for it. The sun, the moon, and the stars were reflecting its golden brilliance. Billions of lightning bolts were weaving the most precise armor around it. It was a giant that had never been seen in the Prison of Reincarnation before and was destined to be the terminator of countless cycles of time.

“This guy…”

Even Li Yao himself was deeply shocked by the surging and furious strength. He looked at the bronze-colored Titan’s Palm in disbelief and said, “It has far exceeded the limits of the Giant God Soldier. From its size to its internal driving mechanism, it is completely different. It is not a Giant God Soldier, but… a ‘super divine weapon’!

Ding Lingdang sensed that Li Yao’s soul waves were spreading out. She was greatly shocked and said, “Any name will do. Really. Time is of the essence. Don’t mind the details.”

“What do you mean? It sounds like the name I’m going to give you is going to be awful.”

Li Yao frowned. He mumbled something to himself, but he was still as obedient as ever. “Alright then. I’ll pick a random one and call you simply ‘Dream Chaser’! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

Li Yao triggered his soul and released tens of thousands of electric arcs through the ‘Super Soldier Dream Chaser’. He pulled the debris of countless starships close to his palm and dissected them into the most fundamental metal dust before he refined a new super saber that was thousands of kilometers long and wreathed in purple electric arcs and crimson flames.

It was literally a saber that could split the ocean or even a planet.

Li Yao raised his saber and pointed it at the silver-white giant that was expanding into the form of a demon not far away.

“It is precisely because we cannot turn back time that we cherish every second of the present and future.

“It is precisely because the mistakes we make can’t be completely erased that we take every mistake or correct option more seriously.

“It is precisely because no one can live forever that we must strive to release the most brilliant brilliance of life!

“The me of yesterday forged the me of today, and the me of today will create the me of tomorrow. It is precisely because there is no medicine for regret in this world and there is no path of retreat behind us that every one of us has to shoulder all the regrets, mistakes, regrets, and wounds. We have to treat these as medals, armor, and blades. We have to advance bravely, break through all difficulties and obstacles, and create an unlimited hope for the future!”

“Looking back at the billions of years of evolution of civilization, right or wrong, betrayal or destruction, the Primeval Lord of Heaven or the super body civilization, yes, there were countless regrets, countless regrets, countless mistakes, and countless things that should have been changed and erased.

“However, our civilization has cast its die and left the most vivid mark in the sea of stars with its clumsy and even wrong strokes. It was the billions of years of history that shaped the civilization of mankind today. The glorious, great, proud, noble, and unique civilization of mankind today, the so-called ‘reversal of time and restart of worlds’, will only destroy the civilization that is loved by all of us. Even if a new civilization of mankind appears in the new timeline, it will not be this one, not mine!

“This is what I’m talking about. The deceased are gone. The ancient spirits should’ve rested in peace a long time ago. The survivors will shoulder all the mistakes, regrets, dreams, and hopes, overcome all the obstacles, overcome the waves, and never look back!

We are not the least interested in being a turtle that hides itself forever. We only hope that someday in the future, the last drop of blood of the last warrior of the great civilization can be shed on the path of glory leading to the end of the universe!

“Because this is our destiny. This is Cultivators. This is… mankind!”

Li Yao’s every word was like a hot shooting star that had been tossed into the depths of the ocean. When he roared the loudest, every raindrop in the ocean and the sky was on fire.

The flood tide was rendered speechless by his words. The computational ability of billions of stars had all turned into garbage.

“Is it really necessary?”

Standing below his feet, all the awakened Cultivators on the ark, including Zhang Daniu, felt that they were ants. “The sound and electricity are too much!”

As if he had heard Zhang Daniu’s shout, Li Yao smiled and extended an invitation to him. “Come on, Master Niu. A war is not something that I can fight alone. The fate of all mankind will be decided by all mankind. Join me and fight side by side!”

“I—I can do it, too?”

Pointing at himself, Zhang Daniu stammered, “I—I haven’t finished ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’!”

“It doesn’t matter. I’m not going to argue with you about the specific theories and formulas. Just tell me the conclusion. Listen carefully.”

Li Yao said, “According to the secret that I dug out from the core of the flood tide, which is the will of the earth, when I almost melded with it, there are only two outcomes of our battle. If we win, all the ‘parallel universes’ that are sealed by it, or at least ‘parallel Earths’, will be freed. In other words, countless Earths with subtle differences might appear out of nowhere in an unbelievable way. There might be another Zhang Daniu in another ‘parallel Earth’.

“Remember, you must remember that emotions, wills, and dreams that are strong enough can break the most solid barrier between parallel universes and cause subtle disturbances to the other side of the universe. In other words, as long as your obsession is strong enough, you will always want to stimulate all your brain cells to think hard and transmit your love and obsession for ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’. Then, one of the countless ‘parallel planets’ where you live will receive your obsession and finish ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ for you!

“Is that so?”

Zhang Daniu was stunned as he listened, “I don’t understand. There’s such a thing?”

“Of course, the so-called ‘parallel universe’ is a place where everything is possible. As long as your obsession is strong enough, or even your own strength, it is enough to split the universe and create new possibilities!

Li Yao said, “You must remember your dream and destiny. If we win the battle, you will certainly finish ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ in a certain ‘parallel Earth’ that has been liberated in the future!”

Chapter 3269: Last Slash!

“Then, what if we fail?”

Zhang Daniu became nervous. He looked at the silver-white giant on the horizon that had also expanded to an indomitable height and had even swallowed half of the sky. Hong Chao flew into a rage out of humiliation and completely tore off his calm and collected facade. It was as if he was a hybrid of a giant vortex that devoured everything and a mutated octopus.

“If we fail…”

Li Yao smiled and handed over the heavy burden. “All the more reason for you to remember everything that happened in front of your eyes. Turn all the sounds and pictures that you’ve observed into the most intense obsessions, including the most noble, the most despicable, the most heroic, and the most melodramatic.

“Listen to my every word. Believe it or not, if we fail, time will be reversed and everything will be restarted. No, it won’t be as simple as a few decades. Billions of years will be reset to the starting point of the timeline, the original ‘origin planet’, and everything in front of you, the entire world that you cherish, all the feelings and dreams of the people living in the world, hope and desperation, flesh and blood, and free will will will all be obliterated.

“However, I still don’t believe that the so-called ‘reversal of time’ can erase everything. At the very least, I believe that the noblest will of mankind, the strongest obsession, is enough to resist the scourge of the flood tide. Therefore, as long as your obsession is strong enough, even if time goes back billions of years, everything will return to the state of the ‘origin earth’. Maybe, under the influence of your obsession, a new you will write ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’!

“If that is the case, you must seize the opportunity to finish ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’. You must write down everything that happened today and tell it to the people after the ‘time line reset’. Let them know the schemes of the Flood Tide, let them know all the glory and dreams, and let them know that they are not as simple as ordinary hairless monkeys on an ordinary planet. They—we can be the center of the universe, the ‘Observers’. We are the one and only, the strongest fighting species in the sea of stars. We are the masters of the stars. We are the adventurers of the multiverse and the multiverse. We are the challengers and the winners!

“As long as they are bold enough to dream, to fight, to risk everything for the slim hope, to ignite the impulse in the deepest part of their soul, to release the most brilliant fire of life in a moment, anything is possible!

“This is your destiny. The odds of victory and failure are fifty-fifty. If we fail, we might have to rely on you to make a comeback. Remember, Master Niu!”

“I—I seem to have remembered it.”

With a determined look on his face, Zhang Daniu nodded his head slowly. Pondering for a moment, he suddenly said, “I’ve thought about it for a while. Maybe that’s what it means. Because of the existence of countless parallel universes, every parallel universe has subtle changes. Theoretically speaking, there are countless versions of me who are not very different from each other, and countless versions of me are interfering with each other based on our obsessions. As long as my obsessions are strong enough in this place, I can influence the other me in the other universe and make him do things that I can’t, can’t finish, and can’t do even if he wants to?”

“Yes, yes, yes!”

Li Yao said excitedly, “Master Niu, your understanding is correct. So, try to accumulate your ‘obsession’. As long as we win, you will certainly finish ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ in a parallel universe!”

“Who cares about that?”

Zhang Daniu waved his hands and legs in excitement. “Who cares whether is the ending or the ending? It doesn’t matter. In any case, what you’re trying to say is that as long as I think hard and use all my strength, I think that in a certain parallel universe, I will be able to create a very popular, very popular, and very profitable web novel. I don’t need to care about anything else. In any case, if it’s popular, will I really be able to write it? Then, I will be able to make a fortune every day. Beautiful women, hehehehe?”

“Uh…”

Li Yao was dazed for a long time before he replied, “Theoretically speaking, everything is possible in the endless universe and hope. It’s not like such a thing has never happened, is it?”

“Roger that!”

Endless aura burst out of Zhang Daniu’s body. His voice was even thicker than before. “Then let’s fight. Let’s show the damn tide the strength of mankind. Take it. It is my strongest belief!”

Zhang Daniu raised his hands and sent his endless determination to Li Yao.

Not just him, all the other awakeners, as well as the visitors from the outside world, heard the conversation between Li Yao and him and resonated with Li Yao.

In the intense soul reverberation, the wrinkles of time outside of the reincarnation prison finally collapsed.

“This—this is—”

All the heroes on Earth saw an unbelievable scene at the same time.

The sky seemed to have ‘disappeared’ all of a sudden. They were directly exposed to the dazzling sea of stars.

In the meantime, the memories of countless generations, including the glory and dreams that shook the universe and roared in the sea of stars before he was dragged into the reincarnation prison, were awakened.

“As it turns out…”

The martyred spirits looked at each other in bewilderment. They clenched their fists and smiled. “We’ve been sleeping for too long. It’s time to stand up and decide our own fate!”

…

With the endless sea of stars as the battlefield.

Countless ‘parallel Earths’ that had been sealed surrounded the tides and the resistors like brilliant pearls.

The sun, the moon, and the stars were all eclipsed by the raging flames.

Billions of kilometers of electric arcs and flames divided the dark vacuum into interconnected chess boards, or rather, rings where all bets were placed.

Centered around Li Yao’s ‘Super Soldier Dream Chaser’, billions of heroes gathered and confronted the tide that seemed to occupy half the universe not far away coldly.

In the dream-like battlefield of the universe, Li Yao raised his saber named ‘Hope’ and took the first step toward the flood.

“I suddenly realized something.

Li Yao stared at the tide and said, one word after another, “You’ve been hoping and even guiding this battle, haven’t you? Your ‘Time Reversal Array’ lacks the last bit of energy. Only the ultimate confrontation between us can trigger enough energy to complete your evil and idiotic plan.

“That’s right.”

The tide turned into a silver monster with three heads and six arms. It bared its sharp teeth at the resistors. “You finally understand that everything is under my control. You have no chance at all.

Li Yao smiled and raised his saber even higher.

“Do you know that I’ve met a lot of opponents who boasted that they had everything under their control? But in the end, they all failed. I’m afraid that it was not a coincidence but inevitable.

“In this world,” Li Yao said coldly, “change is the only constant. The only things that can be controlled are the dead. The will and soul of human beings will never be enslaved, controlled, or manipulated. Those who are confident that they can control everything will often destroy themselves because of ‘loss of control’!

“I am different from them. I am no longer a human being. I am the universe itself.

The tide replied, “I control almost infinite ‘quantum energy’. With a wave of my hand, I can release countless ‘parallel cosmic fission chain reactions’. According to my precise calculation, I have a 99.9999% winning rate in the sea of the multiverse. Your winning rate is only one billionth!”

“No, not one billionth, but 50%. Do you remember Schrödinger’s cat? Dead or alive, it is always 50%.”

Li Yao smiled confidently. He took the third step, as if he had crossed several galaxies with one step and turned countless planets into dust below his feet. “I even think that our odds of winning are much higher than 50%. I should say that we are definitely going to win, and you only have a one in a billion chance. Do you know why?

“Because from the moment you choose to go back in time, you have lost the courage to face the future. When you encounter an enemy that is slightly more difficult to deal with, you will be used to thinking whether or not you can cut off the connection and run away so that you can start all over again. How can you become a real expert if you are so shameless that you are always regretting your decision?

“Therefore, your failure has been decided. Of course, you are not a human being, but you are definitely not a ‘quasi-god civilization’. After all, you are just a cowardly turtle.

“B*stards like you are not qualified to be our enemies. Just stick your head out and accept your death. Don’t hinder us from marching into an unknown universe!

“You lowly cockroach!”

Flood Tide burst into fury and gnashed his teeth.

But faced with Li Yao’s mockery, it could not come up with a rebuttal and could only repeat, “You have no idea…”

Before it finished, Li Yao had already taken the last step.

The saber that pierced through the sea of stars was held high, too.

The bright blade reflected the brilliance of billions of stars. It was more like the most brilliant will and the most touching hope of billions of heroic spirits.

Li Yao knew.

He was well aware of the mistakes and detours that human civilization had made.

The formidability of the flood tide, the coldness of the ‘Great Purging Agreement’, the desperation of the ‘quick collapse’, the cunningness of ‘Lord Singularity’, and the craziness of the ‘Eternal Devouring Beast’, he had learned a thing or two from the information that the flood tide had just sent over. Even if he could not calculate it, he could at least imagine it.

How difficult the road ahead was, how dark the distance was, how much blood was needed to forge the glory, and how many sacrifices were needed to flatten the thorns. He knew all of them.

However, there was no turning back now. The river of time was endless. They could only march forward with their swords and never return.

He bid farewell to the past and ignored the future for now.

Now was the moment.

Behind him were his loyal friends and billions of compatriots who were waving their sabers, swords, and fists to the maximum just like him.

In front of him was the deep universe, the stars that contained endless danger and hope.

The tide between him and the stars was like an insignificant pebble.

The saber in his hand was as hot as the beating heart deep inside his chest.

Therefore, Vulture Li Yao faced the flood directly and slashed out, letting out a final roar:

“Bring it on, universe!”

Chapter 3270: Untitled

Today was the day of the bonus.

The style may be different from the main story. Please understand and prepare yourselves, brothers and sisters.

As mentioned before, the additional chapters were sorted in chronological order. The first chapter told the story of a certain someone billions of years ago, after the nuclear war and before the arrival of the meteoroids. It was named ‘Skinned Mouse’.

The length is relatively long. I hope everyone likes it.

If a friend is willing to write their own story under the framework of the “Four Universities of Cultivation”, they can also fill in some of the details. Please let go of yourself and do whatever you want. As long as you are willing, Old Cow can also post it and everyone can share it together.

Alright, let’s cut the nonsense and start a new journey.

—

Billions of years ago, on the Origin Planet, the End Era.

A war.

The war almost changed everything.

But it could not change who started the war.

…

The city was like a rotten corpse. The broken walls were like its broken bones. The broken roads were twisted veins and nerves. The walking dead that might or might not have been dead were the maggots that lived on the dead bodies of the city.

The boy felt that the rusted chains were deeply embedded in his ankles. Together with the upside-down and upside-down situation, he could barely feel his feet.

But his brain was expanding so much that his bloodshot eyes were about to explode.

Through the interconnecting blood streaks, he could see that the sunlight was struggling in the thick clouds, overflowing the lackluster colors of the decaying bodies. The skyscrapers in the city that had not collapsed yet constituted a raging skyline, which made him realize why the city was named ‘Tombstone Town’.

Cities were tombs. They were the souls of the dead living in the tombs. Or according to the natives, they were ‘demons’.

His line of sight continued to fall. The twisted buildings that had yet to collapse had long been piled up into fortresses by the armor. One after another, deformed faces emerged from the pitch black shooting holes, watching with great interest as those people butchered him.

Further down were coldly shining spears on which all kinds of skulls and body parts had been stabbed. There were the most common double-headed bulls and mutated boars, as well as the expensive ‘delicious food’ such as the Heaven’s Feet Snake and the Terror Chicken.

The boy even saw a hand hanging on an iron hook, placed in the middle by various kinds of meat.

It was a pitch-black hand that did not seem to have much flesh, much less much appetite.

But hands were hands after all. Both Tombstone Town and the people of the Furious Blood Wasteland believed that the meat contained special energy that could help them resist radiation, increase their strength, and bring them good luck.

Therefore, it was the most in demand item in the meat market. Very few cooked meat could be delivered to the meat market. Usually, the meat would be divided among the zombies, mobs, or ‘demons’.

The boy’s hands were fair and tender, like tofu or alfalfa. They even looked like the hands of an underground person. This was a heaven-sent good luck or bad luck.

To avoid trouble, he usually covered his hands with dirty bandages and gloves.

But right now, his hands had been exposed to countless greedy and sordid eyes. Some of them were even drooling.

The boy felt sick. He wanted to throw up, but unfortunately, he had been beaten into a corner.

The boy’s eyes continued to fall. This time, he saw the butcher, who was as strong as a mountain of meat, grinding the meat cleaver with a smile. When the cleaver was done, he even stuck out his half-meter long tongue to test the sharpness.

When the butcher discovered that the boy was looking at him, the smile on his face intensified. He even winked at the boy and made a face.

The boy retched again. His eyes fell to the ground.

The ground of Tombstone or any other town in this world was always the same. It was thick, stinky, and seemed to be piled up by a thick layer of humus. Suspicious wastewater and stains were everywhere. Occasionally, when the sunlight tore apart the clouds and dust and poured them to the ground like magma, the earth would be immediately set on fire, and colorful venomous fog would be raised. Once they were exposed to the venomous fog, even the soldiers or the bandits who had undergone genetic modification would feel dizzy, lose their functions, and even die on the spot.

However, the mice and cockroaches continued to survive in this rotten world. They were like fish in water, reproducing in large numbers and showing signs of replacing human beings.

The boy saw a fat, big mouse crawling out of the sticky, dark gutter and crawling towards a piece of meat that had “accidentally” fallen to the ground.

It was very vigilant. Knowing that the crazily excited giants nearby were creatures even more cruel and cunning than itself, it hesitated for a long time to make sure that nobody was paying attention to it before it lunged at the minced meat like a lightning.

Then, with a ‘swoosh’, a fishing line that was as thin as hair and controlled by a spring tied up one of its legs and tossed it into the air. Then, the butcher rolled it up with his tongue and slapped it on the chopping board.

The butcher had finished sharpening his butcher’s knife. He could use this bastard to test his knife. His seemingly clumsy pair of hands sprayed out a sheet of white light. In a short amount of time, he had peeled off the skin of this fat and large mouse and hung it on an iron hook. At this time, the mouse was still not completely dead. Its tail was twitching, and its pair of lackluster eyes met with the boy’s.

“Poor animals.”

The boy thought to himself, There is no such thing as an ‘unintentional drop’ in the world.

Then, he smiled bitterly in his heart.

Wasn’t his experience exactly the same as the skinning mouse’s?

As the appetizer before the main course, the Skinned Mouse was soon bought by someone at the price of five scrapped cell phones. Mouse meat was one of the few fresh meat that the devil race could eat nowadays, not to mention that they were being skinned alive on the spot. The price was indeed not high. The customer held the meat in his hands and beamed with joy. He started gnawing on the meat on the spot with cracking sounds, which even made the customers salivate.

Perhaps, it was also for the purpose of advertising and telling everyone that the delicious meat had arrived. That was why they were selling the living skinning mice at such a cheap price.

“Boss, it’s time to cut the meat!”

“Hurry up! I can’t wait any longer!”

“Such good meat is worth any price. Tell me, how much are you selling it for?”

The boy heard the howls of the devils.

He saw faces that were either rotten, swollen, covered in leeches, or had three to five eyes that had undergone genetic mutations because of radiation. Cruelness and greed were written all over their faces.

The butcher picked up the boy and threw him to the chopping board where the skinning mice had been skinned.

The butcher stuck out his tongue and licked Li Yao’s face, spraying hot streams of gastric acid at him. He chuckled and said, “Don’t move. It doesn’t hurt. Uncle’s skills are very good. I guarantee that the blood will be drained clean.”

The boy didn’t move.

He did not cry.

Like everyone else in this era, his tears had dried up the moment he was born. He had long forgotten the feeling of crying.

The butcher patted his face with the snow-white butcher knife, patted his chest, and shouted to the impatient customers, “Come on! Come on! Top-grade meat! Top-grade meat! Look at this face! Look at this pair of hands! No horns! No leeches! No rot! No cysts! Not even a trace of mutation! This meat is even more delicious than the meat of the underground world!

“The delicious meat is so delicious that even the gods can’t stand on their feet. One piece of such delicious meat is enough to cure all kinds of diseases. You can even enter and exit the third-degree radiation zone freely. Come on, bid! Nice meat! Nice meat that is fair and tender!”

The boy saw that more and more people were gathering in the meat market. More than half of the devils in Tombstone Town were crowding close. The sounds of their saliva congregated into an ocean of acid.

The dirty eyes were emitting dirty brilliance, but they scared him even more than the butcher’s knife did. He closed his eyes and asked his brother for help.

“Brother, save me.”

The boy said.

“Don’t be scared, Little Lu. Don’t be scared.”

The older brother said, “Tell this butcher that you absolutely won’t run away. You’re so small, and there are so many people here. It’s impossible for you to run away. So, can he let go of the chains on your feet? This way, your blood will flow faster, and the clotted blood won’t remain in your feet. It’s also more delicious. You can call him ‘Uncle’. Your feet can be sold at a higher price. He will definitely agree.”

“Then, you have to listen carefully. This brother won’t say these words a second time. Then, this brother will kill everyone in the meat market. This will buy you approximately five to ten minutes of time. You need to immediately find a military backpack… Do you see the camouflage baldy over there? Use his backpack to fill the meat in the meat market. Then, take the third hair-streaked shotgun on the right and the fifth three-eyed handgun. Remember, don’t forget the bullets. Run to the left. Run to the ‘Old John’s Car Company’ and get a modified car that is suitable for long-distance running in the desert. The day before yesterday, the ‘Nigger Gang’ happened to have a very good one. It was modified at Old John’s place. Today, it should have been modified. Hopefully, it hasn’t been transported away yet. All of this needs to be done in ten minutes, no, seven minutes. Otherwise, the remaining half of the people in Tombstone Town will catch up. Do you understand?”

“I understand.”

The boy nodded silently and stammered, “But—but I don’t know how to drive. What should I do?”

“It doesn’t matter. It’s very simple. Haven’t we seen it several times at Old John’s place? I should be able to.”

His brother said, “But… killing everyone here will consume too much of my strength… I might be able to… Anyway, I’ll drive the shuttle in the beginning, but you must learn it quickly. You’ll have to walk on your own later!”

“What?”

The boy was shocked. He felt that he had missed a step and had no one to rely on. He was even more scared than when he was tied up and sent to the chopping block. “Brother, what are you talking about? We were together when we were born. Don’t leave me. I can’t do it alone!”

“You can do it. In fact, you always can. Your potential is far higher than mine, but you haven’t discovered it yet.

His brother said, “Anyway, don’t think too much about it. Just beg the butcher to let go of your feet first. Remember, speak in a pitiful and helpless tone. Right now, your pity is our greatest weapon.”

The boy swallowed hard and looked at the butcher pitifully.

At this moment, the butcher had already raised his butcher’s knife high. There was no telling whether he was showing off his saber art or he was really going to chop off the butcher’s knife.

The boy’s mind went blank. His throat seemed to be blocked by frozen blood. He could not utter a single word.

“Speak, quickly speak!”

His brother said.

“Fragrant meat! Excellent meat! It’s a fair price!”

The butcher said.

“Hurry up and do it! What are you dragging your feet for? Chip, bullets, gasoline, engines, body armor, sniper rifles, and even armored vehicles. Just tell me what price you want. I can afford it!”

The customers who looked neither human nor ghost said.

“Hehehehehe! Hehehehehehe! Hahahaha!”

The wind that entangled Tombstone Town, savaged the entire Wasteland, and was mixed with radiation dust and a rotten smell.

“I…”

The boy stammered. His voice was hoarse. He could not hear what he was saying at all amid the hysterical screams of the customers.

However, another high-pitched, viper-like voice was a hundred times louder than his. It was like a bucket of cold water that had been poured on a heated meat market.

The boy saw that a very damp hand had grabbed the butcher’s knife, which was about to fall, and the butcher’s posterity bag, which made his face extremely ugly.

It was ugly, if not terrifying.

Then, the boy heard the owner of the damp hands say lazily in an even wetter voice, “This is my sheep. Who says I’m selling it?”

Chapter 3271: Untitled

The heated meat market was immediately frozen.

The customers who were drooling just now seemed to have a lump of wet hair stuck in their throat. They could not utter a single sound.

The boy managed to turn his face and saw a very “wet” man.

Ignoring the cobra-like lumps on his forehead, the man was actually quite decent, but his sweat glands seemed to be affected by radiation. Yellow sweat was flowing out all the time. Illuminated by the lackluster sunlight, it looked like a layer of brown scales.

“Snake—Master Snake…”

The butcher, who had been so confident a moment ago, was now at a loss and stammering as he explained, “I—I didn’t know that you weren’t planning to sell it. This sheep was brought by Fourth Brother. He said—he said—”

“Yes, Master Snake.”

The boy looked at the muscular man who had just tied him up. The muscular man bowed in front of ‘Master Snake’ and said with a flattering smile, “This kid cheated in our casino for three days in a row. Today, he was caught red-handed. According to the rules, he has to compensate for his losses. Check his pockets. He doesn’t have a single bullet. His chips were all stolen. His body of good meat is worth some money. So…”

“Oh.”

‘Fourth Brother’s’ face suddenly turned paler than that of a butcher’s. His legs were shivering, and his voice was even more twisted. “I—this—this old man has been busy making deals with the Black Ghost Gang over the past few days. I thought that I shouldn’t bother—”

“Shh…”

Master Snake put one of his long, thin fingers on his thin lips and blew at ‘Fourth Brother’. He smiled and said, “Fourth Brother, what does Master Snake hate most?”

‘Fourth Brother’ swallowed hard and said in a shivering voice, “I hate excuses the most.

“Very good.”

Master Snake stopped looking at him. He narrowed his eyes and glanced at the butcher with half of his cold eyes, while the other half was distributed to all the customers. He said casually, “Let me repeat myself. This sheep is not for sale. Is there a problem?”

Nothing.

Everyone in Tombstone Town knew that there were two things that Master Snake, the big boss of the ‘Heavenly Wolf Gambling Den’, hated the most.

Excuses.

And questions.

“Master Snake, can—can you please bring this sheep back? I really don’t know!”

With a crying face, the butcher said, “I’ll offer you another 50 pounds of double-headed beef. Don’t feel that it’s too little. Recently, the acid rain outside has been too fierce, and the meat is not easy to handle…”

Master Snake smiled. He grabbed the boy’s ankle with one hand and picked him up, as if he were a newborn lamb. “What happened today was the fault of the staff of the casino. We delayed your business for half a day. Take fifty chips from the casino tonight as a gift from me.”

“Thank you, Master Snake. Thank you, thank you!”

The butcher’s eyes were beaming with joy when he heard ‘cash’. His face was shivering, and he was drooling like the other customers.

“Don’t thank me. You should thank ‘justice’.”

Master Snake said, “Everybody in Tombstone Town and the Furious Blood Wasteland knows that justice is my favorite.”

Having said this, the Snake Lord carried the boy upside down and walked out as if there was no one else.

The crowd was like a divided tide as they made way for them one after another. Although many of them were making “cuckoo” sounds from their throats as they looked at the boy with bloodshot eyes, no one dared to say a single “no”.

“Hey, it’s rare to find such a good sheep. We’ve been waiting for such a long time, and you are not letting us cut any meat at all. The way that Tombstone Town does things is too unreasonable!”

BAM!

A muscular man wearing a level 3 armor with tattoos all over his face blocked in front of Uncle Snake. He threw a thick canvas backpack on the ground. It was heavy inside, filled with wrecked cars and steel springs and freshly sharpened blades.

Spring steel was the best raw material for sabers, especially the spring steel of heavy trucks. Together with the Zhanmadao, which had advanced by leaps and bounds after the nuclear war, the combination of hardness and softness could really cut most of the plate armor in half.

Therefore, it was a hard currency in the desert just like bullets.

“I have plenty of money!”

The muscular man with tattoos all over his face stared at the boy’s hand and licked his lips. “His life is mine. I only want one of his hands!”

Master Snake looked at the muscular man.

The muscular man looked at Master Snake impudently. His two companions behind him also tightened their backpacks that were filled with spring steel and gripped their sabers and pistols.

“You are the iron merchants of ‘Steel Flower City’.

Master Snake laughed. “The ‘Scrap Iron Gang’, right?”

Shua! Shua! Chi!

After three sounds that were even softer than the rustling of willow leaves, the scorpion-shell saber on the tattooed man’s waist was empty. The fourth brother’s left hand flew high into the air, only to be pierced through by a sharp and swift saber in the palm of his hand, which was firmly held by Master Snake.

Everyone’s face turned extremely ugly.

The tattooed man looked at the empty sheath on his waist, his face ashen.

The scrap iron helped him forge his own saber. If he could forge the fastest saber in the Furious Blood Wasteland, he would naturally be able to wield the saber at the fastest speed.

But his saber was in the hands of Master Snake, and he didn’t see what was going on at all.

‘Fourth Brother’ was even more dumbfounded as he stared at his left arm, which had been cut off at the wrist. He was drenched in sweat and did not dare to utter a single word.

Now that Master Snake was going to chop off his hand, he was even guilty of spurting blood.

“Please.”

Slowly, slowly, Master Snake handed over his opponent’s saber and ‘Fourth Brother’s’ left hand. “Your hand.”

The tattooed man’s lips moved. Like his fourth brother, sweat appeared on his forehead. He glanced at the boy who was still being carried by Master Snake and did not say anything.

“I know that you want to eat the lamb’s hand because the meat of this hand is too old, but let me tell you something. The owner of this hand used to be the best watcher of the Heavenly Wolf Gambling Den. His hand was soaked in medicine every day and warmed up countless times. Every piece of meat is alive and not old at all. It is also chewy and delicious.

Looking at the tattooed man, Master Snake almost stabbed the tip of his saber into the man’s nose. “Justice. Master Snake is absolutely just when doing things.”

Master Snake said.

Tombstone Town welcomes all the distinguished guests. If you have time tonight, why don’t you go to the Heavenly Wolf Casino and have a seat? Each person will have 100 chips. The boy was carried by Master Snake and left the meat market.

The whole world was upside down. The lead clouds and the purple sun seemed to have turned into a staggering ocean, and the stinky, thick earth turned into a dark sky.

Chapter 3272: Untitled

The boy was thrown onto the bed by Master Snake.

Although he was not fully awake yet, he could not help but want to scream. This was a bed that he had never slept on before. It was as fluffy and soft as a cloud, but it was so extravagant that countless springs had been stuffed into the mattress. It was much more comfortable than a weed nest!

But the boy’s heart seemed to have hit rock bottom. He was not happy at all.

In this world, there were no ‘unintentional pieces of meat’.

Every piece of meat, every bargaining chip, and every drop of blood had a price.

After being saved by Master Snake and sleeping on such a comfortable bed, what price would he have to pay?

‘gentle’ was not gentle at all.

Behind his eyes, a face and a brain were about to crawl out of Bai Xiaolu’s body and swallow him!

No. A shotgun seemed to have been pressed against his abdomen and fired.

He knew that his brother had made his move.

Sure enough, a bloody mist suddenly dispersed from the depths of Master Snake’s eyes, and zigzagging blood flowed out from his mouth, nose, eyes, and ears.

As if drunk, he threw away the whip and staggered to dance.

It was a sign of serious brain damage.

The big boss of the Heavenly Wolf Gambling Den was indeed impressive. In the past, when he was faced with the brutal bandits in the desert, they would spurt blood and die silently every time his brother launched an attack. But right now, he only had one target. His brother launched an attack, but he failed to kill him.

Bai Xiaolu felt that her internal organs were burning.

By the same logic, Master Snake’s skull should be filled with the raging flames of brainwaves.

Master Snake stared at Bai Xiaolu in a daze. It never occurred to him that this “little deer” would grow sharp antlers. He reached out to grab the boy, but Bai Xiaolu nimbly dodged him—Master Snake’s retina and optic nerves had been burnt out. He had become blind and could not see him at all.

Master Snake wanted to scream, but hot blood was spurting out of his throat, too. He could not utter a single word.

“Ah!”

Bai Xiaolu went behind Uncle Snake and ruthlessly knocked him to the ground. She then pushed the cabinet that was filled with various kinds of equipment to him.

Master Snake did not resist. His struggling became weaker and weaker. The blood and brain matter flowing out of his head congregated into a thick stain.

I made it!

Bai Xiaolu was both in pain and laughing.

But before he could react, Master Snake grabbed his ankle as if his nerves were cramping.

“Ah!”

Bai Xiaolu screamed. She almost fell next to Master Snake.

Master Snake grabbed Li Yao’s ankle with one hand, while his other hand instinctively pulled out the pistol from his boot and fired a random shot.

Knocking on the door.

The door was slammed open.

In Master Snake’s secret chamber, screams were not unusual, but there was definitely something wrong with the gunshots.

The people outside were banging the door and shouting.

What followed was a series of “Da da da da da” sounds, thin and dense, as if molten metal had turned into a torrential rain, raining down from the sky.

Some of them were screaming, some were torn apart before they had the chance to scream, and some of them did not even have the courage or strength to scream.

Bai Xiaolu’s eyes were wide open as she witnessed an inconceivable scene. 20-30 spectators of the Heavenly Wolf Gambling Den who were chasing after her were caught in the storm of bullets. At most, they were wearing ordinary body armor and were completely unable to put up any resistance under the onslaught of the dense storm. In the blink of an eye, they were torn to shreds. Just like the others who were chasing after her for the bounty of a thousand chips, they were mixed into a muddy road of blood.

Murder before interrogation. Judging from the complaints, the broken limbs and body parts lying on the ground did not seem right.

There were many leaders, presidents, and bosses in the Furious Blood Wasteland.

The Heavenly Wolf Gambling Den, the Black Ghost Gang, the Scrap Metal Gang, and even the forces such as ‘Tombstone Town’ or ‘Iron Flower City’ were no match for the ‘Citi Gang’ of Barret, the leader of the Gold Tooth Gang. They were like two-headed bulls versus three mutated lizards. The former could be riddled with holes with a single sneeze, just like what was happening right now.

In the chaotic world, the king of guns was always the king of grass. It was said that the Citibank had occupied a well-equipped military base. Not to mention ordinary firearms, there were even heavy armored vehicles and armed helicopters. Yellow bullets were everywhere. Naturally, they were the dominators of the Furious Blood Wasteland.

The storm of bullets just now had been fired from the armored vehicle. White smoke was still spurting out of the muzzle and mixed with the dust, making it look like a hazy blood mist.

Next to the armored vehicle were two rows of cold, murderous muscular demons.

On their chests and arms, the famous logo of ‘Citi’ was pasted, as if they were serious. They were a hundred times better than the gang of bandits in Tombstone Town. At first glance, they did look like regular soldiers from the underground.

The only difference between them and the regular army was that they did not wear the emblem of ‘Alliance’ or ‘Contract’ on their helmets. Instead, they had painted four capital letters with a grease pen.

“Maga!”

It was their battle cry and their vow. Therefore, the Citibank was also known as the ‘MAGA Gang’ or the ‘Maja Gang’. Whenever the sound of ‘MAGA’ echoed in the Furious Blood Wasteland, the craziest bandits would inevitably shiver in fear.

Bai Xiaolu’s head was dizzy as she thought, They have a military base and are very rich. There is no need for them to plunder such a barren place like Tombstone Town.

Bai Xiaolu saw that the man who had just spoken was bowing to the iron tower under his feet and offering a loudspeaker to her.

Could it be that the person he had accidentally run into was the ‘Overlord’ and ‘Death God’ of the Furious Blood Wasteland, the Gold Tooth Boss, Barete?

Bai Xiaolu felt that she was about to be killed.

But he was not willing to die in confusion. He somehow found the courage to look up.

Through the lackluster sunlight, Bai Xiaolu first saw a pair of coldly shining gold teeth, then a beard that was ready for a fight, and then a face that was full of bumps and hollows and incomplete parts. All the missing parts had been filled with aluminum alloy or even rivets. The two eyeballs had been replaced by red heat-sensitive eyes. On the two sides of the temples, two exaggerated metal horns protruded out, full of unreasonable explosive power.

Between the two horns, on the top of the aluminum skull, was a dirty red hat with four capital letters embroidered in gold threads. It was ‘MAGA’!

Fresh air and clean water. Delicious steaks and cakes. Banquet! Ball! And women! Beautiful women, clean women, normal women, women with two mouths! Chapter 3274: Untitled

None of the people who had survived ten years of nuclear clouds, acid rain, mutant beasts, radiation diseases, and brutal cannibalism were idiots.

Everybody knew that there were two worlds above the ground and below the ground. The nobles and magnates who initiated the nuclear war, after turning the surface of the earth into a wasteland, hid themselves in the underground metropolises with all the wealth that they had exploited and looted. There, they enjoyed life. There was luxury and prosperity. There were even rivers and willow trees. As Boss Gold Tooth had said, everything was available. It was a paradise below the ground!

The descendants of the civilians, on the other hand, suffered from radiation, corrosion, and internal strife in the desert. They were called ‘demons’ and lived in hell under the scorching sun.

Crushing an underground metropolis, plundering its wealth, slaughtering the women who caused the nuclear war, and even burning the underground metropolis to the ground without doing anything to drive out the rich and powerful so that they could have a taste of the miserable life they were living right now… For such a purpose, countless devils were willing to sacrifice their insignificant lives.

The problem was that the underground metropolis where the rich and powerful lived was not something that could be broken through by the insignificant lives of the devils.

Before the nuclear war, the ‘superiors’ had been in control of the most cutting-edge force on the planet. In the collapsed world where order was lost after the nuclear war, they had captured countless ‘experiments’ and conducted all kinds of brutal new weapons research. As it turned out, a lot of super weapons far beyond their time had been developed. The new weapon development systems that were built on piles of bones were known as ‘Armageddon Technology’.

The underground metropolis that had been armed and protected by the ‘Armageddon Technology’ was definitely not something that the automatic rifles of the devil race could breach through.

Moreover, they could not even get close to the entrance of the underground metropolis. Above every underground metropolis was a military zone of thousands of square kilometers. Drones and automated killing machines were patrolling the area nonstop. There were also electromagnetic cannons and prismatic towers buried in the sand and the wasteland. Even the main battle tanks could be burnt to ash. Dealing with the devils was like killing a chicken with a sledgehammer.

Even Barret, the leader of the Citigroup and the leader of the Gold Teeth Brotherhood, wouldn’t be able to break through an underground metropolis even if he led all the devils in the entire Furious Blood Wasteland. Even a seven-year-old would understand the logic.

Therefore, despite Boss Gold Tooth’s thunderous and passionate declaration, the residents of the tombstones were rather indifferent. It was just that nobody dared to question or refute Boss Gold Tooth’s brutality just now.

Even Bai Xiaolu, who was being stepped on by Boss Gold Tooth, didn’t think much of it. She thought to herself, “Is this the level of the legendary Boss Gold Tooth, Barete, who actually wants to break into the underground city with the advantage in numbers? What’s the difference between this and a moth darting into flames?”

The gold-toothed leader grinned. He let go of his right foot that was on Bai Xiaolu’s chest, but he immediately switched to his left foot and changed to a more comfortable position, continuing to bewitch her.

Yes. Under normal circumstances, the devils on the ground would not be able to break into the underground city. He had been the dominator of the Furious Blood Wasteland for more than ten years. He wouldn’t ask his brothers to die for nothing just because he didn’t understand such a simple logic.

However, what if a war broke out between the ‘Underground Clan’ of different forces?

Wars changed almost everything, but they never changed the people who started them.

The only thing that mankind had learned from history was that mankind would never learn any lessons.

It was because ‘learning a lesson’ and ‘learning a lesson’ were often different.

It was the rich and powerful who ‘learned a lesson’.

But it was the filthy, ignorant, inhuman ‘demons’ who learned their lesson.

Therefore, wars never stopped. Wars never changed.

Although only a few decades had passed since the era of global nuclear war, a new war had broken out not long after new forces were born in the broken national and social systems.

Right now, the Underground Clan was divided into two forces—the ‘Alliance’ and the ‘Contract’. They did not have clear racial and national boundaries, but they were engaged in small-scale, low-intensity, but extremely brutal, conflicts in the underground metropolis that looked like countless chess pieces.

Occasionally, when dozens of underground metropolises under the name of ‘Alliance’ or ‘Contract’ were connected, and the tactical communication network between them was finally established, a large-scale battle would be initiated, too, in an attempt to absorb and transform more enemy underground cities.

At this moment, in the northeast of the Furious Blood Wasteland, an “Autumn Offensive” between the Alliance and the Alliance was taking place.

“The name of the underground city is ‘New Gold Mountain’. It is only two days and one night’s journey from Tombstone Town to the northeast. It is one of the last and largest underground metropolises of the ‘Alliance’ in the Furious Blood Wasteland. It is also the main target of the ‘Agreement’!”

Most of the children in the Wasteland knew how to play with knives and guns.

The thick-skinned automatic rifles could still kill people as long as they were close enough, even if they were rusted after decades of hardships.

Even if the bullets were difficult to deal with and they had never actually fired a gun, they would still treat them as toys and carefully study their structure. Moreover, they would receive the teachings of the adults and learn how to aim simply—this was a common experience for all the Children of the Wasteland. It was to the extent that firearms were the only toy for them since childhood.

As long as there were bullets, no child of the Wasteland would say that they could not play with guns.

“Very good. As long as you know how to play with guns, you can play with women. Take down the ‘New Gold Mountain’. There are a lot of women waiting for you to slowly practice and play with. You’re quite lucky, kid. Once you’re on the right path, you can play with real women!”

Boss Gold Tooth did not seem to remember Bai Xiaolu’s name, but it was not important at all. The gloomy redness flooded toward the residents of Tombstone Town. “I’m taking you in. Just follow me. You will get whatever I get. Who else?”

“Crap.”

Bai Xiaolu thought to herself, I’ve made a fool of myself. How can I run away when I’m with Boss Gold Tooth?

“Idiot.”

The elder brother, on the other hand, said, “It’s best to stay by Boss Gold Tooth’s side. It’s obvious that the Citi Gang has come to various towns to capture strong men. Everyone is Boss Gold Tooth’s cannon fodder. I estimate that the first wave will be consumed. Only Boss Gold Tooth’s side is safe.”

“We can figure out a way to run away later. If we can’t even save our own lives, whatever we say will be useless.

Maybe because of Bai Xiaolu’s role model, Boss Gold Tooth shouted a few more times. Finally, almost a hundred residents of Tombstone responded.

Just like Bai Xiaolu, everyone was issued with a rusted automatic rifle and more than ten very valuable magazines. Some of the guys with better arm strength even received two grenades.

Although these pieces of equipment were very tempting, the remaining people were not willing to get involved in such muddy water. They bit their lips and stopped moving.

The leader of the Gold Tooth Gang did not waste any time. He waved his hands, and the team of the Citigroup left Tombstone Town together with the newly recruited soldiers.

Sitting in the back of a military truck, Bai Xiaolu felt that her head was dizzy and she was about to throw up. Suddenly, she heard a few intense explosions behind her. Together with everyone else, she opened the tent of the truck and looked out, only to discover that intense black smoke was burning in the direction of Tombstone Town.

“See? I told you that Boss Gold Tooth wouldn’t let go of the remaining guys so easily.

The elder brother sighed in Bai Xiaolu’s heart and said, “I’m afraid that the public recruitment just now was just a ‘test’. Those who pass the test are barely considered ‘recruits’. Those who fail the test are the shameless slave soldiers who are locked with remote-controlled bombs and the lowest cannon fodder who are forced to the front. It is inevitable.”

Bai Xiaolu looked at the burning Tombstone Town in silence for a long time.

“What about the village? What about my sister?”

Although he did not like the stink of Tombstone, it was his hometown after all. Black flames were raging in the lead clouds, making him feel lonely and insignificant for the first time.

“The village is far away from Tombstone, and it is hidden in the hills. The Citibank might not be able to find it. Considering the urgency of the war, I don’t think they will waste much of their time on a small village without any resources.

His brother said, “As for the future… Just leave it to fate!”

…

Bai Xiaolu could only resign herself to fate.

Over the next few days, they went to a few other large towns nearby that boasted a large population. In the same way, they were ‘recruited’ at a high price before they were coerced by force. Even Bai Xiaolu, the ‘little brat who hasn’t even had her hair fully grown’, was treated as a walking advertisement by Boss Gold Tooth to mock the timidness of the people. Just like that, a team of more than ten thousand people was gathered.

After the incident with ‘Master Snake’, Bai Xiaolu had been traumatized. She was extremely scared and wary of the legendary brutal and notorious Boss Gold Tooth. But thankfully, he was probably not a fan of the arts. Besides, before the war, he had been occupied with military affairs and did not have much time to deal with Bai Xiaolu. Therefore, he had asked Bai Xiaolu to learn some basic operations on the battlefield from one of his guards and beat her brutally. In order to save her own life, Bai Xiaolu had learned a lot.

However, one day, Boss Gold Tooth learned from the recruits of Tombstone Town that Bai Xiaolu had killed ‘Master Snake’, the big boss of the Heaven Wolves Casino. He was quite interested and asked Bai Xiaolu about it during dinner.

Naturally, Bai Xiaolu was not willing to tell her brother’s secret. She simply said that she had taken the opportunity when ‘Master Snake’ was unprepared to draw the pistol from his waist and blow up his head. Then, she crawled out of the ventilation window with her thin body, but she did not mention the details.

‘Master Snake’ was famous in the vicinity of Tombstone Town, but it was nothing in the ears of a brutal demon such as Boss Gold Tooth. He was too lazy to pursue the details and simply opened his hands and patted Bai Xiaolu’s shoulder heavily. “Good boy. You’ve got guts. I knew that I was not wrong about you. After the battle, if you are still alive, I will chop off another five heads and let you join the Citibank Gang!”

Bai Xiaolu grimaced because of the slap. Looking at Boss Gold Tooth’s ugly face that was full of patches, she felt that it was not as scary as before, and the stinky smell on her body seemed to be much thinner.

…

Three days later, they finally reached the top of the ‘New Gold Mountain’, the underground metropolis of the ‘Alliance’.

They were delayed for so many days because new teams of the demon race were joining them.

As it turned out, the capability of the Citigroup was much higher than what Bai Xiaolu had seen in Tombstone Town. They had sent dozens of ‘armed recruitment teams’ to almost every populated town in the Furious Blood Wasteland and gathered countless ‘recruits’.

Dozens of bandit gangs, which were notorious for their crimes in the first place, had come to join them, too, hoping to break the ‘New Gold Mountain’ together with the Citigroup.

It was not until Bai Xiaolu saw so many bizarre-shaped devils that she realized how vast the world was. There were so many distinctive towns and tribes in the entire Furious Blood Wasteland.

Some of the gang members were wearing poisonous armor made of the shells of giant scorpions, which made them look like giant scorpions that were standing on their feet.

Some tribes were covered in feathers from head to toe without leaving any gaps. It was said that the feathers could resist the severe radiation.

There was another town where all the residents’ right arms had deformed and expanded, taking up almost half of their body. It was extremely difficult for them to walk. However, Boss Gold Tooth had said that they were natural-born experts in grenade-throwing. It would be best if they could throw a bundle of grenades hundreds of meters away and attack the city.

Of course, Bai Xiaolu also saw countless slaves who were forced to come.

They were often dressed in ragged clothes and had sad faces. Their necks were tied with chains, and remote-controlled bombs were hanging next to the chains. Three to five of them were locked together. They could only move forward and not retreat. If they moved forward, there was still a chance of survival. If they retreated, their heads and those of their companions would fly into the air. They were simply a bunch of walking corpses.

Some of them were residents of Tombstone Town.

Even those who were lucky enough not to be killed were watching.

Seeing their ending, Bai Xiaolu finally realized how wise her brother had been when he asked her to sign up. One mistake was the difference between heaven and hell.

Even though this ‘heaven’ was just one level above hell.

This was the northeast part of the Furious Blood Wasteland. It was literally hell.

The Furious Blood Wasteland was a death zone made of red deserts and deserts.

After more than half a month of carpet bombing, the top of the mountain had been razed to the ground. The plants that had been struggling on the ground had all been blown to smithereens. The only thing left were shocking craters that were reflecting colorful lights to record the extreme temperature of the explosion.

Dozens of black, bottomless oval craters were particularly eye-catching.

Boss Gold Tooth told Bai Xiaolu that it was the damage caused by the underground bomb.

In order to facilitate the charge of the infantry, the bombs did not fall to the ground vertically. Instead, they were hit into the ground at an oblique angle. Once they exploded, a natural slope would be formed, through which the soldiers of the demon race could rush in even with their bare feet.

BOOM!

BOOM!

BOOM!

Every five minutes, there would be an earsplitting shriek in the air, followed by an earsplitting explosion. A fireball would gradually rise above ‘New Gold Mountain’ and turn into raging black clouds.

It was a train cannon from very far away. It was said that a single cannon was hundreds of meters long and had a shooting range that could cover the entire Furious Blood Wasteland. It would be bombarded every five minutes. Although it wouldn’t cause too much damage, it would cause the entire “New Gold Mountain” to feel an intense shock, greatly affecting the spirit of the defenders.

This further proved his brother’s judgement—Train cannons were definitely not something that the Citibank Gang could have. They must be standard equipment for the ‘contract’.

At two o’clock in the afternoon, everyone, including the slave soldiers, received a very suspicious pill, claiming that it could prevent venomous gas and radiation.

Most of them swallowed the food. Bai Xiaolu, on the other hand, hid her tongue under her mouth and vomited it in secret when she went to the toilet. When she came back and looked at everybody again, she seemed to have lost her mind.

At 2:30 p.m., after the last bombardment, the smoke turned crimson.

It was the general attack command issued by the ‘contract’.

“Kill them all!”

Standing on the armored vehicle, Boss Gold Tooth shouted desperately, “Take down ‘New Gold Mountain’, grab water, grab food, grab women!”

What was going on?

I—I can’t control my hands at all!

For the first time, he discovered that the days when he was not writing were so empty and lonely. It was like an addictive withdrawal reaction!

What do I do? I actually finished such a good novel like 40,000 Years of Cultivation. I won’t be able to write it in the future. Oh heavens, oh earth, what should I do~~~

Chapter 3276: Untitled

It was said that a wise man said something a hundred years ago.

“I don’t know what weapons will be used in the third world war, but I do know that the fourth world war will be fought in the collision of stone axes and wooden sticks.

It was a perfect description for the battlefield today.

The hundreds of thousands of mobs that were temporarily assembled had never received formal training for the charge of a large army except for hunting and killing. The only tactics that they could employ were the tactics of numbers and sieging.

The only thing that Boss Gold Tooth could tell the mobs was to lengthen the battle line and disperse the infantry a little bit so that they wouldn’t crowd together like a bunch of stupid army ants. During the First World War, the strategy of charging together had proved to be the most efficient suicide.

Therefore, they extended the battle line to the maximum and charged at the ‘New Gold Mountain’ from almost every direction.

Although most of the slaves and new soldiers were inexperienced, they were not entirely on foot. Many of them were riding civilian vehicles that had been installed with steel plates, and some of them were even riding bizarre-shaped motorcycles. They were sort of a ‘motorized’ troop.

Although it was useless to add seven or eight layers of steel plates to the advanced apocalyptic weapons, such ‘tight’ protection at least gave the devils a false sense of security and made their charge faster, more determined, and even manic.

For a moment, the depths of the Wasteland were filled with the howls of humans that were even more savage than those of wild beasts. Thousands upon thousands of civilian vehicles raised billowing smoke and dust, as if a bloody jungle had suddenly appeared in the desert.

Bai Xiaolu had once received something called ‘DVD’ from a traveling merchant. She had watched a lot of movies about life before the war, including the ancient war scenes from a hundred years ago.

He had thought that a real war would be one where thousands of cannons were firing and everything was burning.

However, the ‘New Gold Mountain’ in front of them was lifeless. When the first wave of slave soldiers and new soldiers reached the entrance of the underground bomb, there was still no response, as if it were a ghost town.

It was not until a lot of slave soldiers and new soldiers rushed into the underground along the slope that a lot of humming things flew out.

It was a drone.

In the Wasteland, drones were synonymous with death. Once they were targeted, absolutely no one could escape.

As it turned out, the drones danced and landed in the places where the attackers were densest, before they exploded into raging electric arcs that covered a hundred square meters.

The aggressors seemed to have been whipped by a cold whip.

Everyone fell to the ground, tied up by the magnificent blue electric arcs.

They still made no sound.

There was not even smoke or blood.

It was like an absurd, unbelievable dream.

But the lives of countless people had been reaped so quietly and casually.

Such a scene should have scared the attackers, but most of them had swallowed the pills and burnt the nerve in their brain that was responsible for ‘fear’. They were still rushing forward against the electric arcs and dying in the blue brightness.

Bai Xiaolu clenched her fists and glanced at Boss Gold Tooth, who was standing on an armored vehicle not far away.

He found that Boss Gold Tooth was as calm as usual and even yawned, as if it did not matter.

Bai Xiaolu finally understood.

The new soldiers on the attacking side and the drones on the defending side were both expendable.

If he had not been lucky enough to follow Boss Gold Tooth, he would have found him among the dead bodies, too.

In just half an hour.

Tens of thousands of slave soldiers and new soldiers were almost exhausted.

They hadn’t even seen a single shadow of the defenders.

How was he going to fight the war?

Right when she was feeling restless, Bai Xiaolu suddenly heard a series of dense shrieks that were even sharper, faster, and more ear-piercing than the train cannon.

When he raised his head, he saw hundreds of white lines sweeping across the sky and tearing apart the lead-gray clouds. They drew a sharp curve and marched into the cave above the ‘New Gold Mountain’ as if they had eyes.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Thunderous noises echoed below the ground. The ground above the ‘New Gold Mountain’ protruded out. Endless flames spurted hundreds of meters out of the hole, together with a lot of burnt body parts and twisted debris.

But he was not tied up by his emotions for long. Licking his lips, he spat out a mouthful of phlegm and inserted a piece of blood-stained paper into the tank.

All the members of the Citigroup were crazy.

He was not even sitting in the tank, but sitting astride the rapid-fire cannon of the tank, holding the blood-stained Citi in his hand, and shouting the battle hymn of ‘the road to the countryside, bring me home’. He was laughing and uttering weird noises, as if he were a fearless cowboy who did not care whether new drones could fly out of the ground or not.

Even Bai Xiaolu had to sit in the armored vehicle closest to him. She held her head and shivered, fearing that she might be killed together with Boss Gold Tooth.

However, they finally rushed into the underground without encountering any obstacles. The precision guided missile bombardment just now had indeed crushed the defense points at the periphery of ‘New Gold Mountain’ into powder. Once they entered the chaotic street fighting in the downtown area, the damage of the high-precision weapons of the underground would be minimized, and the advantage of numbers of the devils would be maximized.

They sang and rained bullets on the burnt bodies while they charged forward along the rugged slopes. Soon, they reached the ‘New Gold Mountain’ from dozens of different slopes.

The delicate and prosperous underground metropolis before their eyes made all the devils unable to believe their eyes.

Before Bai Xiaolu could see the scenery outside, she was hit in the head by the refreshing air. She was seeing stars and crying.

“So there is such a nice-smelling air in the world.”

Bai Xiaolu’s eyes were brimming with tears. “There is no stink, no smell of rust, no sourness of acid rain, and no sweetness of radiation. It is the air. The purest air!”

He was somewhat drunk.

The soldiers around him were the same.

Some of them were even crying uncontrollably.

All the devils had imagined the underground metropolis countless times—decorating the ‘paradise’ with the most splendid and extravagant scenes that they could imagine.

But the real underground metropolis was hundreds of times more splendid and extravagant than they had imagined.

Not to mention the rows upon rows of automatically glowing, futuristic skyscrapers.

Artificial gardens that could be found everywhere in the city.

Bewildering mist was spurting out of the interconnected tubes above their heads from time to time, which was a waste of the clean water that the devils in the desert valued as much as their own life. It was all for the purpose of bringing an insignificant chill to the underground world.

Such extravagance!

Such absurdity!

Of course, at the cost of the lives of the soldiers, they will either die or be scrapped after a battle!”

As for whether the siege succeeds or not, nobody cares whether we live or die!”

Chapter 3278: Untitled

The sudden appearance of a large batch of magnetic explosive infantry caught everyone off guard.

Bai Xiaolu had seen with her own eyes that Boss Gold Tooth, with the Vulcan Machine Gun on his shoulder, had rushed to the frontline of the magnetic explosion infantry with earsplitting roars.
