The mobs attacking ‘New Gold Mountain’ had been motivated by greed and victory in the first place, which was why they were marching forward without caring about anything else. The toughness of the magnetic explosive infantry was enough to calm them down. Fear occupied every nerve in their body. Some of them were shouting, and some were fleeing. The situation of the battle had suddenly reversed.

It seemed that his brother was right. Boss Gold Tooth did not know that a magnetic explosion infantry factory was hiding in the depths of ‘New Gold Mountain’. Otherwise, he wouldn’t have resorted to such a stupid strategy to exhaust the subordinates that he had accumulated for more than ten years.

The ‘contract’ did not tell him all the intelligence. After all, he was just a demon and a dog of the ‘contract’.

But the strength of almost a hundred soldiers was not enough to reverse the collapse of the city.

Besides, the defenders did not know how many soldiers were on the attacking side, nor did they know whether or not there were regular troops behind them who were preparing to reap the benefits.

Therefore, after the magnetic explosive infantry broke through a gap, a large number of tanks, infantry tanks, and power armors flooded out like a tide, protecting the old, the weak, the women, and the children and breaking through the siege.

The ‘New Gold Mountain’ was doomed to fail. The ecological circulation system of the entire city had been broken. Even if the attackers were wiped out, it would still be difficult for the naturally delicate underground people to survive in such a harsh environment. It was better to move to another underground city while they still had the strength.

These days, although human lives were not worth much, healthy adults who had not been seriously contaminated by radiation and did not have the slightest deformity or mutation were still very popular. Living under someone else’s roof in other underground metropolises, even if they were slaves or soldiers, was certainly not as good as living in their own home, but it was also the least bad choice when they had no other options.

The situation immediately turned into one of fear. Boss Gold Tooth did not want to risk his assets, and the defenders did not want to waste too much of their strength during the break-out. However, since the two parties were locked in a fierce battle, how could they stop at the same time? Together with the fleeing bandits and the new soldiers, the collapsing underground rock stratums, and the exploding gas tubes, the scene could not have been more chaotic.

But it was none of Bai Xiaolu’s business.

The moment Boss Gold Tooth disappeared in the ocean of blue electric arcs, he had turned around and run away under the command of his brother.

It was a pity that his luck seemed to have run out when he was picking up the trophies. Not long after he ran, he encountered a serious explosion of gas pipes. The entire road was blocked, and even he was almost swallowed by the flames.

Spitting out a mouthful of blood and dust, he took a few detours, only to run into the fleeing soldiers, the teams that were trying to break out of the siege, or the collapsing rocks that almost smashed him into a meat pie.

The sounds of battle behind him were getting louder and louder. The battle zone was moving toward him.

Bai Xiaolu was so anxious that she hated herself for not growing two more legs in the radiation mutation.

“Wait—”

His brother suddenly stopped him. “Little Lu, look at the center of the city. A lot of rock stratums are collapsing. It won’t be long before the underground metropolis is completely destroyed!”

“So what?

Bai Xiaolu did not understand why her brother was talking about that all of a sudden. After more than half a month of bombardment and the fierce battle inside the city, it was perfectly normal that the rock stratums above the city collapsed because of the overwhelming pressure.

“I know that both the ‘Alliance’ and the ‘Contract’ are still in the early stages of development. The residents of every underground metropolis are not necessarily connected to each other. That is to say, the identity data of the residents of ‘New Gold Mountain’ is stored in one of the databases in this place at best. When the city collapses, it is very likely to be destroyed!

The brother pondered for a moment and said, “Little Lu, find the body of a resident of ‘New Gold Mountain’ who is similar to us. Take off his identity bracelet, his identity card, and put on his clothes!”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed for a moment. She immediately understood what her brother meant.

“Are we going to sneak into the ‘New Gold Mountain’ and escape?”

He asked while he groped the body.

“Yes. Right now, it seems that the defenders are more likely to break out of the siege successfully. Besides, their organization will not be very tight. In the chaos, nobody will care who we are. By then, it will be much easier for us to get out of the siege team than to run away from Boss Gold Tooth.”

His brother explained.

It took them ten minutes to find a dead body of a resident with a similar body size. The uniform on the dead body seemed to be the uniform of the ‘New Gold Mountain’, with distinctive features.

Although he did not find the identity card, he still had the wristband. The best part was that the wristband happened to be broken by a bullet. Now, even if he could not read the information inside the wristband, he could still retrieve the pictures and other information.

However, their joy turned to sorrow. Just as they changed into the student uniform of ‘New Gold Mountain’ and put on the broken identification wristband, they suddenly heard a familiar roar coming from nearby. It was Boss Gold Tooth.

He was not dead yet!

At this moment, Boss Gold Tooth seemed to have just taken a hot bath in magma. Half of the metal horn on his head was missing, and his entire body was blackened. The Vulcan Machine Gun was gone, but he was miraculously wearing a plasma cannon on his arm. Faced with three soldiers of the Mystic Skeleton Battlesuit, he was fearless like a god of war.

When Bai Xiaolu saw him, he also saw Bai Xiaolu and the boy’s ‘school uniform’.

The gold-tooth leader’s eyes bulged. He immediately understood Bai Xiaolu’s plan.

Hum! Hum! Hum!

Bai Xiaolu had a splitting headache. A devil seemed to be whispering in his ear, asking him to help Boss Gold Tooth.

“Let’s go!”

His brother also activated his own ‘ability’ to fight against the ‘legion’ of Boss Gold Tooth. Bai Xiaolu was immediately back to herself and ran toward the slope of the exit.

“Huh?”

Boss Gold Tooth shouted not far away. Obviously, the collision of the two brainwaves had made him realize a shocking fact—Bai Xiaolu was also an ‘ability user’!

Because of the encounter with Boss Gold Tooth, Bai Xiaolu lost the opportunity to sneak into the team.

He had just escaped from the underground through a slope when he heard the roars of armored vehicles behind him. He turned around and discovered that it was a team of scouts from the Citibank, including the instructors who had been training him over the past few days.

There was no doubt that Boss Gold Tooth had asked them to chase after him.

Of course.

Every ‘ability user’ was as rare as phoenix feathers and unicorn horns. They were priceless existences. Whether it was roping them in, selling them, or conducting live experiments, they could be exchanged for a hundred times the profit. Now that Boss Gold Tooth had seen through his identity as an ‘ability user’, how could he let them go so easily?

“Get on!”

His brother stimulated Bai Xiaolu’s optic nerves and asked him to pay attention to a motorbike lying on the ground next to him, which had been left by the first wave of attack.

In order to survive in the boundless Ardent Blood Wasteland, learning how to drive a vehicle was as important as learning how to hunt. Even if there were no heavy armored trucks in the barren mountain village where they grew up, there were still a few motorcycles.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Thankfully, the motorbike was not damaged by the explosion of the electric arcs just now. Bai Xiaolu rushed out like an arrow released from a bow.

At this moment, a lot of soldiers and break-outs rushed out of the underground.

Some of them were trying to run away, while some others were setting fire to the sky of the desert.

“Where are we going to run to?”

Bai Xiaolu’s eyes turned red when she saw the armored vehicle drawing closer and closer.

The enemy was also spraying bullets around him, picking up countless rocks and slapping his face.

Although she knew that Boss Gold Tooth must have ordered his subordinates to “capture him alive”, Bai Xiaolu was also clear that there were countless methods in the Wasteland called “living hell”.

“Over there!”

His brother asked Bai Xiaolu to turn around and see the black dust storm with electric arcs in the north.

“Thunderstorm!”

Bai Xiaolu gasped. “We ran into a thunderstorm?”

“That’s right.”

His brother smiled. “We were so lucky to run into a thunderstorm.”

Most of the runners, pursuers, and soldiers were shocked by the thunderstorm and hurried to change their direction, even though it was already too late.

But Bai Xiaolu stepped on her motorbike and rushed into the thunderstorm.

BOOM! CRACK!

Thunder rumbled, sand was flying, and rain was pouring. All the extreme weather conditions were interconnecting. For a moment, Bai Xiaolu thought that she was dead and being tortured in the deepest part of hell.

He also saw countless people torn apart by lightning, swallowed by tornadoes, and riddled with holes by the corrosive acid rain.

With her last bit of strength, Bai Xiaolu pressed the two large backpacks on her chest and back.

It was the hope of his sister and the entire village. He would not let go of it even if he had to go to the eighteenth level of hell!

…

An unknown amount of time passed.

There was no telling whether he was blown into hell or heaven by the storm.

Bai Xiaolu struggled to crawl out of the boundless red sand.

Licking his dry lips, he grimaced at the taste of the blood that was mixed with bitterness and sweetness. The two hard packages next to him made him chuckle, too.

The thunderstorm had died down.

He was not dead yet. The item was still there.

Sister, there is still hope for the village.

Then, he saw that a black muzzle was aimed at his eyebrow not far away.

Bai Xiaolu’s smile immediately froze.

“Brother?”

He shouted tentatively.

“I’m here.”

His brother’s voice was very solemn. “I’m too weak to kill him immediately.

“Then—then what do we do?”

Bai Xiaolu was desperate.

The gunman who was holding the gun and aiming at him from seven to eight meters away was a young man around his age. He was also wearing the ragged uniform of a “New Gold Mountain” student. Maybe because he had received an elite education in the underground world and had sufficient nutrition, the young man was apparently much stronger than him. His eyeballs were slightly yellowish-brown, and his eyes were sharp and deep like those of a lizard.

The young man glanced at Bai Xiaolu’s face, uniform, and wristband.

Then, he passed by the two backpacks beside Bai Xiaolu.

“Escaped yesterday?”

The young man asked. His voice was coarse, but it was quite charming.

Bai Xiaolu was dazed for a moment and nodded quickly.

“We are all on the same side. How about I take half of the food and water?”

The young man asked, which was considered ‘courteous’ under such circumstances.

Faced with a gun, there was nothing Bai Xiaolu could do except nod her head.

The young man whistled. He finally put down his gun and forced a smile.

“Let’s get to know each other. My father is the laboratory supervisor of ‘Gold Mountain Clean Water Company’.”

The young man took out an identity card from his pocket and snapped it toward Bai Xiaolu to prove his identity. “My name is ‘Thousand Treasures Sea’.”

—

There was no need to say anything else. How delicious!

Chapter 3279: Untitled

The identity card had been burnt by the flames. The laser-printed photo had turned into a cluster of dark wrinkles, making it impossible to see what it looked like.

However, the name next to it was indeed ‘Thousand Treasures Sea’.

Naturally, Bai Xiaolu could not take out her identity card. She could only raise her identity bracelet and pretend that she was weak and in pain. She mumbled, “My—my name is Bai Xiaolu. I was separated from you when I escaped yesterday. I only remember the explosion, the fire, the blast, and the blood. It hurts. My head hurts. I don’t remember anything!”

“Yes. It was a terrible escape.”

The young man named ‘Wan Zanghai’ sighed. He seemed to believe Bai Xiaolu’s words, but the gun aimed at the boy did not move at all. “Do you have any weapons on you or in your backpack?”

There had been a pistol on Bai Xiaolu’s waist, but it had been lost in the thunderstorm.

There were still two folding automatic rifles and a dozen or so magazines of bullets in his backpack. He was unable to take them out in a short amount of time. However, the muzzle and the grip of the gun were protruding out of his backpack. Even a blind man would be able to see it. It would not be good to lie, so he could only force a nod.

“Very good.”

The smile on Wan Zanghai’s face grew thicker and more unpredictable. His voice became more and more refined as he said, “Student Bai, we are all on the same side. At first, we were attacked by the devils, and then we were swept into a thunderstorm for a day and a night. Even so, we were lucky enough to survive and still meet each other. It can be seen that we are destined for each other. We should be walking side by side in this wretched desert. Don’t worry, I will absolutely not abandon you.

“However, you are not in a good mental state right now. It is too dangerous to hold a weapon. You might hurt yourself or other people. Therefore, your weapons are all in my custody. I will return them to you when necessary. Is that okay?”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed for a moment.

In the Wasteland, a weapon filled with bullets was as important as a heart. No one would hand it over to others easily.

“Huh?”

Wan Canghai shook the muzzle of his gun and narrowed his eyes. His tone turned gloomy as he said, “Little brother, firearms are dangerous things. Not everyone can play with them. With our school’s firearms education, it will be good enough if you can learn where the trigger and safety are. Do you really think that you are not yourself just because you have a gun?”

Bai Xiaolu hesitated for a moment, but her brother said in his heart, “Give him the gun.”

“What?”

Bai Xiaolu exclaimed, “Brother, guns are your life in the desert!”

“It doesn’t matter.”

His brother smiled. “I’ve recovered. I can activate it at least once.”

Bai Xiaolu was greatly relieved.

His brother was his biggest reliance. No matter how strong and cunning his opponent was, he was just a teenager. He wouldn’t be more difficult to deal with than ‘Master Snake’, would he? His brother could blow up his opponent’s cerebrovascular vessels at lightning speed without alarming him. There was no way that his opponent would have the opportunity to pull the trigger.

“Alright.”

Bai Xiaolu nodded. She pretended that she was at a loss and stammered, “Then—then I will teach it to Brother Wan.”

He was about to open the military backpack when Wan Zanghai said, “Wait. Don’t move. Leave the two backpacks where they are and walk ten meters away with your back facing me. Let me remind you that this is a boundless desert. Deadly quicksand, sandworms, Wasteland Wolves, landmines, terrifying robots, and Wasteland Gangs are everywhere. There are also deadly acid rain and radiation zones. Without food, supplies, and radiation detectors, no one will be able to escape. You must not do anything stupid.”

Bai Xiaolu was clearer than Wan Zanghai about the formidability of the Wasteland, especially the uninhabited area in the north of the Wasteland. He did not play any tricks and obediently left his backpack where he was. He walked ten meters away with his back facing Wan Zanghai.

His brother, on the other hand, focused all his attention on the young man behind him.

Crack. Crack.

It was the sound of a folded automatic rifle being loaded.

“Hehehehe!”

Then came Wan Zanghai’s laughter.

The young man’s laughter sounded like that of a lizard.

Although Bai Xiaolu was well aware that lizards wouldn’t smile at all, not even the double-headed venomous lizards that had undergone radiation mutations could.

“Alright, turn around.”

When Bai Xiaolu heard Wan Zanghai’s words, she turned around and discovered that the resources that he had painstakingly gathered were scattered all over the ground.

The young man was sitting cross-legged with one rifle across his legs. He was adjusting the scope of the other rifle, while clicking sounds were echoing from his mouth from time to time.

Then, Wan Canghai threw his shotgun to Bai Xiaolu and it fell to the boy’s feet. “This one is yours!”

Bai Xiaolu was overjoyed. She picked up the shotgun immediately, only to find that something was wrong. She opened the magazine and found that it was empty. There were no bullets at all. Besides, the trigger and the barrel of the gun were somewhat crooked, suggesting that the gun had been seriously burnt and electrocuted. Even if there were bullets, they might not be able to fire.

“Hehe. I’m sorry. I forgot that the gun had no bullets.”

Wan Zanghai aimed at Bai Xiaolu with the automatic rifle. His voice turned extremely cold as he said, “But there must be one in this one.”

Bai Xiaolu’s heart turned cold.

But his brother’s head was burning.

But Wan Zanghai only aimed at him for half a second before he moved the gun away. An innocent smile blossomed on his face, which had been cold and cruel a moment ago. “Ha. Just kidding. Look at how scared you are right now. You are too timid. How did you escape from the siege yesterday?”

It was just a joke…

Bai Xiaolu cursed a hundred times in her heart.

He had thought that only the devils in the Furious Blood Wasteland, especially the ferocious ones such as ‘Master Snake’, ‘Butcher’, and ‘Boss Gold Tooth’, would be so insane.

He did not expect that a teenager from the underground world could be so bad.

“However, you should understand now…”

Wan Canghai’s smile only lasted three seconds before it was entirely gone. He stared at Bai Xiaolu and said, “You should’ve listened to somebody, right?”

“Understood. Understood.”

Bai Xiaolu pretended to be timid and said, “You should’ve listened to Brother Wan.”

“Excellent. Come and eat!”

Wan Zanghai’s smile was like a mysterious mask that could be taken out of one’s face instantly. He gestured to Bai Xiaolu. “Let’s eat and drink so that we can be on our way!”

Bai Xiaolu focused her attention and sat down in front of Wan Zanghai, trembling in fear. She watched him rip open the colorful wrappers one by one and take out a folded container made of tinfoil. He broke off a small piece from a gray-brown solid that seemed to be a ‘compressed biscuit’ and put it into the container. Then he blew a few sparks on the solid with what seemed to be an electronic lighter.

After a ‘Bo’ sound, the solid suddenly expanded into a big bowl of clean water!

“Well—”

Bai Xiaolu’s eyes bulged. She did not know that there was such a marvelous thing in the world. She had thought that she was collecting compressed biscuits, but it turned out to be compressed water! A cluster of solid objects that was smaller than a fingernail could produce such a large bowl of clean water. It was truly unbelievable!

How many treasures did the underground people have? How many lives could be saved if the treasures were passed on to the surface?

Thankfully, he didn’t know how to use them until he ran into Wan Zanghai. Otherwise, he might’ve become a laughing stock and even killed somebody!

Wan Canghai divided the clean water into two halves, soaked the real compressed biscuits in them, and gave Bai Xiaolu a few high-energy sugar pills.

Bai Xiaolu secretly heaved a sigh of relief and swallowed it just like Wan Canghai did. She thought to herself that it was a good thing that Wan Canghai did not ask her to prepare food and water. Otherwise, she would have been exposed.

After eating and drinking to his heart’s content, Wan Canghai’s physical strength was somewhat restored. He studied the compass carefully, only to find that the pointer was shaking and spinning nonstop.

“Damn.”

The young man mumbled, “It is indeed as the textbooks say. The thunderstorm has just passed, and the magnetic field interference is very strong. All kinds of advanced automatic equipment are unusable. Even the compass has been seriously jammed. There is no way to tell which direction is which.”

He narrowed his eyes and raised his head to look at the sky. The sky was as dense as before, as if it would collapse at any moment. There was no sun at all except for a few bleeding clouds in the horizon.

“Hey, do you think that’s the sun?”

Wan Zanghai kicked Bai Xiaolu.

Bai Xiaolu observed for a while and shook her head. “I don’t know.”

“True.”

Wan Canghai grinned. “The sky has been covered in lead clouds for decades, and we’ve been living underground all the time. We’ve never seen the real sun except in our computers. How do you know?”

“If that’s the case, we can only try our luck and pretend that it is! In any case, we can’t just sit here and wait to be killed. The devils attacking the city are not entirely wiped out. There are still countless scattered soldiers and bandit gangs active in the Wasteland. If we are captured by them, the two tender-skinned underground dwellers will be doomed!”

“Let’s go. If the sky is bleeding toward the sun, this should be the west. Let’s go west. Didn’t we agree yesterday that we would meet the reinforcements of the ‘alliance’ if we went west?”

Bai Xiaolu said obediently, “I will listen to Brother Wan.”

The two of them packed up the supplies on the ground. Wan Zanghai, on the other hand, did not watch Bai Xiaolu work alone. He even helped her share a bag. Together with the weight of the two automatic rifles, it was still him who carried the heavier load.

Moreover, he had taken the initiative to walk in front and face unknown dangers.

It confused Bai Xiaolu. Was the ‘Myriad Hidden Sea’ a good thing or a bad one? If he had ill intentions, he could’ve shot the two fully loaded automatic rifles and taken all the assets for himself.

He had the advantage and was willing to team up with Bai Xiaolu. Did it mean that he really considered Bai Xiaolu to be one of his own?

“Don’t trust him too much.

His brother replied casually, “If I were him, I wouldn’t shoot him, either—but that doesn’t mean I’m friendly.”

“Why?”

Bai Xiaolu did not understand.

“Firstly, there are too many heavy assets here. We don’t have any vehicles. We will collapse if we walk in the desert with only two bags of assets.

The elder brother analyzed, “With our age and physical strength, one package is already the best we can do on foot. Even if he kills us, he will only be able to take away one package of supplies. If he lets us go for now, he will be able to get a grateful, unsuspecting mule. If we help him carry one more package, he will be able to earn half more even if we consume half of the supplies.”

Bai Xiaolu exclaimed in her heart.

It was always like this. The biggest problem for him could be solved by his brother instantly.

“Secondly, and more importantly, we are very important assets in the first place, which means a piece of fresh meat.

His brother sneered, “If necessary, he could’ve killed us and harvested dozens of kilograms of meat. It wouldn’t have been a problem for him to survive for three to five days. The guy is apparently very smart. Why would he waste such a good piece of meat?”

Chapter 3280: Untitled

“What?”

Bai Xiaolu found it hard to believe.

It was not because he could not believe that there was such a thing as cannibalism in the world, but because he could not believe that the Underground Clan could eat humans, too.

“Of course, they will eat people, and they will eat more elegantly and cleverly than the devils in Tombstone Town such as ‘Butchers’ and ‘Master Snake’.

His brother sneered, “If they were not better at eating human beings than we are, how could they enjoy themselves in the underground shelters while we have to suffer under the scorching sun, radiation, and the threat of mutated beasts?

Bai Xiaolu was silent. She looked at Wan Zanghai, who was walking in the front with his shoulders bouncing up and down, and felt that he was more like a lizard than ever.

“However, this is good.

His brother replied unhurriedly, “He treats us as ‘lice’ and ‘sheep’, and so do we. He can share half of our weight. If necessary, we can even kill him and cut his flesh, which will significantly increase our odds of survival. Therefore, we are not in a hurry to take action.”

“We—”

Bai Xiaolu was confused. “Do they eat people, too?”

“When necessary, of course.”

The elder brother said, “This is a world of cannibalism. Don’t you want to return to the village alive to save your sister and the children in the village?”

Although his sister and the children in the village were ugly, their young faces appeared in Bai Xiaolu’s mind.

He regained his strength and staggered between the desert with the heavy bag.

The boy and the young man walked toward what they thought was the west for a day and a night.

During the time, Wan Canghai had tried to talk to Bai Xiaolu multiple times, asking her about his family and his life in the ‘New Gold Mountain’. However, Bai Xiaolu had asked him to leave.

Wan Zanghai did not seem to be suspicious. Or rather, for the ‘lice’ and ‘sheep’ in his eyes, their real identities did not matter at all, as long as they could carry and eat. After all, the guns and the bullets were in Wan Zanghai’s hands, and he had everything under control.

Maybe because he was in a good mood, Wan Zanghai talked a lot about his family and his school, which gave Bai Xiaolu a better understanding of the ‘New Gold Mountain’. Of course, Bai Xiaolu had never heard most of what Wan Zanghai said. She was dumbfounded and thought that it was the life of a deity.

After one day and one night, their physical strength had reached the limit, and they were on the verge of a mental breakdown.

Although he was replenishing his food and water, he still felt that he was about to collapse. Faced with the boundless desert, he was more desperate than ever.

“Damn it. We must’ve gone the wrong way.”

Wan Canghai mumbled, “If this is really the west, we’ve been walking for a day and a night, and we should’ve seen the unmanned automated sentry posts and the ‘watch cannons’ of the Alliance no matter what. But right now, there is nothing. Nothing!”

It was impossible to determine the direction by the sun.

Bloody brilliance was spreading out on the edge of the lead clouds in all directions. Silver city remains could even be seen floating in the clouds.

It was a mirage, a very common phenomenon in the Furious Blood Wasteland.

Maybe, a day ago, they were misled by the mirage and went in the opposite direction, which was the uninhabited area in the north of the desert.

The way Wan Canghai looked at Bai Xiaolu was getting weirder and weirder.

He was estimating the most precise ‘input-out ratio’ between the consumption of water and food and the amount of supplies that Bai Xiaolu could carry. He was calculating when he should kill Bai Xiaolu and cut off her flesh.

His brother was also prepared. He was ready to launch a small attack at the cerebrovascular vessels of Wan Zanghai.

But right then, the boy and the young man heard rumbling noises behind the sand dunes ahead of them.

“Someone’s here?”

Wan Canghai hurried to drag Bai Xiaolu to the ground and gestured for him to hide. “Ha. It’s the sound of an engine and a car!”

In the boundless Wasteland, vehicles and gasoline were even more precious than life and blood.

It was impossible to walk dozens of kilometers on foot in one day, but everything would be different with a car.

“We have to get a car.”

Wan Canghai’s eyes were immediately bloodshot. He shushed Bai Xiaolu and gestured for her to climb the sand dune with him.

In the desert ahead, a fierce battle was taking place.

More than ten motorcycles that were embedded with sharp thorns and three off-road vehicles that had been welded with bulletproof plates circled around and attacked only one target.

The motorbikes and the off-road vehicles were all decorated in the style of the devil race. Naturally, the passengers on the vehicles were not regular soldiers, but bandits who were wearing armor made of scorpion shells and covered in feathers.

Bai Xiaolu almost screamed when she saw who the bandits were attacking.

Boss Gold Tooth!

He was not dead yet, but he had become more dangerous than before like a wounded wild boar.

Although he was surrounded by dozens of enemies, he did not seem to be at a disadvantage at all. It seemed that he had surrounded so many bandits all by himself.

He was still wearing the plasma gloves on his right arm, but he was wearing a small fire launcher on his left arm. Lightning and flames interweaved and formed an absolute zone of destruction in the ten square meters nearby. None of the bandits could resist him for even one second.

However, weren’t the bandits Boss Gold Tooth’s subordinates? Why were they attacking each other in this place?

“Of course.”

The elder brother said coldly, “The guys who are wearing armor made of scorpion shells with colorful feathers on them are not the ‘Citi Gang’, but other gangs in the Furious Blood Wasteland. They are only following Boss Gold Tooth’s command because they are coveting the wealth of the ‘New Gold Mountain’.

“But Boss Gold Tooth’s intelligence was wrong. He didn’t expect that there would be a magnetic explosion infantry factory in ‘New Gold Mountain’. All the gangs suffered heavy casualties because of it. The city must’ve collapsed before it was conquered. More than half of their assets were lost for nothing. How could they not seek revenge from Boss Gold Tooth?

“Even if it was not for revenge, hehe, the ‘Citi Gang’ should have suffered the most losses in this battle. Even Boss Gold Tooth has become a lone wolf. This is a great opportunity for the major gangs to exterminate him and replace him. It is rare for such a strong man like Boss Gold Tooth to be in such dire straits. Let him catch his breath for a year and a half, maybe he will rise again… So, of course, we must take this opportunity to eliminate him!”

Looking at Boss Gold Tooth’s current state, Bai Xiaolu thought back to the day before, when he was riding on the armored tank, waving the Citigroup, and shouting the battle hymn in high spirits. She felt quite upset.

Of course, he did not have a good impression of Boss Gold Tooth.

But it was also a fact that the enemy had saved his life.

Anyone who saw the scene of a tiger being bullied by a dog would have mixed feelings. It had nothing to do with whether the tiger was good or bad.

No matter how much he sighed and how anxious Wan Canghai was, there was no way that the two of them could change the outcome of the fierce battle.

One in each hand, the leader of the Gold Teeth Brotherhood tore apart almost ten armed vehicles together with his motorbike.

He had been hit by dozens of bullets, too. Several of his bones would’ve been broken even if he was wearing a body armor.

“Asshole!”

Wan Zanghai gnashed his teeth. “Don’t destroy the motorbikes and the off-road vehicles. Go to hell, you bastard! Go to hell now!”

It was a pity that Boss Gold Tooth was so tenacious that he was like a weed that had grown out of poisonous soil and was contaminated by radiation.

It was not until he broke the neck of the last bandit that he stood straight for half a minute.

The desert was suddenly restored to silence. There was only the eternal, howling wind.

The crimson sand quickly covered the bodies and gradually melded with the dried blood.

The motorbikes and the off-road vehicles had all turned into scrap metal or even burning fireballs. There was no telling whether or not they were still functional.

Wan Canghai and Bai Xiaolu looked at each other and waited for another ten minutes.

Wan Zanghai rolled his eyes and asked, “Do you know how to drive?”

Her brother did, but Bai Xiaolu shook her head. “No.”

“Is that so…”

Wan Canghai was still smiling. He pointed his chin forward and said, “Go down and check whether or not the devils are dead. Also, check whether or not any of the vehicles are still intact. Remember, don’t touch the gun. If you do, the gun might misfire.”

He did not say who the ‘fire’ would be, but Bai Xiaolu knew what he meant.

“Me?”

Bai Xiaolu asked, “Am I alone?”

Boss Gold Tooth continued yelling, “This is your best chance to kill me. Your weapons and ammunition are all here. Do you want to try?”

“I wouldn’t dare. I wouldn’t dare!”

Wan Zanghai shivered and waved his hands, trying to hide further away.

The gold-tooth leader wiped his blood-stained beard. He curled his lips and commanded Bai Xiaolu, “Get the first aid kit and help me dig out the bullets and stitch up the wounds!”

…

“Clang! Clang! Clang!”

Bai Xiaolu dug out twelve bullets from Boss Gold Tooth’s body, which was as thick and thick as a boar’s.

If it were not for the blood on the ground and the muscle spasms that he could not control with his will, he would have suspected that he was faced with a well-camouflaged killing machine or a new type of biochemical modification man.

Taking a deep breath to calm herself down, Bai Xiaolu began to stitch up Boss Gold Tooth’s wounds and spray antifungal foam.

But Boss Gold Tooth took out a thick notebook from his pocket and handed it to Bai Xiaolu. He said softly, “Be careful. Don’t let the little devil see you.”

Bai Xiaolu took the notebook and looked at it. The notebook was somewhat rolled up, but it felt very smooth to the touch. Obviously, its owner had touched it often. She could vaguely see that many pages were soaked in blood with a faint smell of blood.

The cover of the notebook was marked with the ‘Alliance’ logo, with a line of Russian written below.

Russian was one of the two common languages of the ‘Alliance’, but it was rarely used in the ‘Association’.

Wasn’t Boss Gold Tooth’s patron the ‘contract’? What was going on?

Bai Xiaolu shook her head, indicating that she did not understand Russian.

Boss Gold Tooth took out a round lens from his pocket, which was just enough for him to put on one of his ears. After a “beep” sound, the lens followed Bai Xiaolu’s line of sight and automatically translated the Russian, which turned out to be notes and the author’s name.

“On the Strength of the Soul, Yuri Aix.”

—

Well, I’ve discovered that my writing speed is close to 10,000 words a day. I can’t stop writing!

Chapter 3284: Untitled

The zigzagging Russian was like a venomous bug, but Bai Xiaolu was deeply attracted to it. She could not help but touch the notebook with her fingertip. When she touched the name ‘Urey’, she felt a sharp pain as if she had been bitten by a bug.

Retracting his finger, Li Yao discovered that it was not an illusion. Two tiny black holes had appeared on his fingertip, from which a drop of blood was flowing out.

The blood splattered on the cover of the notebook and quickly soaked in, or rather… was sucked in by the two words ‘Urey’.

Bai Xiaolu’s heart was palpitating. She shrank her neck and asked, “Urey? Who is he?”

Maybe it was his imagination, but when Bai Xiaolu said the name, he felt that the muscles and nerves all over Boss Gold Tooth’s body constricted violently, and he was breathing fast.

The tough man did not even grunt when Bai Xiaolu pulled the bullet out of his ribs with surgical forceps. But when he heard the name ‘Urey’, he uttered a high-pitched sound deep inside his throat, which reminded Li Yao of a chicken whose throat had been cut.

After a long time, the gold-tooth demon said in a coarse voice, “…He is not a human being.”

Bai Xiaolu was baffled.

However, Yuri’s ‘telepathic notes’ contained endless magical power that enticed him to keep reading.

The notebook was filled with handwriting that was as long and winding as a reptile’s. It was also attached with many exquisite and realistic anatomy diagrams of the brain, including a sketch of a human face being skinned alive. Even though it was clearly false, it was as if a person’s soul had been forcefully sealed in a 2D plane and was howling towards Bai Xiaolu.

“You don’t need to worry about the rest of the content for now. Focus on page 22 to page 26. It’s the chapter on ‘Hypnosis’.”

Even without Boss Gold Tooth’s instructions, Bai Xiaolu had remembered every word.

Although his personality was weak, perhaps because of the stimulation of his brother, his memory was super good. It was literally a retentive memory.

But he didn’t know what the content actually meant or how to get started.

Bai Xiaolu was greatly alarmed.

In this world, there were indeed no ‘unintentionally dropped minced meat’.

Half an hour later, Boss Gold Tooth, who had just stitched up his wounds and refilled his food, regained his burning vitality. He shouted and commanded Bai Xiaolu and Wan Zanghai to clean up the battlefield, pack up the guns and vehicles, and prepare to leave.

BAM!

Boss Gold Tooth drew two forks on the south side of the map. He looked at Bai Xiaolu, only to find that she was in a daze. He could not help but frown and snap his fingers in front of Bai Xiaolu’s eyes. “Are you listening, stinky brat?”

Bai Xiaolu was listening.

Listening to the wind.

Just a moment ago, when Boss Gold Tooth mentioned ‘Vulture’ of the Vulture Gang, Bai Xiaolu fell into a trance that even she did not know.

He seemed to see black feathers burning furiously in the sky.

He seemed to have seen a vulture soar into the sky and swallow the entire world.

He saw the angry face of Boss Gold Tooth and the shocked face of Wan Zanghai.

Bai Xiaolu bit her lips.

But there was ruthlessness in his eyes.

He hated vultures.

He hated those ugly, despicable, wretched, and evil creatures.

The appearance of the vultures meant death and destruction. Whenever someone died in the village, or even when they breathed their last, the vultures in the desert would surround the village in groups as if they were receiving a mysterious signal, waiting to enjoy a feast of blood. There was no way to drive them away.

The ghouls were not as muscular and straightforward as the falcons. They never attacked head-on, but they would sneak in through all the gaps and loopholes and gnaw the dead bodies until there were only bones left. How annoying!

His brother asked curiously, “What’s that sound?”

“Vulture.”

They will find this place very soon. The ‘Vulture Gang’ has a lot of mutated falcons that can detect us from hundreds of meters in the sky.

Boss Gold Tooth tapped the map hard. “We cannot stay here. We must go north—in this direction!”

Wan Zanghai opened his mouth but did not say anything in the end. He replied dejectedly, “I do.”

Bai Xiaolu blurted out and immediately realized that there was a loophole in his words. She quickly added, “I heard from the teachers in the school that the northern part of the Furious Blood Wasteland is a very terrifying no man’s land. During the great war, a fierce battle broke out there. “Yes. There are no ‘people’.

The gold-tooth leader grinned. “It’s a pity that I’m not a human being but a devil.

They were ready to get on the train.

…

The yellow sand was rolling, and the wheels were spinning. The desert off-road vehicle, which had a skull painted on it, was carrying three guys that looked neither like humans nor ghosts, rushing on the desolate desert.

In the beginning, it was Boss Gold Tooth who was driving. While he was driving, he taught Bai Xiaolu and Wan Zanghai driving skills. If the ‘Vulture Gang’ and the ‘Venomous Scorpion Gang’ caught up to them, Boss Gold Tooth would definitely be responsible for opening fire. The two kids had to learn how to drive as soon as possible.

Thankfully, the two kids were very smart. Since there were no roads in the desert, buildings, and traffic rules, they could step on the gas any way they wanted. Bai Xiaolu and Wan Zanghai were both fast learners. They drove quite well within one day.

“Aha—”

Boss Gold Tooth was finally relieved. He groped inside the SUV and took out something from under the seat. “Look what I found!”

It was a portable speaker that used dry batteries.

Music was one of the few entertainment for the devils in the Furious Blood Wasteland.

It was especially so for the devils who had taken the stimulant drugs. When they were fighting, gambling, or venting their primitive desires, they loved to play crazy rock music to liven things up.

Boss Gold Tooth pressed the play button. The sound of wailing metal immediately came from the speaker.

Bai Xiaolu and Wan Zanghai both had a headache.

Boss Gold Tooth pursed his lips. He pulled out the player on the speaker, crushed it, and threw it out of the window into the sandstorm.

But in the narrow and hot carriage, when the nerves of the boy and the young man were highly tense, his singing was no different from the wailing of the heavy metal just now.

“When I was young, I liked to listen to the radio.

Waiting for my favorite song

How happy the old days were.

My beloved old song, like the reunion of a lost old friend.

Every melody and every note was still shining.

Bai Xiaolu sang in a low voice. At first, she was stammering and could only hum along, but when she reached “Yesterday Once More”, she was already able to keep up with the rhythm and felt the warmth, regret, and hope contained in the song.

“Before the nuclear war, this place used to be a plantation with the best vineyards and fruit gardens. Dozens of wineries produced the best wine on the planet.

Bai Xiaolu shook her head and replied honestly, “I don’t know.”

Grapes had not grown in the desert for a long time. Or rather, Bai Xiaolu had never seen real grapes since she came to this world. Occasionally, she would find a few wild grapes next to the thorny bushes, only to find that they were bitter and unpalatable.

“We… people harvest the red, purple, fragrant, and sweet grapes and pour them into a large grape pond. Then, the most beautiful local girls will take off their shoes and socks, jump in, and stomp the grapes into juice with their feet. This is the best grape juice.”

Bai Xiaolu and Wan Canghai looked out of the window together.

All he saw was red sand, melted tanks, twisted infantry tanks, broken power armors, and struggling bones.

However, under the illumination of the blood-red setting sun, a blurred outline seemed to have appeared in the depths of the sandstorm. There was also a large wooden barrel that was seven to eight meters tall. Of course, it was impossible for it to really be a wooden barrel. It was probably something like a barn. It was packaged into the shape of a wooden barrel and became the signature of the winery.

The songs that only the fathers of our generation could sing, such as ‘Take Me Home, Country Road’ or ‘Yestered Day Once More’, she could sing them all. She liked to sing them, too. All the young men went crazy for her.”

The redness inside the artificial eye was expanding and shrinking, somewhat blinking. Creaking noises were echoing deep inside the skull of the leader of the Gold Teeth Clan, as if an ancient tape was being rewound.

Bai Xiaolu stared at the sandstorm outside the window, the broken winery, and the broken wooden barrels.

He had never seen the grapes and other fruits that Boss Gold Tooth talked about, nor had he ever seen such good girls with snow-white teeth, golden hair, and skin as smooth as honey.

But he could imagine.

He thought of his sister.

If only there were no wars.

If there were no war, no radiation, no acid rain, no mutant beasts, and no bandits, and if this place were still a boundless, fertile farm, perhaps his sister would have become the most beautiful girl in the world, jumping on the sweet grapes, laughing, and singing freely. She loved singing so much that she was like a lark in a fairy tale.

It was not like now, where he had a fever every other day and could not even walk when he was weak. Even his singing had turned into suppressed moans.

Bai Xiaolu closed her eyes. Beautiful illusions appeared on her swollen eyelids. The boy held back his tears and mumbled, “That would be so much better.”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed for a moment.

“Isn’t it?”

There are ten thousand ways that human beings can destroy themselves. Even if you can go back to the past and correct one of their mistakes, how are you going to correct the other 9,999?

Chapter 3286: Untitled

They drove northwards for three days and gradually entered the depths of the ‘Northern Wasteland’. Thankfully, they were not surrounded or blocked yet.

Compared with the slightly populated area in the center of the Furious Blood Wasteland, this place was much more desolate and desolate after the end of the world. Illuminated by the lackluster, thick, and colorful mist, the sand and stones attached to the melted tanks and tanks formed threatening statues; the collapsed underground fortresses formed pitch-black hollows; not to mention the sentry cannons, automatic gun turrets, magnetic storm coils, and prismatic towers that were still functioning after being swept by radiation and blast. Deadly traps were everywhere. The corpses of countless adventurers and the wreckage of vehicles silently told the horror of no man’s land.

Only an overlord of the desert such as Boss Gold Tooth, who had been struggling in the blood-like sand for more than ten years or even his entire life, had the courage and capability to drive an off-road vehicle into an uninhabited area with two kids.

Over the next two days, Boss Gold Tooth pretended that he was not satisfied with Bai Xiaolu’s driving skills. He mainly asked Wan Zanghai to drive, but he asked Bai Xiaolu to curl up in the back seat and focus on the training of Yuri’s notes.

Of all the techniques to utilize the power of the heart, hypnosis was not very difficult. After all, even an ordinary person without superpowers could become a Master Hypnotist.

His brother had grasped it very quickly. Even Bai Xiaolu had learned a thing or two about it. However, he did not stop at all during his journey. He did not have time to be alone with Wan Zanghai and could not find an opportunity to attack.

Three days later, the opportunity finally came.

In the sky behind them, a few black spots were hovering below the dark grey clouds.

It was the mutated falcon of the Vulture Gang. It had excellent eyesight and could communicate with its master telepathically. In many cases, it was even more useful than an unmanned reconnaissance plane.

The Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang were actually wary of Boss Gold Tooth to such an extent that they wouldn’t let go of him even though he had entered the no man’s land. It was as if they wanted to see him dead or alive.

With the enemy scouting in the sky, they could only choose to advance at night. During the day, they would drive the off-road vehicles to the hollows of the sand dunes and camouflage them with desert camouflage canvas.

As it happened, they had been travelling day and night for three days. Their physical and mental strength had reached their limits. It was time for a good rest.

Boss Gold Tooth had taken over the off-road vehicle by himself. All the guns, ammunition, and assets were with him.

Bai Xiaolu and Wan Zanghai, on the other hand, were crouching in a desert camouflage tent not far away from the vehicle. After all, there were no guns, transportation, or supplies. They could not escape anyway.

The two of them craned their necks and pricked up their ears. Very soon, they heard the heavy snoring of Boss Gold Tooth from the SUV.

The snoring didn’t stop for a full three minutes. The interval was very even. The boy and the young man looked at each other. Suddenly, Wan Zanghai grabbed Bai Xiaolu’s collar with an extremely hideous expression.

“Speak!”

Wan Canghai almost bit Bai Xiaolu’s ear off. “What did Boss Gold Tooth ask you when you stitched up his wounds? Did you tell him anything?”

“N-no.”

Pretending to be half-panicked, Bai Xiaolu stammered, “As I said, we are classmates in the underground city. Your name is Wan Zanghai, and you are the son of the laboratory director of Gold Mountain Clean Water Company. That’s all.”

“Is that so?”

Wan Canghai narrowed his eyes and stared at Bai Xiaolu, trying to find a loophole on her face.

Bai Xiaolu had been training with her brother for two days. Wan Zanghai wouldn’t see through her easily.

“Hehe…”

Wan Canghai stared at him for a long time before he resentfully said, “I can see that there’s something wrong with your attitude over the past two days. You’ve been singing with him and helping him change his medication every day. Don’t tell me you’ve accepted his small favors. You haven’t been fooled by him, have you?

“Let me tell you something. Be clear with your head. He is a demon, the most sordid, sordid, and inhumane beast! If necessary, he will kill you and drink your blood, eat your meat, and gnaw your bones without even blinking!

“What?”

Bai Xiaolu pretended to panic. “How come?”

“How come?”

Wan Canghai sneered, “The devils are not human beings at all. They are just animals in human skin. Even their ‘human skin’ is extremely ugly. What can they not do? Even if you don’t remember the education in class, you should remember clearly how the ‘New Gold Mountain’ broke into the city, shouldn’t you? Boss Gold Tooth is the culprit!”

Bai Xiaolu was silent.

“If you think that you can get away as long as you do whatever he says, it is the typical ‘Stockholm syndrome’, or the victim’s reliance and trust in the criminals.

“Please, please be clear about this. Boss Gold Tooth is the enemy, and we are on the same side. We are compatriots and of the same nation!
