The leader of the Gold Teeth Gang gnashed his teeth, one word after another jumping out. “The Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang.”

“What?”

Bai Xiaolu was greatly alarmed.

“Don’t be scared. The reinforcements will definitely come today. It will be fine as long as we can survive this wave. But there is only one entrance and exit in this place. It is a dead end. We can’t stay here and defend ourselves. Otherwise, the entrance and exit will be blocked and burnt. We will be doomed. We have to go to the surface and rely on the ruins of the winery to survive.”

Boss Gold Tooth dragged Bai Xiaolu out of the room and woke up Wan Zanghai, asking him to pack up his weapons and ammunition. He took the opportunity to whisper to Bai Xiaolu, “Don’t worry. The reinforcements are a secret special force of the ‘Association’. They are very fast and travel on all terrains. They will definitely come.”

“You trust them so much?”

Bai Xiaolu was suspicious. “Why?”

“Because the commander of the special forces is Colonel Sterling, who was my captain when I was in the ‘Seal’. He was rescued by me and my other three brothers during the operation to capture Yuri. If it weren’t for our sacrifice, he would’ve been captured by Yuri and thrown into Camp X, too!

The leader of the Gold Teeth Clan said, “When I returned to this place after all the suffering, Colonel Stirling took a lot of care of me. He was also the one who proved my loyalty and got in touch with the ‘contract’ again. If there’s anybody I trust in the Furious Blood Wasteland and the ‘contract’, it’s him! I’m only willing to hand over the cutting-edge water-cleaning technology to Colonel Stirling. He will certainly come to our rescue, too!”

“But—”

Bai Xiaolu said, “A few days ago, when we attacked ‘New Gold Mountain’, he tricked you and did not tell you anything about the magnetic explosion infantry factory.

“No. It’s none of his business!”

Veins were bulging on the forehead of the leader of the Gold Teeth Brotherhood. He said brutally, “Colonel Sterling came to the secret base in the north of the Furious Blood Wasteland a year ago to train new special forces. He didn’t know anything about it. If he had known the truth, he would never have betrayed me. Never!

Bai Xiaolu was intimidated by Boss Gold Teeth’s brutality and nodded subconsciously.

“Believe me. Believe Colonel Stirling. We will be fine. You will be fine. Just like yesterday’s mines. It was just a false alarm. A false alarm.”

Boss Gold Tooth summoned the two kids and climbed back to the ground.

On the horizon, the wind was blowing, raising dozens of pillars of smoke that looked like raging devils.

In the sky, countless falcons were hovering, eager to dive down like the claws of the grim reaper.

Through the telescope, one could vaguely see the tanks of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang. The two teams were distinctly different and decorated in a very distinctive way. The tanks of the Scorpion Gang were embedded with the shells of countless giant scorpions as ‘reactive plate armor’. Often, one or two hooks were attached to the back of the tanks, which were designed to knock over the enemy’s tanks or the tails of the scorpions. The Vulture Gang, on the other hand, covered the tanks with colorful feathers, as if they were decorations on a bandit.

Both the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang had installed countless metal hooks and hooks on their vehicles so that a lot of bandits could cling to them. An off-road vehicle could carry more than ten bandits.

As far as one’s eyes could reach, there were at least thirty tanks, which meant that at least three hundred bandits, the elites of the Venomous Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang, had been deployed.

On their side, there were only three people, a boy, a teenager, and a dying man.

Three versus three hundred. The outcome seemed to have been decided long ago.

Wan Zanghai’s teeth started chattering.

But for some reason, Bai Xiaolu was not scared at all.

“Our weapons and ammunition are running out. Shooting at the enemy is not a good idea.

Boss Gold Tooth glanced at the guns and ammunition on the canvas and said solemnly, “They’re all yours. Don’t be stingy with your bullets. Try to attract the enemy’s attention by opening fire. I’ll go around them and fight my way into their base to retrieve some weapons and ammunition.”

“What—”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed. “What—what do you want?”

“Rest assured. Everything will be fine.”

The gold-tooth leader grinned. “Can’t you feel it? The wind is getting stronger and stronger. Soon, there will be a sandstorm. Visibility will be very low. Nobody will expect that I will suddenly march in. I’m sure that I will be able to kill a lot of people.”

“But there are hundreds of people there!”

Bai Xiaolu wanted to pull Boss Gold Tooth’s sleeve, but she did not dare to.

“Hundreds of people, hehe…”

Boss Gold Tooth patted Bai Xiaolu’s shoulder neither softly nor heavily and said, “Do you know why my ability is named ‘Legion’?”

“Why?”

Bai Xiaolu did not understand why Boss Gold Tooth was talking about that. “Is it because your ability can trigger the morale and combat ability of the entire legion?”

“No.”

“Because when my ability is activated to the maximum, I alone will be a legion!”

He dashed out into the sandstorm and the bloody desert.

The devil-like smoke gradually approached them.

Tears streaming down her face, Bai Xiaolu bit her lips so hard that they were bleeding.

Wan Zanghai rolled his eyes, ready to make a move.

“Don’t move!”

Bai Xiaolu glared at him. With a hideous expression, she said brutally, “If you don’t push back the bandits, it will be useless even if you run to the ends of the earth. They will capture you and skin you alive!

Wan Canghai looked at the veins on Bai Xiaolu’s face and then at his hands that were trembling while he held the gun. He touched his nose and stopped rolling his eyes.

“Alright, I won’t move.”

Crouching in the middle of the ruins of the winery, Wan Zanghai mumbled in a low voice, “It’s a pity that Boss Gold Tooth has already taken action. Do you really think that he is going to attack the tanks of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang and fight hundreds of them by himself? Chances are that he is planning to take advantage of us to distract the enemy’s attention while he sneaks away!

“Shut up.”

Bai Xiaolu narrowed her eyes and said, one word after another, “Shut up.”

Wan Canghai’s heart skipped a beat. Seeing the expressionless face of the boy, he did not dare to say anything.

“Bang! Bang! Bang! Bang!”

Bai Xiaolu aimed at the convoy of bandits not far away and opened fire first.

At such a long distance and with such a huge sandstorm, of course, it was impossible to hit the target, but it was enough to attract the enemy’s firepower. Like a torrential rain, it swept across the ruins of the winery, causing broken stones and sparks to fly everywhere. Cracking noises were everywhere. The boy and the young man could not even raise their heads.

“Are you crazy?”

Wan Zanghai screamed.

“Maybe!”

Bai Xiaolu crouched in a corner. She only extended her hands and her gun to counterattack, which attracted more bullet comments.

“He left us and ran away!”

Wan Zanghai shouted.

“Bullsh*t!”

Bai Xiaolu roared crazily.

The young man lay on the ground and covered his head with another bulletproof vest. The boy, on the other hand, picked up a second gun and fired left and right, causing the web between his thumb and index finger to bleed and his arms to go numb. There was no telling where the bullet had flown to.

The enemy was getting closer and closer.

The sound of the off-road vehicle’s engine, the clear sounds of the enemy firing, and the sounds of cursing could be heard in the sandstorm.

In the sky, a mutated falcon with red eyes suddenly let out a long cry and swooped down at Bai Xiaolu like an arrow released from a bow. Its speed was so fast that Bai Xiaolu could even smell the stench on its body.

Bai Xiaolu bit her lips so hard that her brother’s brain was burning. She helped him lock onto the monster that was diving down. After a beautiful shot, the mutated falcon turned into a pile of minced meat.

In the sandstorm not far away from them, the gunshots and laughter of the bandits came to an abrupt end, too, before they turned into screams, moans, and roars.

“This is—”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed for a moment. She was overjoyed and stuck her head out of the low wall.

“He didn’t leave?”

Wan Canghai found it hard to believe, too. He swallowed and stuck half of his head out of the wall, too.

In the billowing sandstorm, the visibility was extremely low.

However, even the most intense sandstorm and the thickest smoke could not cover Boss Gold Tooth’s vigorous and brilliant vitality when he was burning to the maximum.

They were the dominators of the Furious Blood Wasteland.

It was the God of War on the Wasteland.

Bai Xiaolu saw that Boss Gold Tooth had been lurking in the sand and melding himself with the earth in camouflage until the tanks of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang passed by him slowly. Then he suddenly burst out and overturned an off-road vehicle like a furious rhino.

Bai Xiaolu saw that Boss Gold Tooth was unstoppable. A dagger and a saber slashed out two dominating auras. No matter how many layers of armor the enemy was wearing or how many body armor they were wearing, they were all cut in half by Boss Gold Tooth. Internal organs and broken limbs were everywhere on the way.

Bai Xiaolu saw that bright gold ripples were spreading out of Boss Gold Tooth’s body. All the enemies that were covered by the ripples were slowed down and turned into clay chickens and pottery dogs that were at the mercy of the enemy.

Bai Xiaolu saw that Boss Gold Tooth was snatching grenades from the enemies and throwing them into the off-road vehicles nearby. He was creating countless exploding fireballs in front of him and behind him. Countless enemies were blown away by him, but he was like an iron tower in the middle of a raging ocean.

He was not ‘snatching a few weapons’. He was planning to kill all the bandits of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang by himself!

Of course, Boss Gold Tooth was a human being, too, and there were limits to what he could do. There were too many of them. No matter how low the visibility was and how slow their reactions were, a lot of bullets had pierced through Boss Gold Tooth’s body.

But Boss Gold Tooth seemed to be made of iron and did not react at all. He continued moving forward, slashing, exploding, and bleeding!

Chapter 3296: Untitled

The bandits of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang had lost their courage.

They had thought that, after the failure of Boss Gold Tooth’s assault on ‘New Gold Mountain’, the Citi Gang would be gone, and the ambitions of the dominator of the desert would collapse.

Besides, Boss Gold Tooth and the two kids had been on a long journey in the ‘Northern Desolate Area’ for several days. They should’ve been exhausted and waiting for their doom.

But he did not expect that the dry bones, boosted by the invisible flames, would turn into the undying tyrants of hell again!

At this moment, countless bandits were reminded of the terror when Boss Gold Tooth savaged the Furious Blood Wasteland. Their minds were once again occupied by Boss Gold Tooth’s aggressiveness and the brutal captives of the legions. They were like chicks in front of a ferocious tiger, motionless and waiting to be butchered.

Very soon, almost ten armored off-road vehicles had been overturned or blown away.

There were almost a hundred dead bodies.

It was not until this moment that Boss Gold Tooth’s attack was slowed down. Crimson blood was flowing out of his body, and hot steam was spurting out of his mouth and nose.

Although he was heavily wounded and breathing heavily, he persisted in silence. He strode forward, dodged, waved his saber, and slashed, reaping the lives of the hideous creatures like the sickle of death.

“Boss is tired. We have to help him!”

Bai Xiaolu roared at Wan Zanghai.

Wan Canghai had already lost his cool. He did not dare to look at the eyes of the boy, which were as fierce as those of a tiger. His lips trembled for a long time, but he failed to come up with anything. Therefore, he simply handed the gun and the bullets over to Bai Xiaolu and raised his hands as if it was none of his business.

To be honest, after the previous round of concentrated fire, they did not have many bullets left. They would be committing suicide if they went forward.

Besides, it was a dogfight among the devils. From the perspective of the ‘underground dwellers’ of the Thousand Treasures Sea, it was indeed unnecessary for them to be killed.

But Bai Xiaolu couldn’t care less about that. She kicked Wan Zanghai to the ground, picked up all the guns and ammunition, and rushed toward the raging sandstorm.

“Brother, please!”

The boy’s eyes had never been so determined, nor had the lines on his face ever been so hideous. It was as if he had grown a lot in an instant.

“If you’ve made up your mind—”

His brother was silent for a moment before he replied, “Alright. I’ll try my best!”

When the boy rushed into the sandstorm, Boss Gold Tooth’s blood had already turned the sand below his feet into mud.

The moment he slowed down, the advantage in numbers of the bandits was immediately revealed. Dozens of automatic rifles opened fire at the same time, causing sparks to fly from his body armor and the level-three armor on his body. The limbs that were exposed to the air were spluttering blood one after another.

Seeing that the bandits were suppressing him with their firepower and that Boss Gold Tooth was attracted by the line of fire and could not get away, the boy rushed close.

“Ah!”

The boy roared like an animal, and the elder brother ‘pushed’ him hard. An invisible wave of brainwaves spread out like a blast and hit the brains of all the bandits nearby hard. Many of them were dazed, with blood flowing out of their mouths and noses. They staggered and fell into the ‘concussion’ state.

The bandits who had temporarily lost their combat ability turned into the best live targets. Their throats were cut and their weapons taken away.

Even if Bai Xiaolu’s hands were shaking, she could still blow their heads off easily.

The enemies far away did not dare to come close. They simply relied on the off-road vehicles to shoot at them, which seemed to be beyond the range of Bai Xiaolu’s and her brother’s previous attacks. But her brother still endured the pain and ‘pushed’ them a second, a third, a fourth, and a fifth time.

One by one, the bandits, with blood spurting out of their mouths and noses, staggered out from the back of the SUV and were called out by Boss Gold Tooth.

“Enough, brat. Your brother is about to be overdrafted!”

Boss Gold Tooth threw an automatic rifle to Bai Xiaolu and bellowed.

“Not enough, Boss. Brother said that he could do more. Believe us!”

Bai Xiaolu grinned. Blood was flowing out of her nostrils, ears, eyes, and mouth. She felt that her brother’s stomach was on fire. He seemed to have turned her brother’s brain into a bomb and ‘pushed’ it crazily time and time again, venting all the feelings of loss, fear, pain, and fury that they had experienced since they were kids.

The natural sandstorm gradually died down, but the bloody sandstorm raised by Bai Xiaolu, his brother, and Boss Gold Tooth was getting stronger and stronger, enshrouding the vanguard of the two gangs. The bandits finally couldn’t bear it any longer and ran away, shouting, “Devil! Devil! Devil!”

Bai Xiaolu and Boss Gold Tooth looked at each other and saw the smile in each other’s eyes.

Yes. Of course they were devils. Was there a problem?

The boy and the dominator of the desert strode forward side by side. Their brainwaves surged and supported each other, shooting out ‘mind lightning’ and scorching bullets one after another, until the boy’s throat was bleeding, until the brother’s every brain cell was burnt to ashes, until the wound on the Gold Tooth Boss stopped bleeding, and the brother fell to one of his knees.

At this moment, there was not a single enemy in sight five hundred meters ahead of them.

“Boss, what’s wrong? Boss!”

Bai Xiaolu hurried to help Boss Gold Tooth, only to discover that, although he was wreathed in scorching white smoke, he was as cold as a corpse.

His body armor had been wrecked. His chest was a mess of flesh and blood like a honeycomb. Even the left red optic eye had been embedded with a bullet. The eye had been burnt into a black hole, making it even uglier.

But Bai Xiaolu did not feel that Boss Gold Tooth was ugly or stinky at all. She just wanted to share some of her heat with him and share some of her heartbeat and brainwaves with him, too.

“You will be fine, Boss. Believe me. Reinforcements will be here soon. You said it yourself. You can’t lie to me. You can’t f*cking lie to me. I believe you. You can’t lie to me!”

The boy screamed desperately.

“Stop chattering like I’m really dead.”

Boss Gold Tooth grinned and spat out a mouthful of sticky black blood.

“Re—really?”

Bai Xiaolu was overjoyed.

“Nonsense. What about you? How are you and your brother?”

Boss Gold Tooth observed Bai Xiaolu carefully and wiped away the blood, sweat, and dust on his face with his rough hands.

“We’re fine, too. We’re perfectly fine. Yuri’s ‘mind notebook’ is indeed impressive. My brother has ‘pushed’ it more than ten times in a row. However, he is a little tired right now. He will be fine after a short rest!”

Bai Xiaolu still refused to let go of Boss Gold Tooth. She tried to clean his wounds in vain, but she didn’t know where to start. The wounds were too many and too dense. Boss Gold Tooth’s internal organs had all been torn apart. Everybody was wondering how he was still alive.

“That’s good.”

Boss Gold Tooth said weakly, “That’s good.”

In fact, it was not good at all.

Both of them were very clear that they had only taken down the vanguard of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang.

Although the two bandit gangs were a bunch of mobs, they were not idiots. It was impossible for them to gather all their troops and swarm forward in a mess, especially when they were faced with a well-known, terrifying ‘ability user’ who was the dominator of the desert.

The newcomers were just the vanguards. Those who were drawing close and firing guns in the distance were the real main force of the two gangs.

Bai Xiaolu and Boss Gold Tooth did not even have the strength to stand up.

Whether it was Boss Gold Tooth’s ‘legion’ or his brother’s ‘mind lightning’, they were all gone and were even about to consume their master’s vitality.

They were truly running out of supplies and resources.

However, seeing that the main force of the enemy was drawing close, the two of them found it odd that there were no negative feelings of fear, regret, or regret. The boy and the dominator of the desert looked at each other and saw nothing but calmness in each other’s eyes.

“I’m sorry.”

The overlord of the Wasteland said to the boy.

“No. This is good. I’m going to die anyway. This is good.”

The boy smiled.

“No. I was talking about last night. I should’ve agreed to play ‘Strong Chess’ with you last night.”

The dominator of the desert said solemnly.

“Maybe in my next life. I really hope that I can… see Boss Gold Tooth again in my next life. By then, you can teach me the , alright?”

There were tears in the boy’s smile.

“…Alright. Believe me. Definitely.”

The dominator of the desert said.

The bandits were getting closer and closer to them. The boy and the dominator of the desert were leaning against each other without them knowing.

“There should be some music at this time. It’s a pity that I didn’t bring a loudspeaker. Otherwise, I would sing for you.”

The dominator of the desert said, “Take me home, please?”

“Not good. I’m sick of it.”

The boy laughed. “Do you know how to sing ‘Yesterday? I want to hear that.”

“Ah, that…”

“Sing it. I’ve been listening to you sing ‘The Road to the Village, Bring Me Home’. I’m really curious what will happen when your voice sings ‘Yesterday’s Reappearance’. Sing it, Boss. I like listening to you sing ‘Yesterday’s Reappearance’. Sing it!”

The boy shook the arm of the overlord of the Wasteland.

The pale face of the dominator of the desert was flushed.

He cleared his throat and spat out another mouthful of thick black blood, ready to open his mouth.

But at this moment, the humming sound of the wind and the dense sound of bullets drowned his singing.

Burning bullet comments rained upon the ground.

It was not aimed at the two of them but at the bandits not far away.

Bai Xiaolu narrowed her eyes and saw dozens of soldiers who were carrying jetpacks and had silver wings on their back. They were as fierce as eagles, as agile as dragonflies, and as agile as hummingbirds.

They were well-trained and well-trained. Their firepower was extremely fierce. They were reaping and slaughtering the bandits.

Even though the sunlight was dim, the advanced armor that was full of the colors of the future and the “Contract” sign on their chests were still shining brilliantly.

Reinforcements had finally arrived.

It was none other than the new troop type that had been secretly trained under the “Contract”—the Rocket Flying Soldier!

Chapter 3297: Exposed Yesterday

“This is—”

Hearing the voice that sounded like wild bees dancing, the lips of the leader of the Gold Teeth Brotherhood trembled. He tilted his head and listened for a while. Filthy liquid flowed out of the broken red eye, as if it were hot tears. “Reinforcements. Our men. Help me up, kid. Help me up!”

Bai Xiaolu was so excited that she was rendered speechless, too. She didn’t know whether it was because she had survived or because Boss Gold Tooth and her ‘belief’ had not been let down, but she felt that she had tremendous strength and managed to lift Boss Gold Tooth, who weighed hundreds of kilograms.

“In my belt.”

Boss Gold Tooth said feebly, “There’s something in the belt on the left. Help me—cough cough—draw it out.”

Very soon, Bai Xiaolu found the neatly-collected package. She took it out and opened it against the wind. It was a flag and a blood-stained flag.

Before, Boss Gold Tooth could barely walk, and a gust of wind was enough to knock him over. But now that the blood-stained land was in his hands, he seemed to be granted infinite strength. His broken spine suddenly straightened up. He pushed Bai Xiaolu away and crawled to the remains of a tank that was still burning furiously. He shook the land to the maximum and waved it crazily in the wind.

“Hey!”

Boss Gold Tooth waved Citi hard and gestured to the sky. “We are here! We are here! Here!”

“Boss!”

Bai Xiaolu was so scared that her head was dizzy. “Danger! Come down now!”

Although the main force of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang were still 300-500 meters away and had suffered a head-on strike from the “Contract” rocket aviators, turning them into lambs waiting to be butchered, who knew whether or not a stray bullet would suddenly blow the head of Boss Gold Tooth?

However, Boss Gold Tooth seemed to have turned a deaf ear to Bai Xiaolu’s shouts.

In his eyes, ears, and heart, there seemed to be nothing but the rocket fliers in the sky, the blood-stained flag in his hand, and the glittering battle emblem of the ‘contract’.

“Maga! Maga! Maga!”

Citi was expanding, and the former elites of the Navy Seals were roaring.

“Boss, get down! Get down now!”

Bai Xiaolu was so anxious that she was about to go crazy. She rushed close and tried to drag Boss Gold Tooth out of the wrecked tank, but his feet were deeply embedded into the scrap iron like the roots of a tree. How could she move him?

The boy’s action finally caught the attention of the dominator of the desert. The leader of the Gold Teeth Brotherhood turned around, with an unprecedented gentleness on his ugly face.

“Believe me, everything is fine now.”

The dominator of the desert said to the boy softly, “We are saved—”

Before he could finish his sentence, a bright flower of blood burst out of the chest of the leader of the Gold Teeth Brotherhood. He loosened his grip on Citi, who was blown away by the flames. Dazed, he tried to grab her back, but he failed and fell off the burning tank.

“…Ah!”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed for three seconds, as if her braincells could not keep up with the rhythm of the optic nerves and handle such an abrupt scene. It was not until three seconds later that she lunged at the leader of the Gold Teeth Clan and discovered that he had been blown through from his chest to his back. Not just his heart and lungs, even part of his spine had been blown up.

No one could survive such heavy wounds, not even the dominators of the desert who controlled the legions.

“Boss!”

Bai Xiaolu shouted hysterically, “How are you, Boss? You can’t die. You must not die. Reinforcements are here. Do you hear me? Colonel Sterling, whom you’ve been looking forward to, is coming to your rescue. You must not die!

Boss Gold Tooth’s mouth was full of pink foam, which was soon washed away by the thick black blood. His body, which used to be as tall and strong as a mountain, looked so thin and weak, like a block of melting ice. Lying in Bai Xiaolu’s arms, he let the boy hold his head and cry. He breathed hard for a long time before he stammered, “Believe me. It’s alright now. It’s alright now. Help me. Help me hand over the cutting-edge water-cleaning technology to Colonel Sterling. He will take care of you. You can live on. Live on…”

“No, you have to pay for it yourself!”

Bai Xiaolu could not stop her tears. “Didn’t you say that we would be fine? You can’t die. You must survive. You can’t be a liar. You can’t lie to me. You said that we would be fine!”

“That’s why I said, cough cough, cough cough cough cough. That’s why I said you were stupid.”

The gold-tooth leader laughed again, in an extremely ugly and gentle way. “Didn’t I tell you not to trust anybody? If you don’t listen to me, I will not lie to you. Cough cough. Who am I lying to?”

“No. I won’t let you die!”

Bai Xiaolu stuffed sticky gel and hemostatic bandages into the hole on the chest of the gold-toothed leader, but there was no way that she could stop the blood and internal organs from spurting out. The boy’s face was as crazy as a devil’s, and his dark eyes were burning with the fury of the deepest level of hell. “No matter what price you have to pay, no matter how many things you have to abandon, no matter how far you have to walk, no matter what you have to become, no matter how many lives you have to sacrifice, I will not let you die. Even if you die, I will revive you!”

“Enough, noisy brat. I’ve lived enough. I’ve lived… too long. I’m too tired. Let me rest. You should forget me and live your own life.”

The leader’s voice was getting weaker and weaker. The intact artificial eye gradually dimmed, too. But his hands were still groping. “Where is my flag? I can’t see it.”

“In this place.”

Bai Xiaolu’s tears and snot were all mixed up. She helped Boss Gold Tooth find the Citibank and stuffed it into his hands, while she sobbed, “Didn’t you say that you hate your motherland? Didn’t you say that you hate the Citibank? Didn’t you say that those who believe in ‘motherland’ and ‘flag’ are idiots?

The leader’s hands stiffened, and he was somewhat embarrassed.

“Shut up, brat.”

In the end, he did not defend himself. Instead, he covered his chest with the burnt, blood-stained, and riddled with holes and mumbled, “I’m used to it. I’m used to it.”

“Boss!”

Bai Xiaolu finally couldn’t help but burst into tears. Her tears fell on the face and flag of Boss Gold Tooth.

“Don’t cry.”

The dominator of the desert reached out and wiped the boy’s tears. He paused and said softly, “My child, don’t cry…”

Citi stopped moving. His big hands were frozen. The last expression that ‘Gold Tooth Boss’ left on the black world was serenity and satisfaction. Soft brilliance was flowing out of every wrinkle on his ugly face.

“Boss! Boss! Boss!”

The boy hugged the heavy body of the dominator of the desert with his thin body and vented his grief and anger without restraint.

“Wait, Xiaolu.”

His brother’s brainwaves were somewhat disordered, too. His voice was shaking and coarse. “Something is not right.”

“What’s wrong?”

Bai Xiaolu’s eyes were bloodshot. She stared at the bandits of the Scorpion Gang and the Vulture Gang who were about to be butchered like a wild animal. “I’m going to kill them. I’m going to kill them all!”

“Not them.”

The elder brother quickly said, “Didn’t you notice Boss Gold Tooth’s wound? It’s a through-and-through wound. The wound on the back is small, but the wound on the chest is large. It’s obvious that the bullet entered the chest from the back. After it rolled inside the body, it exploded from the chest—it was shot from the back!”

“The rear?”

Bai Xiaolu was dazed.

“That’s right. The rear!”

The elder brother continued, “Boss Gold Tooth fought valiantly and did not retreat even a single step. Although his body armor and the chest of his armor were damaged, his back was completely intact. It was simply not something that could be blown up by a single bullet, unless it was an armor-piercing bullet. However, we clearly didn’t hear too intense of a sound just now. It was as if a ghost bullet had assaulted us from behind and silently knocked Boss Gold Tooth down!”

“Well—”

Bai Xiaolu was greatly alarmed. She felt a chill run down her spine. When she turned around, there was not a single bandit behind them except for the ruins of the winery.

But at this moment, Bai Xiaolu sensed the danger keenly.

He tilted his head. An object that looked like a bullet whizzed past his ear and cut off half of his earlobe.

“Ah!”

Bai Xiaolu cried in pain and stared at the ruins of the winery, only to discover no traces of the enemy.

However, a broken brick from the left drew a weird curve and slapped his temple brutally, knocking him to the ground.

Bai Xiaolu was exhausted in the first place, and her brother was on the verge of death after more than ten rounds of ‘Mind Lightning’. He felt that the world was spinning and his head was dizzy. How could he stand up?

In a trance, they saw a ghost-like figure slowly emerge from the ruins of the winery and walk toward them unhurriedly.

No. He was not walking. He was floating close. His feet were three feet off the ground.

His eyes were emitting weird purple brightness, and his lips curled into a mocking smile. He opened his hands, where dozens of pebbles with sharp edges like bullets were floating. Tiny electric arcs were dancing around the pebbles, as if they could be accelerated by electromagnetic force at any time and shot out like armor-piercing bullets.

It was the underground teenager, Wan Zanghai!

“It’s you!”

Bai Xiaolu had been wary of Wan Zanghai since the beginning. She had taken away all the weapons and ammunition on purpose when she was charging, thinking that Wan Zanghai would not be able to play any tricks. She did not expect that she had still underestimated the guy. “You are also an ‘ability user’!”

“That’s right.”

No problem.

Nothing.

He had nothing.

Flag, Boss, Friends, Justice, Agreement, Alliance, Wasteland, Underground, Trust, Transaction, Wine, Strong Player, Country Road, Yesterday Reappears, Hehe, Hehe, Haha, Haha, I’m back!

He knew that his brother must be over-exhausting his superpowers. He could feel the burning and corroding pain in his brother’s brain every second. Otherwise, how could he have fought against the rocket pilots?

He wanted to persuade his brother to take it easy, but he knew that it was all nonsense. There was no such thing as ‘unintentionally dropped minced meat’. Everything had a price. In the desert, one more second of survival meant a terrible price.

In the end, he was woken up by the noises of cannon fire.

Through the telescope, he saw a large batch of rocket pilots hovering on the horizon, but three weird-looking tanks were rushing on the ground. They were even stronger and more barbaric than the rhino tanks commonly seen in the ‘Alliance’. He said without any hesitation.

His brother said.

Without any hesitation, he released all the control over his body and allowed his brother to control it.

His brother picked up a modified automatic rifle with a short barrel from the seat next to him and pointed it at the chest of the body where his brother’s brain was located.

Chapter 3300: Untitled

The boy walked among mountains of corpses and seas of blood.

Plots eroded his innocence. Lies whittled away his childishness. Betrayals put solid armor on him. Regrets and desires turned into his sharpest blades.

He grew up by stepping on piles of bones. At first, he turned into a young man. Then, he turned into a young man amid the bloody storms and perils. The young man raised his head and saw a throne of bones standing on the peak of the endless corpses, but all the bones were black.

He smiled and walked unhurriedly to the skeleton throne that was destined to be his.

But right then, a head popped up from the pool of blood nearby.

Vulture.

“You again.”

He frowned, not very happy.

He was dazed for a moment. He did not remember such a vulture. Why did he say ‘again’?

Such an eye-catching vulture with distinctive features would not be forgotten even if it was burnt to ashes. He would definitely remember it if he had seen it before.

“We are finally back online!”

Vulture was somewhat relieved, but he was still very nervous. “Listen, Bai Xiaolu. The interference is too intense. The ‘future you’ will destroy the connection very soon. We only have three to five minutes at best. We must talk!”

He did not understand what Vulture was talking about. He replied subconsciously, “I am not Bai Xiaolu. I am Wan Zanghai.”

“No. That’s what we’re going to talk about. You are Bai Xiaolu. You are not Wan Zanghai, nor are you a super body or a flood!”

Vulture hurried to say, “Alright. The inertia of the ‘time tide’ is too strong. It’s impossible for me to change the past. Every contact between us will be erased. You will eventually become a super body and a flood.

“However, I beg you, no matter how much time has passed, no matter how many things you’ve experienced, no matter how many things you’ve lost, never forget your true self, the innocent, kind, hopeful, and trusting Bai Xiaolu!”

I’m fighting a ‘future you’ in a space battleground that is unimaginably mysterious and boundless. But in fact, the ‘future you’ is not necessarily the future you. Let’s call you that!”

Vulture replied quickly, “To describe it in a way that you can understand, the ‘future you’ is too awesome. After billions of years of development, you’ve grown into a supreme cosmic big shot. When I slashed at him with my blood-boiling passion, he dodged it easily and hit me back with ‘Black Tiger Steals Heart’. Then, I took the opportunity to steal a monkey’s peach. But instead of stealing the peach, he locked his throat with a deadly scissor kick. Then, he bashed me so hard that I almost collapsed.

He found it hard to understand Vulture’s words. Was this how the future generations would speak billions of years later?

He rolled his eyes and smiled. “Alright. You help me, and I’ll help you. Since you said that you are from the future and have grasped the mysteries of time, help me turn back time, stop the war, and change everything! I’ll help you however I can if I can.”

“That’s the point.

Vulture said, “I can’t help you. If I help you change everything, all the history over billions of years will be changed. I wouldn’t have been born at all. Since I was never born, how can I help you change everything? This is a paradox. A time paradox that nobody can get around. I can’t get around it, and neither can you.”

“Hehe!”

His eyes were cold, and his lips curled.

“I know what you are thinking, but it is impossible.

Vulture said, “If you go back to the past and stop the war, or even revive the people you love, your life will be completely different. Maybe you will live a happy life together, and you will never be able to grasp such terrifying abilities. You won’t be able to touch the mysteries of time, and you won’t be able to go back to the past to change everything… Look, we are back to the time paradox, aren’t we?

“If one day you really change everything and return to a new timeline that seems to be peaceful and where all your mistakes have never happened, you will not really return to the past. Instead, you will create a new parallel universe with your supreme techniques. Everything in this parallel universe is just something you created to fool yourself. In the ‘old parallel universe’, nothing can be changed. On the contrary, it will become even more painful and dark because you have consumed too much energy.

“Do you see what I mean? It’s like you accidentally broke a very beautiful vase. You can go to the market and buy an identical one to replace it, but the old one is broken. The new vase can never become the old vase. No matter how you fool yourself, it’s impossible!”

“Asshole…”

He gritted his teeth and clenched his fists. Dangerous feelings were gathering in his black eyes like billions of lightning bolts.

“There are worse things.

But Vulture was not scared of his fury at all. He continued talking. “There’s an old saying that a man will die twice. The first time is when his body perishes, and the second time is when everybody forgets him. The saying has been around since the era of Earth, hasn’t it? It means that not only does a man live in three-dimensional space, he also lives in other people’s memories and feelings. Even if his body perishes, his ‘information’ is still alive.

“If you really choose to go back to the past, in fact, you will bypass the time paradox and jump to another parallel universe. I suspect that you will completely forget the people you truly love. You will forget the sacrifices they made for you, your love for them, and the beautiful days you spent together. In other words, you will erase them with your own hands. Nobody will remember who they are anymore!

“Even if there are other people in the new parallel universe, they are not the real them. They are just puppets that look like them. Clones and inflatable people. They are all fakes!

“Shut up!”

He finally couldn’t help it and roared, “I don’t know what you are talking about. Shut up and get out of my dream!”

“You are very smart. You can understand it. Even if you don’t understand it now, you will have billions of years to understand it later.

Vulture continued, “In terms of real age, you are more than qualified to be my eighteen generations of ancestors. But in terms of life experience, I am slightly richer than you were in the beginning. I am a father as well as an elder brother. Let me tell you, when I make certain sacrifices for my children and juniors as a father or elder brother, I really, really don’t want to see them torture themselves with endless hatred and regret. They will be trapped in the thorny maze of obsessions for billions of years without being able to free themselves. In the end, they will turn themselves into a beast, a monster, and a devil. They will do whatever they want, destroy themselves and other people, and destroy their own future!

“Really. I don’t want them to be like this. All the sacrifices I’ve made only have one purpose, which is to let them live a happy life with a brighter and brighter future than mine. That’s enough. Really enough!

“Your father and your brother, the people who gave you happiness and happiness in the past, must’ve been the same. They hope that you can live a happy life forever instead of being a monster that is consumed by hatred and resentment and punishing yourself for billions of years, right?

“Shut up!”

He held his head in agony. “Shut up. Shut up. Shut up!”

“Enough. Billions of years of pain and suffering, billions of years of wandering and colliding in the labyrinth of regret. It’s really enough. It’s time to wake up, Bai Xiaolu!”

Vulture opened his wings at him. “I can’t change the past, but you can change the future! The odds of me defeating the Flood Tide are only one billionth, but the odds of you defeating the Flood Tide are 100%! Because you are the starting point of the Flood Tide. You are the regret that the Flood Tide will never be able to repair. You are the ‘inner demon’ of the Flood Tide!

“Awaken from the billions of years of regret and the pain that you will never be able to get rid of. Listen to the real voices of the people who sacrificed themselves for you. Collect the past and stride toward a future with infinite possibilities!

“We need you. The civilization of mankind needs you. We need you in the future. Only you can completely transform the tides and bring the civilization of mankind billions of years in the future to a whole new level. Only you can lead us to defeat the ‘Great Purge Agreement’, the ‘Fast Child Collapse’, the ‘Lord of Singularity’ and the ‘Ancient Devourer’. With the sacrifices, hopes, and hopes of countless people over billions of years, we will create the most beautiful tomorrow! Only in such a way can the people who sacrificed for you be truly at peace!”

“I…”

For the first time, he hesitated. He could not see himself clearly in the pool of blood. “The future me is so awesome… What about you?”

“Me?”

Vulture said, “As long as you can be awakened, I will recognize you as a big shot immediately. I will be satisfied if I can be your leg accessory.”

“A leg accessory?”

He was confused. “What is that?”

“The leg hair.”

Vulture said, “You are the real unparalleled expert in the multiverse and the well-deserved top expert in the billions of years of human civilization. I will be a hair on your leg and follow you to the multiverse and the high-dimensional space to cheer for you. I will be satisfied when I see you sweep across billions of universes. Now, you should know how sharp and important you are, right?

“So, so it was like this…”

He was confused and felt that something was not right.

“Of course it is. Do not doubt it. Maybe you don’t know me, but in the future billions of years from now, I will be known for my honesty and integrity.

Vulture said, “Not good. The ‘future you’ has discovered me. It is harassing me again. I have to retreat. Don’t forget the secret code!”

“Secret code?”

He was dazed. “What secret code?”

“It’s that everyone has a duty to protect Vulture!”

Vulture shouted, “The inertia of the ‘time tide’ is so strong that it will erase all traces of me billions of years ago without leaving a single ripple. Therefore, you will forget the dream and me very soon.

“But I still hope that, no matter what, you don’t forget that you are Bai Xiaolu, the Bai Xiaolu who is righteous, kind, and full of hope for tomorrow. And I hope that you can firmly bind your ‘belief’ to the words ‘love the Vulture, everyone has a duty’ and imprint it in the deepest part of your soul.

“In such a way, in the space battleground billions of years from now, when the ‘future you’ and I are engaged in the most intense battle, I will shout ‘it is everyone’s responsibility to protect Vulture’, and you will be able to awaken and recall your past. You will be able to recreate your true self, the original Bai Xiaolu!

“Remember, you must remember. Give your real self a chance. Give the future civilization of mankind a chance. Give ‘hope’, ‘tomorrow’, and ‘belief’ a chance. This is the only possibility for us to defeat the flood. I believe you, Bai Xiaolu, Bai Xiaolu, Bai Xiaolu—”

Vulture seemed to have been grabbed by the throat and dragged back to billions of years later.

Very soon, he forgot the existence of Vulture, the conversation just now, and the embarrassing secret code.

However, the throne of bones on the bloody ocean was somewhat dazzling and showing signs of collapse.

When he lowered his head, the clear face in the pool of blood was blurred again. There was no telling whether it was ‘Thousand Treasures Sea’ or ‘Bai Xiaolu’.

…

Brother, we’ve made it. I’m not dead. I’ve entered the underground metropolis of the ‘Alliance’. They don’t have any doubts and really think that I’m the ‘Thousand Treasures Sea’!

Brother, I’ve been sent to a hospital. The medical treatment here is very good, and the wounds will be healed soon. They didn’t find any traces of you. Haha. Your plan is indeed marvelous. I am now an ‘Underground Clan’!

‘Brother, the director of this hospital is named ‘Urey’, too. Of course, he is not the terrible Yuri Aix. He is a good man, an angel without wings. He took special care of me and helped me solve a lot of unnecessary problems. He also said that I had a rare gift and that he was going to recruit me as his student! Hehe. It seems that our luck has changed for the better. Our future will be better and better, won’t it?’

Brother, I seem to remember that, when Boss Gold Tooth died, and when you… died, you said something to me. What did you want me to do? Damn it. I forgot it.

Please, come to my dream and tell me again. The underground metropolis is very prosperous, but my heart is a bit lost. I don’t know what road I should walk on in the future.

But it didn’t matter. There was still a long way to go. There was still a lot of time. Eventually, I would remember, right?

[Other than that, the replay of yesterday has concluded.]
